 81: - **The WebSocket path is barely covered.** Only the oversized-payload case is tested, with a stub socket. Authentication, the success path, the gRPC streaming path and the error path (including the internal-URL leak described in [Security](security.md)) have no tests.
 82: - **`PredictStream` has no test.** The only gRPC test is a unary 6 MiB call.
 83: - **ClickHouse is never contacted.** Metric tests use fakes.
 84: - **No coverage of the Kafka modules, the Dockerfiles, the entrypoints, the Kubernetes manifests, or the load test.**
 85: - **No test of `GET /v1/jobs` over HTTP**, of JWT issuance of API keys through the endpoint, or of the "full Redis" failure.
 86: - **Real model behaviour** (OCR accuracy, transcript quality) is not tested; the fixtures are a rendered line of text and two seconds of silence.
 87: - No coverage tool, linter, type checker, security scan or CI pipeline is configured.
 88: 
 89: An earlier mutation check (reported in Phase 7) confirmed two key tests fail when the behaviour is removed: dropping the admin check broke the admin test, and removing the 429 branch broke the rate-limit test.
 90: 
 91: ## Test hygiene
 92: 
 93: Each full run leaves rows in the test database: **one disabled `test-ocr-<hex>` model, five users and four API keys** (verified over three runs). Session fixtures create users and keys without cleanup. This is why the instructions say to use a scratch database. Reset it by recreating the database, or just delete the rows.
 94: 
 95: Redis is flushed before every test, so never point `TEST_REDIS_URL` at a Redis you care about; the guard only enforces database number 1, not the host.
 96: 
 97: ## The other scripts
 98: 
 99: ### `scripts/smoke_test.sh`
100: 
101: Runs against a live Compose stack. Checks and requirements are in [Getting started](getting-started.md#smoke-test). Things to know:
102: 
103: - It exits 1 if any check fails; the `bad-upload` check is informational and never fails the run.
104: - The admin check uses `docker compose exec`, so it does not work against Kubernetes.
105: - `--latency N` runs N **sequential** jobs per model and polls every 2 seconds. The first poll happens immediately, then it sleeps 2 s between polls, so a job that takes a fraction of a second is recorded at about 2 seconds. With N = 5, the 95th percentile is simply the slowest of five.
106: - It depends on `fixtures/`, which is not in the repository.
107: 
108: ### Reported smoke and latency results (author-reported, not reproduced)
109: 
110: | Point                   | OCR p95 (s) | ASR p95 (s) | Notes                                                                                                                          |
111: | ----------------------- | ----------: | ----------: | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
112: | Baseline (Phase 0 to 6) |      16.665 |       2.142 | Before reliability and metrics fixes; Compose timing included synchronous ClickHouse metric retry logging, removed in Phase 10 |
113: | Phase 8                 |      18.493 |       2.268 |                                                                                                                                |
114: | Phase 10                |       2.118 |       2.120 | `--latency 5`                                                                                                                  |
115: | Phase 11                |       2.154 |       2.118 | `--latency 5`                                                                                                                  |
116: 
117: Do not read the later numbers as a performance improvement: different sample sizes (20 vs 5), one tiny fixture each, a 2-second polling floor, and a baseline that included a since-fixed overhead. They show only that nothing got worse than the 25% regression gate allowed.
118: 
119: Other reported figures: an invalid image failed in about 92 seconds before the reliability work and about 3 seconds after; a successful OCR job took 2.04 s and an ASR job 4.06 s in the first baseline.
120: 
121: ### `load-test.yml` (Artillery)
122: 
123: Phases of 5, 20 and 100 requests per second for 30, 30 and 15 seconds, against OCR, ASR and `GET /v1/models`. Setup is in [Getting started](getting-started.md#load-test). The recorded baseline measured **enqueue latency** for accepted requests only (p50 2 ms, p95 4 ms, p99 29 ms) and noted that about two thirds of the requests in that run were rejected with `401` or `422`; it is not a throughput or success-rate figure.
124: 
125: ### `ws_latency_compare.py`
126: 
127: `python ws_latency_compare.py <admin_key> <model> <file> [n]` sends `n` sequential WebSocket requests with the model registered over HTTP and then over gRPC, and prints min, p50, p95, p99, max and mean. Kubernetes only (it writes in-cluster DNS names into the registry), and it **leaves the model registered as gRPC** afterwards. Reported results: [Registry and canary](registry-and-canary.md#switching-a-models-protocol-websocket-path-only).
128: 
129: ### `test_ws_client.py` and `test_ws_client.html`
130: 
131: Reference WebSocket clients, described in [API](api.md#websocket-v1wsinfermodel_name).
132: 
133: ## History of the suite
134: 
135: Full-suite counts reported at the end of each phase: 20 passed and 1 expected failure (Phase 7), 43 (Phase 8), 54 (Phase 9), 64 (Phase 10), 75 (Phase 11), **102** after the review follow-up (reproduced here). The run before any hardening had 20 failing tests and 1 passing, caused by an asyncio loop mismatch, tests assuming a running database backend, and a module-name collision.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[PY]  export_code.py
================================================================================
  1: import os
  2: 
  3: 
  4: def get_code_files(directory, excluded_files=None, excluded_dirs=None):
  5:     """Fetch all Python/SQL/YAML/Docker project files excluding sensitive configuration files."""
  6:     if excluded_files is None:
  7:         excluded_files = {
  8:             # Lock files & OS artifacts
  9:             "poetry.lock",
 10:             "Pipfile.lock",
 11:             ".DS_Store",
 12:             "Thumbs.db",
 13:             "Desktop.ini",
 14:             # Sensitive configuration files
 15:             ".env",
 16:             ".env.development",
 17:             ".env.production",
 18:             ".env.local",
 19:             ".env.staging",
 20:             ".env.test",
 21:             "keys.py",
 22:             "secrets.py",
 23:             "credentials.py",
 24:             "auth_config.py",
 25:             "aws_config.py",
 26:             "api_keys.py",
 27:             # Generated / exported artifacts — no need to include in review
 28:             "ModelMesh_Code_Export.pdf",
 29:             "Code_Export.pdf",
 30:             "ModelMesh_Code_Export.txt",
 31:             "Code_Export.txt",
 32:         }
 33: 
 34:     if excluded_dirs is None:
 35:         excluded_dirs = {
 36:             "modelmesh-env",   # Python virtualenv
 37:             ".venv",
 38:             "venv",
 39:             ".git",
 40:             "__pycache__",
 41:             ".pytest_cache",
 42:             ".mypy_cache",
 43:             "build",
 44:             "dist",
 45:             ".idea",
 46:             ".vscode",
 47:             "logs",
 48:             "uploads",
 49:             "secrets",
 50:             ".env.d",
 51:             "node_modules",    # in case any frontend creeps in
 52:         }
 53: 
 54:     code_files = {}
 55: 
 56:     # Source code extensions to include
 57:     source_extensions = {".py", ".sql"}
 58: 
 59:     # Safe config / meta files to include
 60:     safe_config_files = {
 61:         "requirements.txt",
 62:         "pytest.ini",
 63:         "docker-compose.yml",
 64:         "docker-compose.yaml",
 65:         "Dockerfile",
 66:         ".dockerignore",
 67:         ".gitignore",
 68:         "load-test.yml",
 69:         "load-test.yaml",
 70:         "init.sql",
 71:         "entrypoint.sh",
 72:         "README.md",
 73:     }
 74: 
 75:     # Extensions that are always safe/plain-text config even without a fixed filename
 76:     safe_config_extensions = {".yml", ".yaml", ".md", ".sh", ".ini", ".cfg", ".toml", ".html"}
 77: 
 78:     for root, dirs, files in os.walk(directory):
 79:         # Prune excluded directories in-place so os.walk won't descend into them
 80:         dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in excluded_dirs]
 81: 
 82:         # Also skip if any folder INSIDE the project is excluded
 83:         # (only the part below the project root is checked, so a project that
 84:         # lives under e.g. ".../logs/ModelMesh" is not skipped by mistake)
 85:         rel_root = os.path.relpath(root, directory)
 86:         if rel_root != "." and any(
 87:             part in excluded_dirs for part in rel_root.replace("\\", "/").split("/")
 88:         ):
 89:             continue
 90: 
 91:         for file in files:
 92:             # Skip explicitly excluded files
 93:             if file in excluded_files:
 94:                 continue
 95: 
 96:             # Skip files that look like they contain secrets
 97:             if any(
 98:                 pattern in file.lower()
 99:                 for pattern in [".env", "secret", "password", "credential", "apikey", "api_key"]
100:             ):
101:                 continue
102: 
103:             # Skip binary / image assets even if they sneak past directory filters
104:             _, ext = os.path.splitext(file)
105:             if ext.lower() in {
106:                 ".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".gif", ".svg", ".webp",
107:                 ".ico", ".woff", ".woff2", ".ttf", ".eot",
108:                 ".zip", ".pdf", ".map", ".pyc", ".so",
109:             }:
110:                 continue
111: 
112:             file_path = os.path.join(root, file)
113: 
114:             if (
115:                 ext.lower() in source_extensions
116:                 or file in safe_config_files
117:                 or ext.lower() in safe_config_extensions
118:             ):
119:                 try:
120:                     # errors="replace" keeps every character visible instead of
121:                     # silently dropping undecodable bytes
122:                     with open(file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
123:                         code_files[file_path] = f.read().splitlines()
124:                 except Exception as e:
125:                     print(f"❌ Error reading {file_path}: {e}")
126:                     code_files[file_path] = [f"[Error reading file: {str(e)}]"]
127: 
128:     return code_files
129: 
130: 
131: def get_file_type_label(path):
132:     """Return a short bracketed label for the file type."""
133:     if path.endswith(".py"):
134:         return "[PY]"
135:     if path.endswith(".sql"):
136:         return "[SQL]"
137:     if path.endswith(".yml") or path.endswith(".yaml"):
138:         return "[YAML]"
139:     if path.endswith(".sh"):
140:         return "[SH]"
141:     if path.endswith(".ini") or path.endswith(".cfg") or path.endswith(".toml"):
142:         return "[CFG]"
143:     if path.endswith(".md"):
144:         return "[MD]"
145:     if path.endswith("requirements.txt"):
146:         return "[REQ]"
147:     if "Dockerfile" in path or "docker-compose" in path or ".dockerignore" in path:
148:         return "[DOCKER]"
149:     return "[CONFIG]"
150: 
151: 
152: def create_txt(code_data, root_dir, output_txt="ModelMesh_Code_Export.txt"):
153:     file_paths = sorted(code_data.keys())
154:     rule = "=" * 80
155:     thin = "-" * 80
156: 
157:     with open(output_txt, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as out:
158:         # ── Title + file index ───────────────────────────────────────────────
159:         out.write("ModelMesh — Project Code Export\n")
160:         out.write(f"{rule}\n")
161:         out.write("Included: Python / SQL / YAML / Docker / Shell / Config\n\n")
162: 
163:         out.write(f"FILES ({len(file_paths)})\n")
164:         out.write(f"{thin}\n")
165:         for path in file_paths:
166:             display_path = os.path.relpath(path, root_dir)
167:             out.write(f"- {get_file_type_label(display_path)} {display_path}\n")
168:         out.write("\n")
169: 
170:         # ── File contents (no wrapping, no truncation) ───────────────────────
171:         for file_path in file_paths:
172:             lines = code_data[file_path]
173:             print(f"  Adding: {file_path}")
174: 
175:             rel_path = os.path.relpath(file_path, root_dir)
176:             label = get_file_type_label(rel_path)
177: 
178:             out.write(f"\n{label}  {rel_path}\n")
179:             out.write(f"{rule}\n")
180: 
181:             width = max(3, len(str(len(lines))))
182:             for line_num, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
183:                 out.write(f"{line_num:{width}d}: {line}\n")
184: 
185:             out.write(f"{thin}\n")
186: 
187:     print(f"\n✅ TXT created: {output_txt}")
188:     print(f"   Total files: {len(code_data)}")
189: 
190:     # Breakdown
191:     py_count    = sum(1 for f in code_data if f.endswith(".py"))
192:     sql_count   = sum(1 for f in code_data if f.endswith(".sql"))
193:     yaml_count  = sum(1 for f in code_data if f.endswith((".yml", ".yaml")))
194:     other       = len(code_data) - py_count - sql_count - yaml_count
195: 
196:     print(f"   Python files : {py_count}")
197:     print(f"   SQL files    : {sql_count}")
198:     print(f"   YAML files   : {yaml_count}")
199:     print(f"   Other        : {other}")
200: 
201: 
202: def main():
203:     root_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
204: 
205:     print("🔍 Scanning ModelMesh project for Python/SQL/YAML/Docker/config files...")
206:     print("🔒 Sensitive files, venv/, .git/, __pycache__/, and binary assets excluded.\n")
207: 
208:     code_files = get_code_files(root_dir)
209: 
210:     if not code_files:
211:         print("❌ No source files found!")
212:         return
213: 
214:     print(f"📁 {len(code_files)} files will be included:\n")
215:     for fp in sorted(code_files.keys()):
216:         rel = os.path.relpath(fp, root_dir)
217:         print(f"   {get_file_type_label(rel)} {rel}")
218: 
219:     print()
220:     create_txt(code_files, root_dir)
221: 
222: 
223: if __name__ == "__main__":
224:     main()
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[PY]  gateway\auth.py
================================================================================
  1: import os
  2: import secrets
  3: import hashlib
  4: import logging
  5: from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
  6: from typing import Optional
  7: 
  8: import bcrypt
  9: import jwt
 10: from databases import Database
 11: from fastapi import HTTPException, Header
 12: from dataclasses import dataclass
 13: 
 14: from db import database
 15: 
 16: SECRET_KEY = os.getenv("JWT_SECRET", "change-me-in-production")
 17: if SECRET_KEY == "change-me-in-production":
 18:     logging.getLogger(__name__).warning(
 19:         "JWT_SECRET is using the insecure placeholder; set JWT_SECRET in production"
 20:     )
 21: ALGORITHM = "HS256"
 22: ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES = 60 * 24  # 24 hours
 23: 
 24: 
 25: @dataclass
 26: class ApiKeyRecord:
 27:     id: str
 28:     user_id: str
 29:     rate_limit_per_min: int
 30:     is_admin: bool = False
 31: 
 32: 
 33: def hash_password(password: str) -> str:
 34:     return bcrypt.hashpw(password.encode(), bcrypt.gensalt()).decode()
 35: 
 36: 
 37: def verify_password(password: str, hashed: str) -> bool:
 38:     return bcrypt.checkpw(password.encode(), hashed.encode())
 39: 
 40: 
 41: def create_access_token(user_id: str) -> str:
 42:     expire = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES)
 43:     return jwt.encode({"sub": user_id, "exp": expire}, SECRET_KEY, algorithm=ALGORITHM)
 44: 
 45: 
 46: def decode_token(token: str) -> Optional[str]:
 47:     try:
 48:         payload = jwt.decode(token, SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[ALGORITHM])
 49:         return payload.get("sub")
 50:     except jwt.PyJWTError:
 51:         return None
 52: 
 53: 
 54: def _hash_key(raw_key: str) -> str:
 55:     return hashlib.sha256(raw_key.encode()).hexdigest()
 56: 
 57: 
 58: async def create_api_key(
 59:     db: Database, user_id: str, rate_limit_per_min: int = 60, is_admin: bool = False
 60: ) -> str:
 61:     raw_key = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
 62:     key_hash = _hash_key(raw_key)
 63:     await db.execute(
 64:         """INSERT INTO api_keys (user_id, key_hash, rate_limit_per_min, is_admin)
 65:            VALUES (:user_id, :key_hash, :rate_limit_per_min, :is_admin)""",
 66:         {
 67:             "user_id": user_id,
 68:             "key_hash": key_hash,
 69:             "rate_limit_per_min": rate_limit_per_min,
 70:             "is_admin": is_admin,
 71:         },
 72:     )
 73:     return raw_key
 74: 
 75: 
 76: async def lookup_key(db: Database, raw_key: str) -> Optional[ApiKeyRecord]:
 77:     key_hash = _hash_key(raw_key)
 78:     row = await db.fetch_one(
 79:         "SELECT id, user_id, rate_limit_per_min, is_admin FROM api_keys WHERE key_hash = :h AND revoked = FALSE",
 80:         {"h": key_hash},
 81:     )
 82:     if not row:
 83:         return None
 84:     return ApiKeyRecord(
 85:         id=str(row["id"]),
 86:         user_id=str(row["user_id"]),
 87:         rate_limit_per_min=row["rate_limit_per_min"],
 88:         is_admin=row["is_admin"],
 89:     )
 90: 
 91: 
 92: _lookup_key = lookup_key
 93: 
 94: 
 95: def _extract_bearer(authorization: Optional[str]) -> str:
 96:     if not authorization or not authorization.startswith("Bearer "):
 97:         raise HTTPException(401, "Missing or invalid Authorization header")
 98:     return authorization.removeprefix("Bearer ").strip()
 99: 
100: 
101: async def verify_api_key(authorization: Optional[str] = Header(None)) -> ApiKeyRecord:
102:     raw_key = _extract_bearer(authorization)
103:     record = await lookup_key(database, raw_key)
104:     if not record:
105:         raise HTTPException(401, "Invalid or revoked API key")
106:     return record
107: 
108: 
109: async def verify_admin_key(authorization: Optional[str] = Header(None)) -> ApiKeyRecord:
110:     record = await verify_api_key(authorization)
111:     if not record.is_admin:
112:         raise HTTPException(403, "Admin key required")
113:     return record

[PY]  gateway\canary_guard.py
================================================================================
  1: import argparse
  2: import json
  3: import os
  4: import re
  5: import sys
  6: from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
  7: from datetime import datetime, timezone
  8: from typing import Any
  9: 
 10: 
 11: @dataclass
 12: class Stats:
 13:     n: int
 14:     err: float
 15: 
 16: 
 17: @dataclass
 18: class Config:
 19:     min_requests: int = 30
 20:     error_delta: float = 0.10
 21: 
 22: 
 23: def decide(
 24:     stable: Stats, canary: Stats, canary_percent: int, cfg: Config
 25: ) -> tuple[str, str]:
 26:     if canary_percent == 0:
 27:         return "noop", "canary already at 0"
 28:     if stable.n < cfg.min_requests:
 29:         return "hold", "not enough stable traffic"
 30:     expected = stable.n * canary_percent / max(1, 100 - canary_percent)
 31:     if expected >= cfg.min_requests and canary.n < 0.2 * expected:
 32:         return (
 33:             "rollback",
 34:             f"canary starved: {canary.n} completed vs about {expected:.0f} expected",
 35:         )
 36:     if canary.n < cfg.min_requests:
 37:         return "hold", "not enough canary traffic"
 38:     if canary.err - stable.err > cfg.error_delta:
 39:         return (
 40:             "rollback",
 41:             f"canary error rate {canary.err:.2f} vs stable {stable.err:.2f}",
 42:         )
 43:     return "hold", "healthy"
 44: 
 45: 
 46: def _version_number(version: str) -> int:
 47:     match = re.search(r"\d+", version)
 48:     return int(match.group()) if match else 0
 49: 
 50: 
 51: def select_stable_and_canary(
 52:     models: list[dict[str, Any]],
 53: ) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]] | None:
 54:     active = [model for model in models if model["status"] == "active"]
 55:     if len(active) < 2:
 56:         return None
 57: 
 58:     stable = min(active, key=lambda model: _version_number(model["version"]))
 59:     canary = max(active, key=lambda model: _version_number(model["version"]))
 60:     return stable, canary
 61: 
 62: 
 63: def query_model_stats(
 64:     model_pairs: list[tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]],
 65:     window_min: int,
 66:     host: str,
 67:     port: int,
 68: ) -> dict[str, tuple[Stats, Stats]]:
 69:     import clickhouse_connect
 70: 
 71:     client = clickhouse_connect.get_client(host=host, port=port)
 72:     results: dict[str, tuple[Stats, Stats]] = {}
 73:     query = """
 74:         SELECT model_version, count() AS n,
 75:                countIf(status = 'error') / count() AS err
 76:         FROM request_metrics
 77:         WHERE model_name = {name:String}
 78:           AND timestamp > now() - INTERVAL {window:UInt32} MINUTE
 79:         GROUP BY model_version
 80:     """
 81: 
 82:     for stable, canary in model_pairs:
 83:         rows = client.query(
 84:             query,
 85:             parameters={"name": canary["name"], "window": window_min},
 86:         ).result_rows
 87:         stats = {
 88:             version: Stats(int(n), float(err))
 89:             for version, n, err in rows
 90:         }
 91:         results[canary["name"]] = (
 92:             stats.get(stable["version"], Stats(0, 0.0)),
 93:             stats.get(canary["version"], Stats(0, 0.0)),
 94:         )
 95:     return results
 96: 
 97: 
 98: def _positive_int(value: str) -> int:
 99:     try:
100:         result = int(value)
101:     except ValueError as exc:
102:         raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("must be an integer") from exc
103:     if result < 1:
104:         raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("must be greater than zero")
105:     return result
106: 
107: 
108: def _error_delta(value: str) -> float:
109:     try:
110:         result = float(value)
111:     except ValueError as exc:
112:         raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("must be a number") from exc
113:     if not 0 <= result <= 1:
114:         raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("must be between 0 and 1")
115:     return result
116: 
117: 
118: def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
119:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Evaluate active model canaries.")
120:     parser.add_argument("--window-min", type=_positive_int, default=10)
121:     parser.add_argument("--min-requests", type=_positive_int, default=30)
122:     parser.add_argument("--error-delta", type=_error_delta, default=0.10)
123:     parser.add_argument("--apply", action="store_true")
124:     args = parser.parse_args(argv)
125: 
126:     gateway_url = os.getenv("GATEWAY_URL", "http://localhost:8000").rstrip("/")
127:     admin_key = os.getenv("ADMIN_KEY")
128:     if args.apply and not admin_key:
129:         parser.error("--apply requires ADMIN_KEY in the environment")
130: 
131:     clickhouse_port = _positive_int(os.getenv("CLICKHOUSE_PORT", "8123"))
132: 
133:     import httpx
134: 
135:     with httpx.Client(timeout=10.0) as http:
136:         response = http.get(f"{gateway_url}/v1/models")
137:         response.raise_for_status()
138:         models = response.json()
139:         if not isinstance(models, list):
140:             raise ValueError("GET /v1/models did not return a JSON array")
141: 
142:         grouped: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
143:         for model in models:
144:             grouped.setdefault(model["name"], []).append(model)
145: 
146:         model_pairs = [
147:             pair
148:             for name in sorted(grouped)
149:             if (pair := select_stable_and_canary(grouped[name])) is not None
150:         ]
151:         query_pairs = [
152:             pair for pair in model_pairs if pair[1]["canary_percent"] > 0
153:         ]
154:         stats_by_model = (
155:             query_model_stats(
156:                 query_pairs,
157:                 args.window_min,
158:                 os.getenv("CLICKHOUSE_HOST", "clickhouse"),
159:                 clickhouse_port,
160:             )
161:             if query_pairs
162:             else {}
163:         )
164: 
165:         output = []
166:         for stable_model, canary_model in model_pairs:
167:             if canary_model["canary_percent"] == 0:
168:                 stable_stats, canary_stats = Stats(0, 0.0), Stats(0, 0.0)
169:             else:
170:                 stable_stats, canary_stats = stats_by_model[canary_model["name"]]
171:             decision, reason = decide(
172:                 stable_stats,
173:                 canary_stats,
174:                 canary_model["canary_percent"],
175:                 Config(args.min_requests, args.error_delta),
176:             )
177:             applied = False
178:             if args.apply and decision == "rollback":
179:                 patch = http.patch(
180:                     f"{gateway_url}/v1/models/{canary_model['id']}",
181:                     json={"canary_percent": 0},
182:                     headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {admin_key}"},
183:                 )
184:                 patch.raise_for_status()
185:                 applied = True
186: 
187:             output.append(
188:                 {
189:                     "ts": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat().replace(
190:                         "+00:00", "Z"
191:                     ),
192:                     "model": canary_model["name"],
193:                     "stable": stable_model["version"],
194:                     "canary": canary_model["version"],
195:                     "canary_percent": canary_model["canary_percent"],
196:                     "stable_stats": asdict(stable_stats),
197:                     "canary_stats": asdict(canary_stats),
198:                     "decision": decision,
199:                     "reason": reason,
200:                     "applied": applied,
201:                 }
202:             )
203: 
204:     for record in output:
205:         print(json.dumps(record))
206:     return 0
207: 
208: 
209: if __name__ == "__main__":
210:     sys.exit(main())
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[PY]  gateway\grpc_client.py
================================================================================
  1: import grpc
  2: import json
  3: import logging
  4: import os
  5: import inference_pb2
  6: import inference_pb2_grpc
  7: 
  8: logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
  9: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
 10: MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = int(os.getenv("MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES", "10485760"))
 11: MAX_MSG = MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES + 1048576
 12: 
 13: 
 14: async def call_worker_grpc(endpoint: str, model_name: str, payload: bytes) -> dict:
 15:     """
 16:     Call worker via gRPC Predict RPC (unary).
 17: 
 18:     Args:
 19:         endpoint: Worker gRPC endpoint (e.g., "doc-ocr-worker:50051")
 20:         model_name: Model name for the request
 21:         payload: Raw bytes to process
 22: 
 23:     Returns:
 24:         dict: Parsed result from worker
 25:     """
 26:     try:
 27:         channel_options = [
 28:             ("grpc.max_send_message_length", MAX_MSG),
 29:             ("grpc.max_receive_message_length", MAX_MSG),
 30:         ]
 31:         async with grpc.aio.insecure_channel(endpoint, options=channel_options) as channel:
 32:             stub = inference_pb2_grpc.InferenceWorkerStub(channel)
 33: 
 34:             request = inference_pb2.InferenceRequest(
 35:                 payload=payload,
 36:                 model_name=model_name
 37:             )
 38: 
 39:             response = await stub.Predict(request, timeout=30.0)
 40: 
 41:             logger.info(f"gRPC call to {endpoint} completed in {response.latency_ms:.2f}ms")
 42: 
 43:             return json.loads(response.result_json)
 44: 
 45:     except grpc.RpcError as e:
 46:         logger.error(f"gRPC error calling {endpoint}: {e.code()}: {e.details()}")
 47:         raise RuntimeError(f"Worker gRPC call failed: {e.details()}")
 48:     except Exception as e:
 49:         logger.error(f"Error calling worker via gRPC: {e}")
 50:         raise
 51: 
 52: 
 53: async def stream_worker_grpc(endpoint: str, model_name: str, chunks):
 54:     """
 55:     Call worker via gRPC PredictStream RPC (bidirectional streaming).
 56: 
 57:     Args:
 58:         endpoint: Worker gRPC endpoint
 59:         model_name: Model name
 60:         chunks: Async generator/iterator of (bytes, sequence, is_final) tuples
 61: 
 62:     Yields:
 63:         dict: Partial results as they arrive
 64:     """
 65:     try:
 66:         channel_options = [
 67:             ("grpc.max_send_message_length", MAX_MSG),
 68:             ("grpc.max_receive_message_length", MAX_MSG),
 69:         ]
 70:         async with grpc.aio.insecure_channel(endpoint, options=channel_options) as channel:
 71:             stub = inference_pb2_grpc.InferenceWorkerStub(channel)
 72: 
 73:             # Create request generator
 74:             async def request_generator():
 75:                 async for chunk_data, sequence, is_final in chunks:
 76:                     yield inference_pb2.InferenceChunk(
 77:                         chunk=chunk_data,
 78:                         sequence=sequence,
 79:                         is_final=is_final
 80:                     )
 81: 
 82:             # Call streaming RPC
 83:             response_stream = stub.PredictStream(request_generator(), timeout=60.0)
 84: 
 85:             # Yield partial results as they arrive
 86:             async for result in response_stream:
 87:                 yield {
 88:                     "text": result.partial_text,
 89:                     "is_final": result.is_final,
 90:                     "confidence": result.confidence
 91:                 }
 92: 
 93:     except grpc.RpcError as e:
 94:         logger.error(f"gRPC streaming error: {e.code()}: {e.details()}")
 95:         raise RuntimeError(f"Worker gRPC stream failed: {e.details()}")
 96:     except Exception as e:
 97:         logger.error(f"Error in gRPC streaming: {e}")
 98:         raise
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[PY]  gateway\job_queue.py
================================================================================
  1: import json
  2: import os
  3: import uuid
  4: from typing import Optional
  5: from redis.asyncio import Redis
  6: 
  7: from registry import ModelRecord
  8: 
  9: PAYLOAD_TTL = int(os.getenv("PAYLOAD_TTL_SECONDS", "3600"))
 10: STREAM_MAXLEN = int(os.getenv("STREAM_MAXLEN", "10000"))
 11: JOB_OWNER_TTL = 7200
 12: 
 13: 
 14: async def enqueue_job(
 15:     redis: Redis,
 16:     model: ModelRecord,
 17:     payload: bytes,
 18:     api_key_id: str,
 19:     owner_id: str,
 20: ) -> str:
 21:     """
 22:     Enqueue an inference job to Redis Streams.
 23: 
 24:     Args:
 25:         redis: Async Redis client
 26:         model: ModelRecord resolved from registry
 27:         payload: Raw file bytes to process
 28:         api_key_id: ID of the API key making the request
 29:         owner_id: ID of the user who owns the job
 30: 
 31:     Returns:
 32:         job_id: Unique job identifier for status tracking
 33:     """
 34:     job_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
 35: 
 36:     await redis.setex(f"payload:{job_id}", PAYLOAD_TTL, payload)
 37:     await redis.setex(f"job:{job_id}:owner", JOB_OWNER_TTL, owner_id)
 38:     await redis.setex(
 39:         f"job:{job_id}:status",
 40:         3600,
 41:         json.dumps({
 42:             "job_id": job_id,
 43:             "status": "queued",
 44:             "model_name": model.name,
 45:             "model_version": model.version
 46:         })
 47:     )
 48: 
 49:     await redis.xadd("inference_jobs", {
 50:         "job_id": job_id,
 51:         "model_id": model.id,
 52:         "model_name": model.name,
 53:         "model_version": model.version,
 54:         "worker_name": model.worker_name,
 55:         "api_key_id": api_key_id,
 56:         "status": "queued"
 57:     }, maxlen=STREAM_MAXLEN, approximate=True)
 58: 
 59:     return job_id
 60: 
 61: 
 62: async def get_job_status(
 63:     redis: Redis, job_id: str, owner_id: str
 64: ) -> Optional[dict]:
 65:     """
 66:     Retrieve job status and result from Redis.
 67: 
 68:     Args:
 69:         redis: Async Redis client
 70:         job_id: Job identifier
 71:         owner_id: ID of the user requesting the job
 72: 
 73:     Returns:
 74:         dict with job_id, status, and result (if available), or None if expired
 75:     """
 76:     owner = await redis.get(f"job:{job_id}:owner")
 77:     if owner != owner_id:
 78:         return None
 79: 
 80:     # Check for result first
 81:     result = await redis.get(f"result:{job_id}")
 82:     if result:
 83:         return json.loads(result)
 84: 
 85:     # Check for status
 86:     status = await redis.get(f"job:{job_id}:status")
 87:     if status:
 88:         return json.loads(status)
 89: 
 90:     return None
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[PY]  gateway\kafka_queue.py
================================================================================
  1: # DORMANT: not imported by the gateway and not deployed. See README.
  2: import json
  3: import uuid
  4: from typing import Optional
  5: from aiokafka import AIOKafkaProducer
  6: from redis.asyncio import Redis
  7: 
  8: from registry import ModelRecord
  9: 
 10: 
 11: # Global Kafka producer (initialized at startup)
 12: kafka_producer: Optional[AIOKafkaProducer] = None
 13: 
 14: 
 15: async def init_kafka_producer(bootstrap_servers: str = "kafka:9092"):
 16:     """Initialize Kafka producer at startup."""
 17:     global kafka_producer
 18:     kafka_producer = AIOKafkaProducer(
 19:         bootstrap_servers=bootstrap_servers,
 20:         value_serializer=lambda v: json.dumps(v).encode("utf-8"),
 21:         key_serializer=lambda k: k.encode("utf-8") if k else None,
 22:     )
 23:     await kafka_producer.start()
 24: 
 25: 
 26: async def close_kafka_producer():
 27:     """Close Kafka producer at shutdown."""
 28:     global kafka_producer
 29:     if kafka_producer:
 30:         await kafka_producer.stop()
 31: 
 32: 
 33: async def enqueue_job_kafka(redis: Redis, model: ModelRecord, payload: bytes, api_key_id: str) -> str:
 34:     """
 35:     Enqueue an inference job to Kafka.
 36: 
 37:     Args:
 38:         redis: Async Redis client (for storing job status)
 39:         model: ModelRecord resolved from registry
 40:         payload: Raw file bytes to process
 41:         api_key_id: ID of the API key making the request
 42: 
 43:     Returns:
 44:         job_id: Unique job identifier for status tracking
 45:     """
 46:     if not kafka_producer:
 47:         raise RuntimeError("Kafka producer not initialized")
 48: 
 49:     job_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
 50: 
 51:     job_data = {
 52:         "job_id": job_id,
 53:         "model_id": model.id,
 54:         "model_name": model.name,
 55:         "model_version": model.version,
 56:         "worker_name": model.worker_name,
 57:         "payload": payload.hex(),  # Encode bytes as hex string for JSON
 58:         "api_key_id": api_key_id,
 59:         "status": "queued"
 60:     }
 61: 
 62:     # Send to Kafka, partitioned by worker_name
 63:     await kafka_producer.send_and_wait(
 64:         "inference-jobs",
 65:         value=job_data,
 66:         key=model.worker_name
 67:     )
 68: 
 69:     # Store initial job status in Redis
 70:     await redis.setex(
 71:         f"job:{job_id}:status",
 72:         3600,  # 1 hour TTL
 73:         json.dumps({
 74:             "job_id": job_id,
 75:             "status": "queued",
 76:             "model_name": model.name,
 77:             "model_version": model.version
 78:         })
 79:     )
 80: 
 81:     return job_id
 82: 
 83: 
 84: async def update_job_status_kafka(redis: Redis, job_id: str, status: str, result: Optional[dict] = None, error: Optional[str] = None):
 85:     """
 86:     Update job status in Redis (same as Redis Streams version).
 87: 
 88:     Args:
 89:         redis: Async Redis client
 90:         job_id: Job identifier
 91:         status: New status (processing, success, failed)
 92:         result: Optional result data for successful jobs
 93:         error: Optional error message for failed jobs
 94:     """
 95:     data = {
 96:         "job_id": job_id,
 97:         "status": status
 98:     }
 99: 
100:     if result:
101:         data["result"] = result
102: 
103:     if error:
104:         data["error"] = error
105: 
106:     if status in ("success", "failed"):
107:         await redis.setex(f"result:{job_id}", 7200, json.dumps(data))
108:         await redis.delete(f"job:{job_id}:status")
109:     else:
110:         await redis.setex(f"job:{job_id}:status", 3600, json.dumps(data))

 42: class _ApiKeyAccessLogFilter(logging.Filter):
 43:     def filter(self, record: logging.LogRecord) -> bool:
 44:         args = record.args
 45:         if record.name.startswith("uvicorn.") and isinstance(args, tuple):
 46:             record.args = tuple(
 47:                 re.sub(r"([?&]api_key=)[^&\s]+", r"\1[REDACTED]", value)
 48:                 if isinstance(value, str)
 49:                 else value

 75: 
 76: def _jwt_dep():
 77:     """Verify JWT token and return user_id — used for issuing API keys."""
 78:     async def inner(authorization: str | None = Header(None)) -> str:
 79:         if not authorization or not authorization.startswith("Bearer "):
 80:             raise HTTPException(401, "Missing or invalid Authorization header")
 81:         token = authorization.removeprefix("Bearer ").strip()
 82:         user_id = decode_token(token)
 83:         if not user_id:
 84:             raise HTTPException(401, "Invalid or expired token")
 85:         return user_id
 86:     return Depends(inner)
 87: 
 88: 
 89: async def route_request(endpoint: str, protocol: str, payload: bytes, filename: str = "file", model_name: str = ""):
 90:     if protocol == "http":
 91:         async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=30.0) as client:
 92:             files = {"file": (filename, payload, "application/octet-stream")}
 93:             resp = await client.post(endpoint, files=files)
 94:             resp.raise_for_status()
 95:             return resp.json()
 96:     elif protocol == "grpc":
 97:         return await call_worker_grpc(endpoint, model_name, payload)
 98:     else:
 99:         raise ValueError(f"Unsupported protocol: {protocol}")
100: 
101: 
102: async def get_model_with_canary(db, model_name: str):
103:     """
104:     Registry-driven canary routing. With two or more active versions, the
105:     highest numeric version is the canary and the lowest numeric version is
106:     stable; versions in between receive no traffic. A model with no active
107:     version is disabled.
108:     """
109:     versions = await registry.list_versions(db, model_name)
110:     if not versions:
111:         model = await registry.get_model(db, model_name)
112:         if model is not None:
113:             raise HTTPException(503, "Model is currently disabled")
114:         return None
115:     if len(versions) == 1:
116:         return versions[0]
117:     canary = max(versions, key=_vnum)
118:     stable = min(versions, key=_vnum)
119:     roll = random.randint(1, 100)
120:     return canary if roll <= canary.canary_percent else stable
121: 
122: 
123: def _vnum(model) -> int:
124:     found = re.search(r"\d+", model.version)
125:     return int(found.group()) if found else 0
126:

130: @app.post("/v1/auth/register", tags=["auth"])
131: async def register(payload: UserCreate):
132:     existing = await database.fetch_one(
133:         "SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = :email", {"email": payload.email}
134:     )
135:     if existing:
136:         raise HTTPException(400, "Email already registered")
137:     password_hash = hash_password(payload.password)
138:     await database.execute(
139:         "INSERT INTO users (email, password_hash) VALUES (:email, :pw)",
140:         {"email": payload.email, "pw": password_hash},
141:     )
142:     return {"message": "User registered"}
143: 
144: 
145: @app.post("/v1/auth/login", response_model=TokenResponse, tags=["auth"])
146: async def login(payload: LoginRequest):
147:     row = await database.fetch_one(
148:         "SELECT id, password_hash FROM users WHERE email = :email", {"email": payload.email}
149:     )
150:     if not row or not verify_password(payload.password, row["password_hash"]):
151:         raise HTTPException(401, "Invalid credentials")
152:     token = create_access_token(str(row["id"]))
153:     return {"access_token": token}
154:

156: @app.post("/v1/auth/api-keys", response_model=ApiKeyResponse, tags=["auth"])
157: async def issue_api_key(payload: ApiKeyCreate, user_id=_jwt_dep()):
158:     raw_key = await create_api_key(database, user_id, payload.rate_limit_per_min)
159:     return {"key": raw_key, "rate_limit_per_min": payload.rate_limit_per_min}

162: # ── inference endpoint ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
163: 
164: @app.post("/v1/infer/{model_name}", tags=["inference"])
165: async def infer(
166:     model_name: str,
167:     file: UploadFile = File(...),
168:     version: str | None = None,
169:     key: ApiKeyRecord = Depends(verify_api_key),
170: ):
171:     await check_rate_limit(key.id, key.rate_limit_per_min, redis)
172: 
173:     if version is None:
174:         # No version pinned by the caller — apply registry-driven canary routing.
175:         model = await get_model_with_canary(database, model_name)
176:     else:
177:         # Caller explicitly pinned a version (e.g. ?version=v1) — always honor it,
178:         # bypassing canary logic entirely. This is what lets some traffic stay
179:         # deliberately on stable during a rollout.
180:         model = await registry.get_model(database, model_name, version)
181: 
182:     if not model:
183:         raise HTTPException(404, "Unknown model")
184:     if model.status != "active":
185:         raise HTTPException(503, "Model is currently disabled")
186: 
187:     payload_bytes = await file.read(MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES + 1)
188:     if len(payload_bytes) > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES:
189:         raise HTTPException(413, "Upload exceeds maximum allowed size")
190: 
191:     job_id = await enqueue_job(redis, model, payload_bytes, key.id, key.user_id)
192: 
193:     return {
194:         "job_id": job_id,
195:         "status": "queued",
196:         "model": model.name,
197:         "version": model.version,
198:     }
199:

203:     result = await _get_job_status(redis, job_id, key.user_id)
204:     if not result:
205:         raise HTTPException(404, "Job not found or expired")
206:     return result

277:     try:
278:         await database.fetch_one("SELECT 1")
279:     except Exception as exc:
280:         logger.warning("Readiness check failed: Postgres unavailable: %s", exc)
281:         raise HTTPException(503, "Not ready: Postgres unavailable") from exc
282:     try:
283:         await redis.ping()
284:         return {"status": "ready"}
285:     except Exception as exc:
286:         logger.warning("Readiness check failed: Redis unavailable: %s", exc)
287:         raise HTTPException(503, "Not ready: Redis unavailable") from exc

289: 
290: # ── WebSocket streaming endpoint ─────────────────────────────────────────────
291: 
292: @app.websocket("/v1/ws/infer/{model_name}")
293: async def ws_infer(websocket: WebSocket, model_name: str):
294:     """
295:     WebSocket endpoint for streaming inference.
296: 
297:     Query params:
298:         api_key: API key for authentication
299:         version: Model version (default: v1)
300: 
301:     Sends partial results as JSON messages: {"partial": result, "is_final": bool}
302:     """
303:     await websocket.accept()
304: 
305:     try:
306:         # Authenticate via query param
307:         api_key = websocket.query_params.get("api_key")
308:         if not api_key:
309:             await websocket.send_json({"error": "Missing api_key query parameter"})
310:             await websocket.close(code=4401)
311:             return
312: 
313:         # Verify API key
314:         key_record = await lookup_key(database, api_key)
315: 
316:         if not key_record:
317:             await websocket.send_json({"error": "Invalid API key"})
318:             await websocket.close(code=4401)
319:             return
320: 
321:         # Rate limiting
322:         await check_rate_limit(key_record.id, key_record.rate_limit_per_min, redis)
323: 
324:         # Resolve model from registry
325:         version = websocket.query_params.get("version", "v1")
326:         model = await registry.get_model(database, model_name, version)
327: 
328:         if not model:
329:             await websocket.send_json({"error": "Unknown model"})
330:             await websocket.close(code=4404)
331:             return
332: 
333:         if model.status != "active":
334:             await websocket.send_json({"error": "Model is currently disabled"})
335:             await websocket.close(code=4503)
336:             return
337: 
338:         # Send ready signal
339:         await websocket.send_json({"status": "ready", "model": model.name, "protocol": model.protocol})
340: 
341:         # Handle streaming based on protocol
342:         if model.protocol == "grpc" and model.supports_streaming:
343:             from grpc_client import stream_worker_grpc
344: 
345:             chunk_count = 0
346: 
347:             async def chunk_generator():
348:                 nonlocal chunk_count
349:                 try:
350:                     while True:
351:                         data = await websocket.receive_bytes()
352:                         if len(data) > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES:
353:                             await websocket.close(code=1009)
354:                             return
355:                         chunk_count += 1
356:                         is_final = True  # simplified: one chunk for now
357:                         yield data, chunk_count, is_final
358:                         if is_final:
359:                             break
360:                 except WebSocketDisconnect:
361:                     pass
362: 
363:             async for partial_result in stream_worker_grpc(model.endpoint, model.name, chunk_generator()):
364:                 await websocket.send_json({
365:                     "partial": partial_result.get("text", ""),
366:                     "is_final": partial_result.get("is_final", False),
367:                     "confidence": partial_result.get("confidence", 0.0)
368:                 })
369: 
370:         else:
371:             # Covers: http protocol, AND grpc protocol without streaming support (e.g. doc-ocr)
372:             data = await websocket.receive_bytes()
373:             if len(data) > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES:
374:                 await websocket.close(code=1009)
375:                 return
376:             result = await route_request(model.endpoint, model.protocol, data, model_name=model.name)
377:             await websocket.send_json({"result": result, "is_final": True})
378: 
379:     except WebSocketDisconnect:
380:         pass
381:     except Exception as e:
382:         try:
383:             await websocket.send_json({"error": str(e)})
384:         except:
385:             pass
386:     finally:
387:         try:
388:             await websocket.close()
389:         except:
390:             pass
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[PY]  gateway\rate_limit.py
================================================================================
  1: from fastapi import HTTPException
  2: import redis.asyncio as aioredis
  3: 
  4: 
  5: async def check_rate_limit(key_id: str, limit_per_min: int, redis: aioredis.Redis) -> None:
  6:     redis_key = f"rl:{key_id}"
  7:     pipe = redis.pipeline()
  8:     pipe.set(redis_key, 0, ex=60, nx=True)
  9:     pipe.incr(redis_key)
 10:     _, count = await pipe.execute()
 11:     if count > limit_per_min:
 12:         raise HTTPException(429, "Rate limit exceeded")
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 20: async def get_model(db: Database, model_name: str, version: str = "v1") -> Optional[ModelRecord]:

 24:     )

 64:         "SELECT * FROM models WHERE status != 'deleted' ORDER BY name, version"
 65:     )
 66:     return [
 67:         ModelRecord(
 68:             id=str(r["id"]),
 69:             name=r["name"],
 70:             version=r["version"],
 71:             worker_name=r["worker_name"],
 72:             protocol=r["protocol"],
 73:             endpoint=r["endpoint"],
 74:             status=r["status"],
 75:             supports_streaming=r["supports_streaming"],
 76:             canary_percent=r["canary_percent"],
 77:             description=r["description"],
 78:         )
 79:         for r in rows
 80:     ]
 81: 
 82: 
 83: async def list_versions(db: Database, model_name: str) -> list[ModelRecord]:
 84:     """All active versions of a model, ordered by version string.
 85:     Used by canary routing to find the 'stable' (lowest) and 'canary' (highest) versions."""
 86:     rows = await db.fetch_all(
 87:         "SELECT * FROM models WHERE name = :name AND status = 'active' ORDER BY version",
 88:         {"name": model_name},
 89:     )
 90:     return [
 91:         ModelRecord(
 92:             id=str(r["id"]),
 93:             name=r["name"],
 94:             version=r["version"],
 95:             worker_name=r["worker_name"],
 96:             protocol=r["protocol"],
 97:             endpoint=r["endpoint"],
 98:             status=r["status"],
 99:             supports_streaming=r["supports_streaming"],
100:             canary_percent=r["canary_percent"],
101:             description=r["description"],
102:         )
103:         for r in rows
104:     ]
105: 
106: 
107: async def register_model(
108:     db: Database,
109:     name: str,
110:     version: str,
111:     worker_name: str,
112:     protocol: str,
113:     endpoint: str,
114:     supports_streaming: bool = False,
115:     canary_percent: int = 0,
116:     description: Optional[str] = None,
117: ) -> str:
118:     row = await db.fetch_one(
119:         """INSERT INTO models (name, version, worker_name, protocol, endpoint, supports_streaming, canary_percent, description)
120:            VALUES (:name, :version, :worker_name, :protocol, :endpoint, :supports_streaming, :canary_percent, :description)
121:            RETURNING id""",
122:         {
123:             "name": name,
124:             "version": version,
125:             "worker_name": worker_name,
126:             "protocol": protocol,
127:             "endpoint": endpoint,
128:             "supports_streaming": supports_streaming,
129:             "canary_percent": canary_percent,
130:             "description": description,
131:         },
132:     )
133:     return str(row["id"])
134: 
135: 
136: async def update_model(db: Database, model_id: str, **fields) -> None:
137:     if not fields:
138:         return
139:     allowed = {"worker_name", "protocol", "endpoint", "status", "supports_streaming", "canary_percent", "description"}
140:     safe_fields = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed and v is not None}
141:     if not safe_fields:
142:         return
143:     set_clause = ", ".join(f"{k} = :{k}" for k in safe_fields)
144:     safe_fields["model_id"] = model_id
145:     await db.execute(
146:         f"UPDATE models SET {set_clause} WHERE id = :model_id",
147:         safe_fields,
148:     )
149: 
150: 
151: async def disable_model(db: Database, model_id: str) -> None:
152:     await db.execute(
153:         "UPDATE models SET status = 'disabled' WHERE id = :id",
154:         {"id": model_id},
155:     )
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[PY]  gateway\schemas.py
================================================================================
  1: from pydantic import BaseModel, EmailStr, Field
  2: from typing import Literal, Optional
  3: 
  4: 
  5: class UserCreate(BaseModel):
  6:     email: EmailStr
  7:     password: str = Field(min_length=8)
  8: 
  9: 
 10: class LoginRequest(BaseModel):
 11:     email: EmailStr
 12:     password: str
 13: 
 14: 
 15: class TokenResponse(BaseModel):
 16:     access_token: str
 17:     token_type: str = "bearer"
 18: 
 19: 
 20: class ApiKeyCreate(BaseModel):
 21:     rate_limit_per_min: int = Field(default=60, ge=1, le=1000)
 22: 
 23: 
 24: class ApiKeyResponse(BaseModel):
 25:     key: str
 26:     rate_limit_per_min: int
 27: 
 28: 
 29: class ModelRegistration(BaseModel):
 30:     name: str = Field(min_length=1)
 31:     version: str = Field(default="v1", pattern=r"^v\d+$")
 32:     worker_name: str
 33:     protocol: Literal["http", "grpc"]
 34:     endpoint: str
 35:     supports_streaming: bool = False
 36:     canary_percent: int = Field(default=0, ge=0, le=100)
 37:     description: Optional[str] = None
 38: 
 39: 
 40: class ModelUpdate(BaseModel):
 41:     worker_name: Optional[str] = None
 42:     protocol: Optional[Literal["http", "grpc"]] = None
 43:     endpoint: Optional[str] = None
 44:     status: Optional[Literal["active", "disabled"]] = None
 45:     supports_streaming: Optional[bool] = None
 46:     canary_percent: Optional[int] = Field(default=None, ge=0, le=100)
 47:     description: Optional[str] = None

[YAML]  k8s\postgres\init-configmap.yaml
================================================================================
  1: apiVersion: v1
  2: data:
  3:   init.sql: |-
  4:     -- Enable UUID generation
  5:     CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pgcrypto";
  6: 
  7:     CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
  8:         id            UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  9:         email         TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
 10:         password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
 11:         created_at    TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
 12:     );
 13: 
 14:     CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS api_keys (
 15:         id                 UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
 16:         user_id            UUID REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
 17:         key_hash           TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
 18:         rate_limit_per_min INT DEFAULT 60,
 19:         is_admin           BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
 20:         created_at         TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
 21:         revoked            BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE
 22:     );
 23: 
 24:     CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS models (
 25:       id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
 26:       name TEXT NOT NULL,
 27:       version TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'v1',
 28:       worker_name TEXT NOT NULL,
 29:       protocol TEXT NOT NULL,
 30:       endpoint TEXT NOT NULL,
 31:       status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
 32:       supports_streaming BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
 33:       canary_percent INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
 34:       description TEXT,
 35:       created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
 36:       CONSTRAINT models_protocol_chk CHECK (protocol IN ('http','grpc')),
 37:       CONSTRAINT models_status_chk CHECK (status IN ('active','disabled','deleted')),
 38:       CONSTRAINT models_canary_chk CHECK (canary_percent BETWEEN 0 AND 100),
 39:       UNIQUE(name, version)
 40:     );
 41: 
 42:     CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS requests (
 43:         id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
 44:         api_key_id  UUID REFERENCES api_keys(id),
 45:         model_id    UUID REFERENCES models(id),
 46:         model_type  TEXT NOT NULL,
 47:         status      TEXT NOT NULL,            -- 'success' | 'error' | 'rate_limited'
 48:         latency_ms  INT,
 49:         created_at  TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
 50:     );
 51: 
 52:     -- Seed the two initial models
 53:     INSERT INTO models (name, version, worker_name, protocol, endpoint, supports_streaming, description)
 54:     VALUES
 55:     ('doc-ocr', 'v1', 'doc-ocr-worker', 'http', 'http://doc-ocr-worker:8001/infer', FALSE, 'EasyOCR document text extraction'),
 56:     ('asr', 'v1', 'asr-worker', 'http', 'http://asr-worker:8002/infer', TRUE, 'Whisper-tiny speech-to-text')
 57:     ON CONFLICT (name, version) DO NOTHING;
 58: kind: ConfigMap
 59: metadata:
 60:   name: postgres-init-sql
 61:   namespace: modelmesh
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[SQL]  migrations\003_add_supports_streaming.sql
================================================================================
  1: ALTER TABLE models
  2: ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS supports_streaming BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE;
  3: 
  4: UPDATE models
  5: SET supports_streaming = TRUE
  6: WHERE name = 'asr'
  7: AND version = 'v1';
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[SQL]  migrations\004_canary_percent_and_constraints.sql
================================================================================
  1: -- Idempotent: safe on a DB where canary_percent was added by hand.
  2: ALTER TABLE models ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS canary_percent INT;
  3: UPDATE models SET canary_percent = 0 WHERE canary_percent IS NULL;
  4: ALTER TABLE models ALTER COLUMN canary_percent SET DEFAULT 0;
  5: ALTER TABLE models ALTER COLUMN canary_percent SET NOT NULL;
  6: 
  7: ALTER TABLE models DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS models_protocol_chk;
  8: ALTER TABLE models ADD CONSTRAINT models_protocol_chk CHECK (protocol IN ('http','grpc'));
  9: ALTER TABLE models DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS models_status_chk;
 10: ALTER TABLE models ADD CONSTRAINT models_status_chk CHECK (status IN ('active','disabled','deleted'));
 11: ALTER TABLE models DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS models_canary_chk;
 12: ALTER TABLE models ADD CONSTRAINT models_canary_chk CHECK (canary_percent BETWEEN 0 AND 100);
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[SH]  scripts\smoke_test.sh
================================================================================
  1: #!/usr/bin/env bash
  2: 
  3: set -uo pipefail
  4: 
  5: BASE="${BASE:-http://localhost:8000}"
  6: LATENCY_N=0
  7: 
  8: if [[ "${1:-}" == "--latency" ]]; then
  9:     LATENCY_N="${2:-}"
 10:     if [[ ! "$LATENCY_N" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || [[ "$LATENCY_N" -lt 1 ]]; then
 11:         echo "CHECK latency: FAIL invalid N" >&2
 12:         exit 2
 13:     fi
 14: fi
 15: 
 16: failures=0
 17: API_KEY=""
 18: JOB_PIDS=()
 19: TMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)
 20: trap 'rm -rf "$TMP_DIR"' EXIT
 21: 
 22: pass_check() {
 23:     echo "CHECK $1: PASS $2"
 24: }
 25: 
 26: fail_check() {
 27:     echo "CHECK $1: FAIL $2"
 28:     failures=$((failures + 1))
 29: }
 30: 
 31: info_check() {
 32:     echo "CHECK $1: INFO $2"
 33: }
 34: 
 35: find_python() {
 36:     local candidates=()
 37:     if [[ -n "${MODEL_MESH_PYTHON:-}" ]]; then
 38:         candidates+=("$MODEL_MESH_PYTHON")
 39:     fi
 40:     if [[ -n "${VIRTUAL_ENV:-}" ]]; then
 41:         candidates+=("$VIRTUAL_ENV/bin/python")
 42:     fi
 43:     candidates+=(
 44:         "$HOME/.venvs/modelmesh/bin/python"
 45:         "$PWD/.venv/bin/python"
 46:         "$PWD/modelmesh-env/bin/python"
 47:     )
 48:     if command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
 49:         candidates+=("$(command -v python3)")
 50:     fi
 51:     local candidate
 52:     for candidate in "${candidates[@]}"; do
 53:         if [[ -x "$candidate" ]] && "$candidate" -c 'import websockets' >/dev/null 2>&1; then
 54:             printf '%s' "$candidate"
 55:             return 0
 56:         fi
 57:     done
 58:     return 1
 59: }
 60: 
 61: PYTHON_BIN=$(find_python || true)
 62: 
 63: health_code=$(curl -sS -o "$TMP_DIR/health.json" -w '%{http_code}' "$BASE/health" 2>/dev/null || true)
 64: ready_code=$(curl -sS -o "$TMP_DIR/ready.json" -w '%{http_code}' "$BASE/ready" 2>/dev/null || true)
 65: if [[ "$health_code" == "200" && "$ready_code" == "200" ]]; then
 66:     pass_check health "health=200 ready=200"
 67: else
 68:     fail_check health "health=$health_code ready=$ready_code"
 69: fi
 70: 
 71: models_json=$(curl -sS "$BASE/v1/models" 2>/dev/null || true)
 72: if printf '%s' "$models_json" | jq -e 'type == "array" and any(.[]; .name == "doc-ocr") and any(.[]; .name == "asr")' >/dev/null 2>&1; then
 73:     pass_check models "HTTP 200 with doc-ocr and asr"
 74: else
 75:     fail_check models "missing models or HTTP failure"
 76: fi
 77: 
 78: register_payload='{"email":"baseline@example.com","password":"baseline-pass-1"}'
 79: register_code=$(curl -sS -o "$TMP_DIR/register.json" -w '%{http_code}' \
 80:     -X POST "$BASE/v1/auth/register" \
 81:     -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d "$register_payload" 2>/dev/null || true)
 82: login_json=$(curl -sS -X POST "$BASE/v1/auth/login" \
 83:     -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d "$register_payload" 2>/dev/null || true)
 84: JWT=$(printf '%s' "$login_json" | jq -r '.access_token // empty')
 85: 
 86: if [[ -n "$JWT" ]]; then
 87:     key_json=$(curl -sS -X POST "$BASE/v1/auth/api-keys" \
 88:         -H "Authorization: Bearer $JWT" \
 89:         -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
 90:         -d '{"rate_limit_per_min":100}' 2>/dev/null || true)
 91:     API_KEY=$(printf '%s' "$key_json" | jq -r '.key // empty')
 92: fi
 93: 
 94: if [[ -n "$API_KEY" ]]; then
 95:     pass_check auth "registration=$register_code login=200 key=issued"
 96: else
 97:     fail_check auth "registration=$register_code login=$(printf '%s' "$login_json" | jq -r '.detail // "no access_token"')"
 98: fi
 99: 
100: poll_job() {
101:     local job_id="$1"
102:     local headers="$2"
103:     local result=""
104:     local i
105:     for i in $(seq 1 30); do
106:         result=$(curl -sS "$BASE/v1/jobs/$job_id" -H "Authorization: Bearer $headers" 2>/dev/null || true)
107:         local status
108:         status=$(printf '%s' "$result" | jq -r '.status // empty')
109:         if [[ "$status" == "success" || "$status" == "failed" ]]; then
110:             printf '%s' "$result"
111:             return 0
112:         fi
113:         sleep 2
114:     done
115:     printf '%s' "$result"
116: }
117: 
118: ocr_start=$(date +%s)
119: ocr_job=$(curl -sS -X POST "$BASE/v1/infer/doc-ocr" \
120:     -H "Authorization: Bearer $API_KEY" \
121:     -F 'file=@fixtures/sample_document.jpg' 2>/dev/null | jq -r '.job_id // empty')
122: ocr_final=""
123: if [[ -n "$ocr_job" ]]; then
124:     ocr_final=$(poll_job "$ocr_job" "$API_KEY")
125: fi
126: ocr_elapsed=$(( $(date +%s) - ocr_start ))
127: ocr_status=$(printf '%s' "$ocr_final" | jq -r '.status // empty')
128: ocr_text=$(printf '%s' "$ocr_final" | jq -r '.result.text // ""')
129: if [[ "$ocr_status" == "success" ]] && [[ "$ocr_text" == *"HELLO"* ]]; then
130:     pass_check doc-ocr-job "status=success seconds=$ocr_elapsed text_contains_HELLO=yes"
131: else
132:     fail_check doc-ocr-job "status=$ocr_status seconds=$ocr_elapsed"
133: fi
134: 
135: asr_start=$(date +%s)
136: asr_job=$(curl -sS -X POST "$BASE/v1/infer/asr" \
137:     -H "Authorization: Bearer $API_KEY" \
138:     -F 'file=@fixtures/sample_audio.wav' 2>/dev/null | jq -r '.job_id // empty')
139: asr_final=""
140: if [[ -n "$asr_job" ]]; then
141:     asr_final=$(poll_job "$asr_job" "$API_KEY")
142: fi
143: asr_elapsed=$(( $(date +%s) - asr_start ))
144: asr_status=$(printf '%s' "$asr_final" | jq -r '.status // empty')
145: if [[ "$asr_status" == "success" ]]; then
146:     pass_check asr-job "status=success seconds=$asr_elapsed"
147: else
148:     fail_check asr-job "status=$asr_status seconds=$asr_elapsed"
149: fi
150: 
151: if [[ -n "$PYTHON_BIN" ]]; then
152:     "$PYTHON_BIN" test_ws_client.py "$API_KEY" asr fixtures/sample_audio.wav >"$TMP_DIR/ws-asr.raw" 2>&1
153:     ws_asr_rc=$?
154:     sed -E "s/$API_KEY/[REDACTED]/g" "$TMP_DIR/ws-asr.raw" >"$TMP_DIR/ws-asr.out"
155:     if [[ $ws_asr_rc -eq 0 ]] && grep -q '^FINAL_RESULT=yes$' "$TMP_DIR/ws-asr.out"; then
156:         pass_check ws-asr "exit=0 final_result=yes"
157:     else
158:         fail_check ws-asr "exit=$ws_asr_rc output=$(grep -v 'api_key=' "$TMP_DIR/ws-asr.out" | tr '\n' ' ' | cut -c1-160)"
159:     fi
160: 
161:     "$PYTHON_BIN" test_ws_client.py "$API_KEY" doc-ocr fixtures/sample_document.jpg >"$TMP_DIR/ws-doc.raw" 2>&1
162:     ws_doc_rc=$?
163:     sed -E "s/$API_KEY/[REDACTED]/g" "$TMP_DIR/ws-doc.raw" >"$TMP_DIR/ws-doc.out"
164:     if [[ $ws_doc_rc -eq 0 ]] && grep -q '^FINAL_RESULT=yes$' "$TMP_DIR/ws-doc.out"; then
165:         pass_check ws-doc-ocr "exit=0 final_result=yes"
166:     else
167:         fail_check ws-doc-ocr "exit=$ws_doc_rc output=$(grep -v 'api_key=' "$TMP_DIR/ws-doc.out" | tr '\n' ' ' | cut -c1-160)"
168:     fi
169: else
170:     fail_check ws-asr "WebSocket Python environment unavailable"
171:     fail_check ws-doc-ocr "WebSocket Python environment unavailable"
172: fi
173: 
174: limit_key_json=$(curl -sS -X POST "$BASE/v1/auth/api-keys" \
175:     -H "Authorization: Bearer $JWT" \
176:     -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
177:     -d '{"rate_limit_per_min":2}' 2>/dev/null || true)
178: LIMIT_KEY=$(printf '%s' "$limit_key_json" | jq -r '.key // empty')
179: rate_codes=""
180: if [[ -n "$LIMIT_KEY" ]]; then
181:     for i in 1 2 3; do
182:         code=$(curl -sS -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' -X POST "$BASE/v1/infer/doc-ocr" \
183:             -H "Authorization: Bearer $LIMIT_KEY" \
184:             -F 'file=@fixtures/sample_document.jpg' 2>/dev/null || true)
185:         rate_codes="${rate_codes}${rate_codes:+,}${code}"
186:     done
187: fi
188: if [[ "$rate_codes" == "200,200,429" ]]; then
189:     pass_check rate-limit "codes=$rate_codes"
190: else
191:     fail_check rate-limit "codes=${rate_codes:-none}"
192: fi
193: 
194: if [[ -n "$API_KEY" ]]; then
195:     model_id=$(printf '%s' "$models_json" | jq -r '.[] | select(.name=="doc-ocr") | .id' | head -1)
196:     docker compose exec -T postgres psql -U user -d modelmesh -c \
197:         "UPDATE api_keys SET is_admin = TRUE WHERE key_hash = encode(digest('$API_KEY','sha256'),'hex');" \
198:         >/dev/null 2>&1
199:     patch1=$(curl -sS -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' -X PATCH "$BASE/v1/models/$model_id" \
200:         -H "Authorization: Bearer $API_KEY" \
201:         -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
202:         -d '{"description":"baseline-check"}' 2>/dev/null || true)
203:     patch2=$(curl -sS -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' -X PATCH "$BASE/v1/models/$model_id" \
204:         -H "Authorization: Bearer $API_KEY" \
205:         -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
206:         -d '{"description":"EasyOCR document text extraction"}' 2>/dev/null || true)
207:     if [[ "$patch1" == "200" && "$patch2" == "200" ]]; then
208:         pass_check admin-patch "change=200 restore=200"
209:     else
210:         fail_check admin-patch "change=$patch1 restore=$patch2"
211:     fi
212: else
213:     fail_check admin-patch "API key unavailable"
214: fi
215: 
216: printf 'not an image\n' >"$TMP_DIR/bad.txt"
217: bad_start=$(date +%s)
218: bad_job=$(curl -sS -X POST "$BASE/v1/infer/doc-ocr" \
219:     -H "Authorization: Bearer $API_KEY" \
220:     -H 'Accept: application/json' \
221:     -F 'file=@'"$TMP_DIR"'/bad.txt;type=image/jpeg' 2>/dev/null | jq -r '.job_id // empty')
222: bad_final=""
223: if [[ -n "$bad_job" ]]; then
224:     for i in $(seq 1 120); do
225:         bad_final=$(curl -sS "$BASE/v1/jobs/$bad_job" -H "Authorization: Bearer $API_KEY" 2>/dev/null || true)
226:         if [[ $(printf '%s' "$bad_final" | jq -r '.status // empty') == "failed" ]]; then
227:             break
228:         fi
229:         sleep 1
230:     done
231: fi
232: bad_elapsed=$(( $(date +%s) - bad_start ))
233: bad_error=$(printf '%s' "$bad_final" | jq -r '.error // .detail // "unknown"')
234: info_check bad-upload "seconds=$bad_elapsed error=$bad_error"
235: 
236: if [[ "$LATENCY_N" -gt 0 ]]; then
237:     for model in doc-ocr asr; do
238:         fixture="fixtures/sample_document.jpg"
239:         [[ "$model" == "asr" ]] && fixture="fixtures/sample_audio.wav"
240:         times_file="$TMP_DIR/${model}.times"
241:         : >"$times_file"
242:         for i in $(seq 1 "$LATENCY_N"); do
243:             start_ns=$(date +%s%N)
244:             job_id=$(curl -sS -X POST "$BASE/v1/infer/$model" \
245:                 -H "Authorization: Bearer $API_KEY" \
246:                 -F "file=@$fixture" 2>/dev/null | jq -r '.job_id // empty')
247:             if [[ -n "$job_id" ]]; then
248:                 final=$(poll_job "$job_id" "$API_KEY")
249:             fi
250:             end_ns=$(date +%s%N)
251:             elapsed_ms=$(( (end_ns - start_ns) / 1000000 ))
252:             awk -v ms="$elapsed_ms" 'BEGIN { printf "%.3f\n", ms / 1000 }' >>"$times_file"
253:         done
254:         sorted=$(sort -n "$times_file")
255:         count=$(printf '%s\n' "$sorted" | wc -l)
256:         median_idx=$(( (count + 1) / 2 ))
257:         p95_idx=$(( (95 * count + 99) / 100 ))
258:         median=$(printf '%s\n' "$sorted" | sed -n "${median_idx}p")
259:         p95=$(printf '%s\n' "$sorted" | sed -n "${p95_idx}p")
260:         echo "LATENCY $model: median=${median}s p95=${p95}s"
261:     done
262: fi
263: 
264: if [[ $failures -eq 0 ]]; then
265:     echo "SMOKE: PASS"
266:     exit 0
267: else
268:     echo "SMOKE: FAIL ($failures checks failed)"
269:     exit 1
270: fi

 55:             color: #000;
 56:             font-weight: bold;
 57:         }

 59:             color: #d00;
 60:         }
 61:         .status {
 62:             color: #070;
 63:         }
 64:     </style>
 65: </head>
 66: <body>
 67:     <h1>ModelMesh WebSocket Test Client</h1>
 68: 
 69:     <div class="form-group">
 70:         <label for="apiKey">API Key:</label>
 71:         <input type="text" id="apiKey" placeholder="Enter your API key">
 72:     </div>
 73: 
 74:     <div class="form-group">
 75:         <label for="modelName">Model Name:</label>
 76:         <select id="modelName">
 77:             <option value="asr">asr</option>
 78:             <option value="doc-ocr">doc-ocr</option>
 79:         </select>
 80:     </div>
 81: 
 82:     <div class="form-group">
 83:         <label for="fileInput">Select File:</label>
 84:         <input type="file" id="fileInput" accept="audio/*,image/*">
 85:     </div>
 86: 
 87:     <button id="connectBtn" onclick="connect()">Connect & Send</button>
 88: 
 89:     <h2>Results:</h2>
 90:     <div id="results"></div>
 91: 
 92:     <script>
 93:         let ws = null;
 94:         const resultsDiv = document.getElementById('results');
 95:         const connectBtn = document.getElementById('connectBtn');
 96: 
 97:         function log(message, className = '') {
 98:             const span = document.createElement('span');
 99:             span.className = className;
100:             span.textContent = message + '\n';
101:             resultsDiv.appendChild(span);
102:             resultsDiv.scrollTop = resultsDiv.scrollHeight;
103:         }
104: 
105:         function clearResults() {
106:             resultsDiv.innerHTML = '';
107:         }
108: 
109:         async function connect() {
110:             const apiKey = document.getElementById('apiKey').value.trim();
111:             const modelName = document.getElementById('modelName').value;
112:             const fileInput = document.getElementById('fileInput');
113: 
114:             if (!apiKey) {
115:                 alert('Please enter an API key');
116:                 return;
117:             }
118: 
119:             if (!fileInput.files.length) {
120:                 alert('Please select a file');
121:                 return;
122:             }
123: 
124:             clearResults();
125:             connectBtn.disabled = true;
126: 
127:             const file = fileInput.files[0];
128:             const uri = `ws://localhost:8000/v1/ws/infer/${modelName}?api_key=${apiKey}&version=v1`;
129: 
130:             log(`Connecting to ${uri}...`, 'status');
131: 
132:             try {
133:                 ws = new WebSocket(uri);
134: 
135:                 ws.onopen = async () => {
136:                     log('Connected!', 'status');
137:                 };
138: 
139:                 ws.onmessage = (event) => {
140:                     const data = JSON.parse(event.data);
141: 
142:                     if (data.error) {
143:                         log(`ERROR: ${data.error}`, 'error');
144:                         ws.close();
145:                         return;
146:                     }
147: 
148:                     if (data.status === 'ready') {
149:                         log(`Ready: ${data.model} (${data.protocol})`, 'status');
150:                         log(`Sending file (${file.size} bytes)...`, 'status');
151: 
152:                         // Read and send file
153:                         const reader = new FileReader();
154:                         reader.onload = (e) => {
155:                             ws.send(e.target.result);
156:                             log('File sent, waiting for results...', 'status');
157:                         };
158:                         reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
159:                         return;
160:                     }
161: 
162:                     if (data.partial !== undefined) {
163:                         log(`[Partial] ${data.partial}`, 'partial');
164:                         if (data.is_final) {
165:                             log('[Final result received]', 'final');
166:                             ws.close();
167:                         }
168:                     } else if (data.result) {
169:                         log(`[Result] ${JSON.stringify(data.result, null, 2)}`, 'final');
170:                         if (data.is_final) {
171:                             ws.close();
172:                         }
173:                     }
174:                 };
175: 
176:                 ws.onerror = (error) => {
177:                     log(`WebSocket error: ${error}`, 'error');
178:                     connectBtn.disabled = false;
179:                 };
180: 
181:                 ws.onclose = () => {
182:                     log('Connection closed', 'status');
183:                     connectBtn.disabled = false;
184:                 };
185: 
186:             } catch (error) {
187:                 log(`Failed to connect: ${error}`, 'error');
188:                 connectBtn.disabled = false;
189:             }
190:         }
191:     </script>
192: </body>
193: </html>

[PY]  test_ws_client.py
================================================================================
  1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2: """
  3: WebSocket test client for streaming inference.
  4: 
  5: Usage:
  6:     python test_ws_client.py <api_key> <model_name> <audio_file.wav>
  7: """
  8: import asyncio
  9: import sys
 10: import websockets
 11: import json
 12: 
 13: 
 14: async def test_streaming(api_key: str, model_name: str, audio_file: str):
 15:     """Test WebSocket streaming inference."""
 16:     uri = f"ws://localhost:8000/v1/ws/infer/{model_name}?api_key={api_key}&version=v1"
 17: 
 18:     print(f"Connecting to {uri}...")
 19: 
 20:     async with websockets.connect(uri) as websocket:
 21:         print("Connected!")
 22: 
 23:         # Wait for ready signal
 24:         ready_msg = await websocket.recv()
 25:         print(f"Ready: {ready_msg}")
 26: 
 27:         # Read and send audio file
 28:         with open(audio_file, "rb") as f:
 29:             audio_data = f.read()
 30: 
 31:         print(f"Sending audio file ({len(audio_data)} bytes)...")
 32:         await websocket.send(audio_data)
 33: 
 34:         # Receive streaming results
 35:         print("\nReceiving results:")
 36:         print("-" * 60)
 37: 
 38:         while True:
 39:             try:
 40:                 message = await asyncio.wait_for(websocket.recv(), timeout=30.0)
 41:                 result = json.loads(message)
 42: 
 43:                 if "error" in result:
 44:                     print(f"ERROR: {result['error']}")
 45:                     return 1
 46: 
 47:                 if "partial" in result:
 48:                     print(f"[Partial] {result['partial']}")
 49:                     if result.get("is_final"):
 50:                         print("\n[Final result received]")
 51:                         break
 52: 
 53:                 elif "result" in result:
 54:                     print(f"[Result] {result['result']}")
 55:                     if result.get("is_final"):
 56:                         break
 57: 
 58:             except asyncio.TimeoutError:
 59:                 print("Timeout waiting for response")
 60:                 return 1
 61: 
 62:         print("-" * 60)
 63:         if not result.get("is_final"):
 64:             print("ERROR: stream ended without a final result")
 65:             return 1
 66:         print("FINAL_RESULT=yes")
 67:         print("Stream complete")
 68:         return 0
 69: 
 70: 
 71: if __name__ == "__main__":
 72:     if len(sys.argv) != 4:
 73:         print("Usage: python test_ws_client.py <api_key> <model_name> <audio_file.wav>")
 74:         print("\nExample:")
 75:         print("  python test_ws_client.py your-api-key-here asr test-audio.wav")
 76:         sys.exit(1)
 77: 
 78:     api_key = sys.argv[1]
 79:     model_name = sys.argv[2]
 80:     audio_file = sys.argv[3]
 81: 
 82:     sys.exit(asyncio.run(test_streaming(api_key, model_name, audio_file)))
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[PY]  tests\conftest.py
================================================================================
  1: """
  2: Test fixtures.
  3: 
  4: Tests run against a real Postgres + Redis (via docker-compose or a local instance).
  5: Set TEST_DATABASE_URL and TEST_REDIS_URL env vars, or rely on the defaults below.
  6: """
  7: 
  8: import asyncio
  9: import os
 10: import secrets
 11: import subprocess
 12: import sys
 13: import tempfile
 14: 
 15: import pytest
 16: import pytest_asyncio
 17: from httpx import AsyncClient, ASGITransport
 18: import redis
 19: 
 20: # ── point at the test DB ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 21: TEST_DATABASE_URL = os.getenv(
 22:     "TEST_DATABASE_URL", "postgresql://user:pass@localhost/modelmesh_test"
 23: )
 24: TEST_REDIS_URL = os.getenv("TEST_REDIS_URL", "redis://localhost:6379/1")
 25: 
 26: # Set configuration before importing the application singletons.
 27: os.environ["DATABASE_URL"] = TEST_DATABASE_URL
 28: os.environ["REDIS_URL"] = TEST_REDIS_URL
 29: os.environ["JWT_SECRET"] = "test-secret"
 30: 
 31: ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))
 32: _PROTO_OUTPUT = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="modelmesh-test-proto-")
 33: subprocess.run(
 34:     [
 35:         sys.executable,
 36:         "-m",
