[PY]  tests\helpers.py
================================================================================
  1: import importlib.util
  2: import os
  3: import sys
  4: 
  5: 
  6: ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))
  7: 
  8: 
  9: def load_module_from_path(unique_name, rel_path, stubs=None, monkeypatch=None):
 10:     """Load a worker module with temporary dependency stubs."""
 11:     for name, stub in (stubs or {}).items():
 12:         if monkeypatch is not None:
 13:             monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, name, stub)
 14:         else:
 15:             sys.modules[name] = stub
 16: 
 17:     path = os.path.join(ROOT, rel_path)
 18:     spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(unique_name, path)
 19:     if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
 20:         raise ImportError(f"Cannot load module from {path}")
 21:     module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
 22:     spec.loader.exec_module(module)
 23:     return module
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  2: Unit tests for the ASR worker inference function.
  3: These run without Docker or a downloaded Whisper model.

  5: 
  6: import io
  7: import wave
  8: from types import SimpleNamespace

 15: 
 16: 
 17: def _make_silence_wav() -> bytes:
 18:     """Generate a minimal valid WAV file (one second of silence)."""
 19:     buf = io.BytesIO()
 20:     with wave.open(buf, "wb") as wav:
 21:         wav.setnchannels(1)
 22:         wav.setsampwidth(2)
 23:         wav.setframerate(16000)
 24:         wav.writeframes(b"\x00\x00" * 16000)
 25:     return buf.getvalue()
 26: 
 27: 
 28: @pytest.fixture
 29: def asr_module(monkeypatch):
 30:     whisper_stub = MagicMock()
 31:     module = load_module_from_path(
 32:         "asr_inference",
 33:         "workers/asr/inference.py",
 34:         stubs={"whisper": whisper_stub},
 35:         monkeypatch=monkeypatch,
 36:     )
 37:     return module, whisper_stub
 38:

 41:     module, whisper_stub = asr_module

 43:     mock_model.transcribe.return_value = {"text": " hello world"}
 44:     whisper_stub.load_model.return_value = mock_model

 46:     assert module.transcribe(_make_silence_wav()) == {"text": " hello world"}

109: async def test_rate_limit_enforced(client, db, regular_user):
110:     """A key with limit=2 should 429 on the third request."""
111:     import auth as _auth
112: 
113:     raw_key = await _auth.create_api_key(db, regular_user["id"], rate_limit_per_min=2)
114:     headers = auth_header(raw_key)
115: 
116:     # Exhaust the limit (requests may 404/503 on the worker, that's fine)
117:     for _ in range(2):
118:         await client.post(
119:             "/v1/infer/doc-ocr",
120:             headers=headers,
121:             files={"file": ("x.jpg", b"\xff\xd8\xff", "image/jpeg")},
122:         )
123: 
124:     # Third call should be rate-limited
125:     resp = await client.post(
126:         "/v1/infer/doc-ocr",
127:         headers=headers,
128:         files={"file": ("x.jpg", b"\xff\xd8\xff", "image/jpeg")},
129:     )
130:     assert resp.status_code == 429
131: 
132:

135:     client, regular_user, rate_limit
136: ):
137:     import auth as _auth
138: 
139:     token = _auth.create_access_token(regular_user["id"])
140:     response = await client.post(
141:         "/v1/auth/api-keys",
142:         headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"},
143:         json={"rate_limit_per_min": rate_limit},
144:     )
145: 
146:     assert response.status_code == 422

[PY]  tests\test_canary_guard.py

  1: import canary_guard
  2: 
  3: import pytest
  4: 
  5: 
  6: @pytest.mark.parametrize(
  7:     ("stable", "canary", "canary_percent", "expected"),
  8:     [
  9:         (canary_guard.Stats(0, 0.0), canary_guard.Stats(0, 0.0), 0, "noop"),
 10:         (canary_guard.Stats(10, 0.0), canary_guard.Stats(10, 0.0), 20, "hold"),
 11:         (canary_guard.Stats(200, 0.01), canary_guard.Stats(50, 0.02), 20, "hold"),
 12:         (canary_guard.Stats(200, 0.01), canary_guard.Stats(50, 0.30), 20, "rollback"),
 13:         (canary_guard.Stats(200, 0.01), canary_guard.Stats(0, 0.0), 20, "rollback"),
 14:         (canary_guard.Stats(200, 0.01), canary_guard.Stats(10, 0.50), 5, "hold"),
 15:         (canary_guard.Stats(200, 0.40), canary_guard.Stats(50, 0.45), 20, "hold"),
 16:         (canary_guard.Stats(200, 0.10), canary_guard.Stats(50, 0.20), 20, "hold"),
 17:     ],
 18: )
 19: def test_decide_cases(stable, canary, canary_percent, expected):
 20:     decision, _ = canary_guard.decide(
 21:         stable, canary, canary_percent, canary_guard.Config()
 22:     )
 23: 
 24:     assert decision == expected
 25: 
 26: 
 27: def test_selects_highest_and_lowest_numeric_versions_and_ignores_middle():
 28:     versions = [
 29:         {"id": "v9", "version": "v9", "status": "active", "canary_percent": 0},
 30:         {"id": "v10", "version": "v10", "status": "active", "canary_percent": 50},
 31:         {"id": "v11", "version": "v11", "status": "active", "canary_percent": 20},
 32:         {"id": "disabled", "version": "v12", "status": "disabled", "canary_percent": 0},
 33:     ]
 34: 
 35:     stable, canary = canary_guard.select_stable_and_canary(versions)
 36: 
 37:     assert stable["id"] == "v9"
 38:     assert canary["id"] == "v11"
 39: 
 40: 
 41: def test_version_number_selection_orders_v10_after_v9():
 42:     versions = [
 43:         {"id": "v10", "version": "v10", "status": "active", "canary_percent": 20},
 44:         {"id": "v9", "version": "v9", "status": "active", "canary_percent": 0},
 45:     ]
 46: 
 47:     stable, canary = canary_guard.select_stable_and_canary(versions)
 48: 
 49:     assert stable["id"] == "v9"
 50:     assert canary["id"] == "v10"
 51: 
 52: 
 53: def test_zero_percent_canary_pair_remains_reportable_as_noop():
 54:     versions = [
 55:         {"id": "v1", "version": "v1", "status": "active", "canary_percent": 0},
 56:         {"id": "v2", "version": "v2", "status": "active", "canary_percent": 0},
 57:     ]
 58: 
 59:     stable, canary = canary_guard.select_stable_and_canary(versions)
 60: 
 61:     assert canary_guard.decide(
 62:         canary_guard.Stats(0, 0.0),
 63:         canary_guard.Stats(0, 0.0),
 64:         canary["canary_percent"],
 65:         canary_guard.Config(),
 66:     ) == ("noop", "canary already at 0")
 67:     assert stable["id"] == "v1"
 68:     assert canary["id"] == "v2"

148: def _job_fields(module, job_id="job-metrics", retry_count="0"):
149:     return {
150:         b"job_id": job_id.encode(),
151:         b"worker_name": module.WORKER_NAME.encode(),
152:         b"model_id": b"model-1",
153:         b"model_name": _model_name(module).encode(),
154:         b"model_version": b"v1",
155:         b"retry_count": retry_count.encode(),
156:         b"payload": b"test-payload",
157:     }

160: def _model_name(module):
161:     return "doc-ocr" if module.WORKER_NAME == "doc-ocr-worker" else "asr"
162: 
163: 
164: def _inference_function(inference):
165:     if hasattr(inference, "extract_text"):
166:         return inference.extract_text
167:     return inference.transcribe

[PY]  tests\test_grpc_transport.py
================================================================================
  1: import asyncio
  2: import io
  3: import json
  4: import wave
  5: from concurrent import futures
  6: 
  7: import grpc
  8: import pytest
  9: 
 10: from grpc_client import MAX_MSG, call_worker_grpc
 11: import inference_pb2
 12: import inference_pb2_grpc
 13: 
 14: 
 15: @pytest.mark.asyncio(scope="session")
 16: async def test_six_mib_wav_payload_traverses_grpc_transport():
 17:     target_size = 6 * 1024 * 1024
 18:     wav_data_size = target_size - 44
 19:     assert wav_data_size % 2 == 0
 20:     buffer = io.BytesIO()
 21:     with wave.open(buffer, "wb") as wav:
 22:         wav.setnchannels(1)
 23:         wav.setsampwidth(2)
 24:         wav.setframerate(16000)
 25:         wav.writeframes(b"\x00" * wav_data_size)
 26:     payload = buffer.getvalue()
 27:     assert len(payload) == target_size
 28:     assert payload[:4] == b"RIFF" and payload[8:12] == b"WAVE"
 29:     assert len(payload) < MAX_MSG
 30: 
 31:     class PayloadSizeServicer(inference_pb2_grpc.InferenceWorkerServicer):
 32:         def Predict(self, request, context):
 33:             return inference_pb2.InferenceResponse(
 34:                 result_json=json.dumps({"payload_bytes": len(request.payload)}),
 35:                 latency_ms=0,
 36:             )
 37: 
 38:     executor = futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1)
 39:     server = grpc.server(
 40:         executor,
 41:         options=[
 42:             ("grpc.max_send_message_length", MAX_MSG),
 43:             ("grpc.max_receive_message_length", MAX_MSG),
 44:         ],
 45:     )
 46:     inference_pb2_grpc.add_InferenceWorkerServicer_to_server(
 47:         PayloadSizeServicer(), server
 48:     )
 49:     port = server.add_insecure_port("127.0.0.1:0")
 50:     server.start()
 51:     try:
 52:         result = await call_worker_grpc(
 53:             f"127.0.0.1:{port}", "asr", payload
 54:         )
 55:     finally:
 56:         await asyncio.to_thread(lambda: server.stop(0).wait())
 57:         executor.shutdown(wait=True)
 58: 
 59:     assert result == {"payload_bytes": target_size}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 32:     metrics_module, monkeypatch, caplog
 33: ):
 34:     module, clickhouse_connect = metrics_module
 35:     monkeypatch.setenv("CLICKHOUSE_HOST", "metrics-db")

 41:         _log_one_metric(module)

 48: 
 49:         now[0] = 160.0
 50:         _log_one_metric(module)
 51: 
 52:     assert clickhouse_connect.get_client.call_count == 2
 53:     assert len(caplog.records) == 2
 54:     assert all(
 55:         "metric insert failed (skipping for 60s)" in record.message
 56:         for record in caplog.records
 57:     )

 14: 
 15:     async def __aenter__(self):
 16:         return self
 17: 
 18:     async def __aexit__(self, exc_type, exc, tb):
 19:         return False
 20: 
 21:     async def send(self, data):
 22:         return None
 23: 
 24:     async def recv(self):
 25:         message = self.messages.pop(0)
 26:         if isinstance(message, BaseException):
 27:             raise message
 28:         return message
 29: 
 30: 
 31: @pytest.mark.parametrize(
 32:     ("messages", "expected_status"),
 33:     [
 34:         (['{"status":"ready"}', '{"error":"worker failed"}'], 1),
 35:         (['{"status":"ready"}', asyncio.TimeoutError()], 1),
 36:         (
 37:             [
 38:                 '{"status":"ready"}',
 39:                 json.dumps({"result": {"text": "ok"}, "is_final": True}),
 40:             ],
 41:             0,
 42:         ),
 43:     ],
 44: )
 45: @pytest.mark.asyncio(scope="session")
 46: async def test_websocket_client_exit_status_matches_final_result(
 47:     tmp_path, monkeypatch, capsys, messages, expected_status
 48: ):
 49:     audio_file = tmp_path / "sample.wav"
 50:     audio_file.write_bytes(b"sample")

 52:     websockets_stub = SimpleNamespace(
 53:         connect=MagicMock(return_value=connection)
 54:     )
 55:     module = load_module_from_path(
 56:         "ws_client_under_test",
 57:         "test_ws_client.py",
 58:         stubs={"websockets": websockets_stub},
 59:         monkeypatch=monkeypatch,
 60:     )
 61: 
 62:     status = await module.test_streaming("key", "asr", str(audio_file))
 63: 
 64:     assert status == expected_status
 65:     output = capsys.readouterr().out
 66:     assert ("FINAL_RESULT=yes" in output) == (expected_status == 0)

[MD]  verification\00_environment.md

  1: # Verification Environment
  2: 
  3: This file records observed environment facts. It is not an implementation report.
  4: 
  5: ## Host
  6: 
  7: - OS: Microsoft Windows 11 Home Single Language
  8: - Windows build: 26300
  9: - Host RAM: 16,025 MB reported by `systeminfo`
 10: - CPU: 13th Gen Intel(R) Core(TM) i5-1340P
 11: - CPU topology: 12 physical cores, 16 logical processors
 12: - Base clock reported by Windows: 1.9 GHz
 13: - GPU: Intel Iris Xe Graphics, 128 MiB reported by Windows adapter metadata
 14: 
 15: ## Runtime
 16: 
 17: - Docker CLI: Docker 29.9.2
 18: - Docker Compose: v5.6.1
 19: - Docker daemon: NOT RUN at initial capture (`npipe:////./pipe/dockerDesktopLinuxEngine` unavailable); Docker Desktop was started, and Docker engine 29.9.2 then became reachable.
 20: - Windows Python: 3.12.10
 21: - WSL2 Python: 3.12.3
 22: - WSL2 Docker client: Docker CLI not found in the initial WSL environment
 23: - Docker Desktop integration: disabled/unavailable in WSL2 at initial capture
 24: 
 25: ## Services
 26: 
 27: - PostgreSQL: 16.16, `postgres (PostgreSQL) 16.16 (Debian 16.16-1.pgdg13+1)`
 28: - Redis: 7.4.12
 29: 
 30: ## Container limits
 31: 
 32: - Compose container CPU/memory limits: no CPU or memory limits are set (`NanoCpus=0`, `Memory=0`) for gateway, both workers, PostgreSQL, and Redis.
 33: - Kubernetes: NOT VERIFIED during this environment pass.
 34: 
 35: ## Other heavy processes
 36: 
 37: - Initial process scan was inconclusive for package-level resource use because several Windows processes reported 0 MiB in that snapshot.
 38: - Docker Desktop was stopped at the initial capture.
 39: - No independent benchmark process was identified before test setup.
 40: 
 41: ## Repository
 42: 
 43: - Commit: `d6fd970b76ed5e0a747be709e0f62e7a009d31da`
 44: - Dirty: yes
 45: - Status: application/source/docs and verification work coexist in the dirty worktree; verification output will remain under `verification/`.
 46: 
 47: ## Benchmark runtime confirmation
 48: 
 49: - Docker server: 29.9.2; daemon reported 16 CPUs and 8,139,243,520 bytes of memory (about 7.58 GiB).
 50: - Compose services: PostgreSQL 16.16 and Redis 7.4.12; no per-container CPU or memory limits were configured.
 51: - The stack was running during the benchmark and `/ready` returned HTTP 200 after cleanup.
 52: - A run-05 OCR-worker sample recorded 791.78% CPU and 2.682 GiB memory use against the Docker daemon's 7.58 GiB limit. This is a point-in-time sample, not a run-wide average.
 53: - Other host workloads were not continuously profiled during the multi-hour runs (NOT VERIFIED).

[MD]  verification\EVIDENCE.md
================================================================================
  1: # Resume evidence audit
  2: 
  3: Evidence snapshot: commit `d6fd970b76ed5e0a747be709e0f62e7a009d31da`; the worktree was dirty during the runs and remains dirty. The verification runs use Windows 11 with Docker Desktop, a local Docker Compose stack, an Intel Core i5-1340P (12 physical / 16 logical cores), and 16,025 MB host RAM. Docker limits and run-specific conditions are noted below and in [00_environment.md](./00_environment.md).
  4: 
  5: This report reviews the four numeric/reliability claims that can be recovered from the resume-related requests: the OCR and ASR gRPC speed claims, the 2 ms / 4 ms enqueue claim, and the queue delivery claim. Repository documentation is treated only as the source of a claim, never as proof. Numeric summaries below are based on the raw run artifacts, not documentation-only numbers.
  6: 
  7: The verification tree contained 1,877 files (112,186,341 bytes), including a bundled Python virtual environment and binary fixtures. All 62 Markdown files were reviewed. JSON, JSONL, and CSV files were parsed (362 JSON, 113 JSONL, 67 CSV); no parsing errors were found. Logs were scanned for error, warning, rate-limit, and OOM indicators, and binary files were inventoried by hash. The bundled environment files are tooling, not benchmark evidence. This review added only this file under `verification/`; pre-existing application, documentation, test, and configuration changes remain untouched. `verification/` is ignored by the current `.gitignore`, whose change predates this report and has unknown origin.
  8: 
  9: For replacement measurements, "reproducible" means the procedure and raw observations are saved and can be re-analyzed on the recorded setup; it does not mean a new run will return identical timing values. Historical documentation-only values are not reproducible from saved raw evidence. A confidence interval is marked N/A where no interval was calculated or where the result is a finite test count rather than a population estimate.
 10:

For exact finite test counts, `N/A` means no population-rate or between-arm confidence interval was calculated.

120: 
121: ### Baseline tests
122: 
123: The requested first three consecutive test runs each reported 102 passed and 8 warnings. Pytest-reported durations were 5.92 s, 5.36 s, and 4.37 s (n=3 runs; median 5.36 s, p95 5.92 s, spread 1.55 s). Measured wall durations were 9,781 ms, 9,116 ms, and 7,131 ms (n=3; median 9,116 ms, p95 9,781 ms, spread 2,650 ms). `pytest --collect-only -q` collected 102 tests across 11 files. Three additional complete-metadata runs (runs 10-12) also each reported 102 passed and 8 warnings; pytest duration median 5.65 s, p95 6.01 s, spread 0.56 s. The commands and raw outputs are retained, though some earlier run wrappers did not save end timestamps. These are local test-suite results, not production availability evidence. See [run 01](./runs/01_baseline_tests/README.md).
124: 
125: ## 3. Failures, skips, and anomalies
126: 
127: - The historical gRPC and Artillery figures have no saved raw request evidence in `verification/`; the docs that repeat them are claims only. The old Artillery rejection fraction supplied in the request is not independently verified.
128: - In run 03, the page-like OCR comparison failed heavily: only 134/500 HTTP and 15/500 gRPC measured requests succeeded. Only two of five runs had successes in both arms. Five failed page-OCR requests had recorded elapsed values beyond the 90-second receive timeout; the maximum was 17,573,222 ms. A host suspension or clock discontinuity was suspected, not proved.
129: - The run 03 analysis implementation had three preserved failed attempts (IndentationError, KeyError, TypeError). Earlier generated analysis outputs were overwritten during correction and are not recoverable; request-level raw CSVs remain. The corrected analysis completed, but its page-OCR interval is based on only two paired run clusters.
130: - Run 14's retry/DLQ harness is invalid and excluded: its inference stub was not rebound into the imported consumer modules, and reclaim retries were not drained. Its counts and 14-second fallback timings are not evidence for the requested behavior.
131: - The first two queue setup attempts stopped before submitting jobs: one used a reserved `.invalid` email domain; one readiness probe checked stdout but the worker's consumer-group log was on stderr. Neither is counted as a queue trial.
132: - The initial queue trial series used a 135,371-byte image rather than the requested 2,113-byte fixture. It is retained but excluded from the corrected primary totals.
133: - Live invalid-upload setup attempts were rejected first for the reserved `.invalid` email and then for a 10,000/minute API-key limit above the 1,000/minute schema maximum. No inference jobs were submitted in those attempts; the successful run used 1,000/minute and had zero 429s.
134: - Registry pilots 16 and 17 failed during harness execution and are retained but excluded. Pilot 16 stopped at the first PATCH after a stale keep-alive connection. Pilot 17 applied changes but failed to save WebSocket request data due to a missing output directory. Pilot 18 completed only reduced smoke settings and is not a full benchmark.
135: - In run 19, schedule-lag summaries for canary/pinning samples included negative medians despite target-paced scheduling. They do not affect the reported request latencies or version counts, but the schedule-lag metric should not be used as evidence. Run 19 did complete its requested samples; Part A had 100% HTTP 200, and all high latency outliers were retained.
136: - Run 19's Redis DB 1 was empty after cleanup, but process-wide Redis memory was 41,840 bytes above preflight. It was not byte-identical to baseline.
137: - Optional no-op HTTP/gRPC transport-overhead measurements were **NOT RUN**. Host workloads were not continuously profiled during the benchmark runs (**NOT VERIFIED**).
138: - The environment capture initially found the Docker engine unavailable; Docker Desktop was started before the later runs. WSL2 Docker integration was unavailable at the initial capture.
139: - Baseline runs 1-3 and some remediation runs did not record end timestamps; they remain reported with that metadata gap rather than being replaced. The complete-metadata runs 10-12 are supplemental, not substitutes.
140: - Registry pilots 16-17 failed as described above, and pilot 18 was reduced. The successful full run is run 19; post-processing had separate command failures, and exact failed command text was not retained.
141: - The worktree was dirty at the evidence commit and had unrelated pre-existing modifications and deletions. The prior run report also records a `.gitignore` change adding `verification/`, with origin unknown. No unrelated change was reverted or edited for this report.
142: 
143: ## 4. Safe-to-state facts
144: 
145: The five-run WebSocket comparison did not establish a measurable HTTP-versus-gRPC p50 difference for the existing OCR or ASR fixtures; the relevant run-cluster 95% confidence intervals include zero. The page-like OCR comparison was not a valid five-run result because most measured requests failed and only two run clusters had successful paired observations. In seven local Compose SIGKILL trials using the 2,113-byte OCR image, all 420 accepted jobs reached success, while seven job IDs appeared in processing logs more than once; this does not prove exactly-once processing or general loss-free delivery. In the local 100 KiB dummy-worker benchmark, enqueue p50 ranged from 15.35 to 22.09 ms and p95 from 44.90 to 80.87 ms at target rates of 10, 50, and 100 requests per second, with 100% HTTP 200 in all 18 runs. In one 2,000-request sample at each configured canary percentage, observed canary shares were 10.95%, 29.50%, and 49.90%, each statistically consistent with its configured 10%, 30%, or 50% share by the reported chi-square tests. The corrected real-Redis harness observed the configured 2, 4, and 8 second transient retry waits, immediate permanent-error failure into the DLQ, and successful reclaim of 20 ghost-consumer entries for each worker; those tests used stubbed inference and do not establish production delivery guarantees. The current live invalid-upload samples failed with specific errors in 10/10 requests per model, with median observation times of 61.951 ms for OCR and 168.972 ms for ASR in this single local campaign. The static source audit found PostgreSQL registry queries on the REST and WebSocket request paths, canary selection on REST but not WebSocket, and protocol/endpoint routing only in the WebSocket path; it also found Redis consumer groups with 2/4/8-second retry delays, a DLQ stream, and `XAUTOCLAIM` reclaim in both worker consumers. These are source findings recorded in [run 02](./runs/02_static_audit/README.md), not performance or end-to-end guarantees.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[MD]  verification\RULES.md
================================================================================
  1: # Verification Rules
  2: 
  3: 1. Never modify application code, configs, Dockerfiles, or tests. Only add files under `verification/`. If a code change is needed to make a measurement possible, add a separate script under `verification/` and report that limitation instead of changing application code.
  4: 2. Record every run, including failed, aborted, and bad runs. Never rerun solely to obtain a better number without preserving the earlier run.
  5: 3. Save raw data, CSV/JSON, logs, and command output for every run under `verification/runs/<NN>_<name>/raw/`. Each run README lists exact commands, start/end time, git commit hash, whether the worktree is dirty, configuration/environment deviations, and anomalies.
  6: 4. Report `n`, median, p95, and spread for every latency number. Any percentage comparison requires a 95% bootstrap confidence interval and the number of independent runs. If the interval includes zero, state "no measurable difference".
  7: 5. Use a scratch PostgreSQL database and Redis DB 1 for tests. Never flush another Redis database. Clean up verification-created models and keys and restore changed registry values.
  8: 6. If a result cannot be run or checked, report `NOT RUN` or `NOT VERIFIED` and the reason. Never estimate, extrapolate, or copy a number from repository documentation.
  9: 7. Repository documentation is a claim source, not evidence. Evidence comes from commands, source, tests, and raw run artifacts.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[PY]  verification\http_endpoint_probe.py
================================================================================
  1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2: """Small HTTP echo endpoint used to observe live WebSocket registry updates."""
  3: 
  4: from __future__ import annotations
  5: 
  6: import argparse
  7: import json
  8: from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
  9: 
 10: 
 11: class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
 12:     def do_POST(self):
 13:         length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", "0"))
 14:         body = self.rfile.read(length)
 15:         observation = {
 16:             "path": self.path,
 17:             "body_bytes": len(body),
 18:             "content_type": self.headers.get("Content-Type"),
 19:         }
 20:         print(json.dumps(observation, sort_keys=True), flush=True)
 21:         response = json.dumps({"endpoint_probe": self.path, "body_bytes": len(body)}).encode()
 22:         self.send_response(200)
 23:         self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
 24:         self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response)))
 25:         self.end_headers()
 26:         self.wfile.write(response)
 27: 
 28:     def log_message(self, _format, *_args):
 29:         return
 30: 
 31: 
 32: def main():
 33:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
 34:     parser.add_argument("--host", default="0.0.0.0")
 35:     parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8765)
 36:     args = parser.parse_args()
 37:     server = ThreadingHTTPServer((args.host, args.port), Handler)
 38:     print(json.dumps({"event": "listening", "host": args.host, "port": args.port}), flush=True)
 39:     server.serve_forever()
 40: 
 41: 
 42: if __name__ == "__main__":
 43:     main()

[PY]  verification\invalid_upload_latency.py
================================================================================
  1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2: """Measure invalid-upload failure latency through an isolated ModelMesh gateway."""
  3: 
  4: from __future__ import annotations
  5: 
  6: import argparse
  7: import hashlib
  8: import json
  9: import random
 10: import statistics
 11: import time
 12: import uuid
 13: from datetime import datetime, timezone
 14: from pathlib import Path
 15: from urllib.parse import urlparse
 16: from urllib.error import HTTPError, URLError
 17: from urllib.parse import urljoin
 18: from urllib.request import Request, urlopen
 19: 
 20: from redis.asyncio import Redis
 21: 
 22: 
 23: STREAM = "inference_jobs"
 24: DLQ = "inference_jobs_dlq"
 25: TERMINAL = {"success", "failed"}
 26: 
 27: 
 28: def utc_now():
 29:     return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
 30: 
 31: 
 32: def append_jsonl(path: Path, value: dict):
 33:     with path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as output:
 34:         output.write(json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True) + "\n")
 35: 
 36: 
 37: def quantile(values: list[float], percentile: float):
 38:     ordered = sorted(values)
 39:     if not ordered:
 40:         return None
 41:     position = (len(ordered) - 1) * percentile
 42:     lower = int(position)
 43:     upper = min(lower + 1, len(ordered) - 1)
 44:     fraction = position - lower
 45:     return ordered[lower] + (ordered[upper] - ordered[lower]) * fraction
 46: 
 47: 
 48: def latency_summary(values: list[float]):
 49:     if not values:
 50:         return {"n": 0, "median_ms": None, "p95_ms": None, "spread_ms": None}
 51:     return {
 52:         "n": len(values),
 53:         "median_ms": statistics.median(values),
 54:         "p95_ms": quantile(values, 0.95),
 55:         "spread_ms": max(values) - min(values),
 56:     }
 57: 
 58: 
 59: def http_request(url: str, method="GET", body=None, headers=None, timeout=30):
 60:     request = Request(url, data=body, headers=headers or {}, method=method)
 61:     try:
 62:         with urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:
 63:             response_body = response.read()
 64:             return response.status, response.headers, response_body
 65:     except HTTPError as exc:
 66:         return exc.code, exc.headers, exc.read()
 67:     except URLError:
 68:         raise
 69: 
 70: 
 71: def json_request(base_url: str, path: str, method="GET", payload=None, headers=None):
 72:     request_headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
 73:     request_headers.update(headers or {})
 74:     body = json.dumps(payload).encode() if payload is not None else None
 75:     status, _, response_body = http_request(
 76:         urljoin(base_url.rstrip("/") + "/", path.lstrip("/")),
 77:         method=method,
 78:         body=body,
 79:         headers=request_headers,
 80:     )
 81:     try:
 82:         response_json = json.loads(response_body) if response_body else None
 83:     except json.JSONDecodeError:
 84:         response_json = {"raw": response_body.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")}
 85:     return status, response_json
 86: 
 87: 
 88: def multipart_body(filename: str, content_type: str, payload: bytes):
 89:     boundary = f"----modelmesh-{uuid.uuid4().hex}"
 90:     body = (
 91:         f"--{boundary}\r\n"
 92:         f'Content-Disposition: form-data; name="file"; filename="{filename}"\r\n'
 93:         f"Content-Type: {content_type}\r\n\r\n"
 94:     ).encode() + payload + f"\r\n--{boundary}--\r\n".encode()
 95:     return boundary, body
 96: 
 97: 
 98: def as_dict(fields):
 99:     return {
100:         key.decode() if isinstance(key, bytes) else key:
101:         value.decode() if isinstance(value, bytes) else value
102:         for key, value in fields.items()
103:     }
104: 
105: 
106: async def cleanup_redis(
107:     client: Redis,
108:     job_ids: set[str],
109:     initial_keys: set[str],
110:     keep_worker_groups: bool,
111: ):
112:     cleanup = {"stream_ids": [], "dlq_ids": [], "job_keys": [], "rate_limit_keys": []}
113:     stream_ids = []
114:     for entry_id, fields in await client.xrange(STREAM):
115:         if as_dict(fields).get("job_id") in job_ids:
116:             stream_ids.append(entry_id)
117:     if stream_ids:
118:         await client.xdel(STREAM, *stream_ids)
119:     cleanup["stream_ids"] = [str(value) for value in stream_ids]
120: 
121:     dlq_ids = []
122:     for entry_id, fields in await client.xrange(DLQ):
123:         if as_dict(fields).get("job_id") in job_ids:
124:             dlq_ids.append(entry_id)
125:     if dlq_ids:
126:         await client.xdel(DLQ, *dlq_ids)
127:     cleanup["dlq_ids"] = [str(value) for value in dlq_ids]
128: 
129:     job_keys = []
130:     for job_id in job_ids:
131:         job_keys.extend(
132:             [
133:                 f"payload:{job_id}",
134:                 f"result:{job_id}",
135:                 f"job:{job_id}:status",
136:                 f"job:{job_id}:owner",
137:             ]
138:         )
139:     if job_keys:
140:         await client.delete(*job_keys)
141:     cleanup["job_keys"] = job_keys
142: 
143:     current_keys = {key async for key in client.scan_iter()}
144:     new_rate_keys = [
145:         key for key in current_keys - initial_keys
146:         if (key.decode() if isinstance(key, bytes) else key).startswith("rl:")
147:     ]
148:     if new_rate_keys:
149:         await client.delete(*new_rate_keys)
150:     cleanup["rate_limit_keys"] = [
151:         key.decode() if isinstance(key, bytes) else key for key in new_rate_keys
152:     ]
153: 
154:     known_groups = {"doc-ocr-worker_group", "asr-worker_group"}
155:     try:
156:         groups = await client.xinfo_groups(STREAM)
157:     except Exception as exc:
158:         if "no such key" in str(exc).lower():
159:             groups = []
160:         else:
161:             raise
162:     for group_info in groups:
163:         group_name = group_info["name"]
164:         group_name = group_name.decode() if isinstance(group_name, bytes) else group_name
165:         if group_name in known_groups and not keep_worker_groups:
166:             await client.xgroup_destroy(STREAM, group_name)
167: 
168:     for key in (STREAM, DLQ):
169:         try:
170:             length = await client.xlen(key)
171:             groups = await client.xinfo_groups(key) if key == STREAM else []
172:         except Exception as exc:
173:             if "no such key" in str(exc).lower():
174:                 continue
175:             raise
176:         if length == 0 and not groups:
177:             await client.delete(key)
178:     return cleanup
179:

181: async def run(args):
182:     output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)
183:     output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
184:     raw_dir = output_dir / "raw"
185:     raw_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
186:     request_log = raw_dir / "invalid_upload_requests.jsonl"
187:     poll_log = raw_dir / "invalid_upload_polls.jsonl"
188:     payload_dir = raw_dir / "payloads"
189:     payload_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
190: 
191:     run_id = uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
192:     started_at = utc_now()
193:     parsed_redis_url = urlparse(args.redis_url)
194:     if parsed_redis_url.path.rstrip("/") != "/1":
195:         failure_path = output_dir / "invalid_upload_failure.json"
196:         failure_path.write_text(
197:             json.dumps(
198:                 {
199:                     "started_at_utc": started_at,
200:                     "error": f"Only Redis DB 1 is allowed; got {parsed_redis_url.path!r}",
201:                 },
202:                 indent=2,
203:             ),
204:             encoding="utf-8",
205:         )
206:         raise ValueError(f"Only Redis DB 1 is allowed; got {parsed_redis_url.path!r}")
207: 
208:     client = Redis.from_url(args.redis_url, decode_responses=False)
209:     initial_keys = {key async for key in client.scan_iter()}
210:     initial_dbsize = await client.dbsize()
211:     key_names = {
212:         key.decode() if isinstance(key, bytes) else key for key in initial_keys
213:     }
214:     initial_groups = []
215:     if key_names == {STREAM}:
216:         initial_stream_length = await client.xlen(STREAM)
217:         initial_groups = await client.xinfo_groups(STREAM)
218:         initial_group_names = {
219:             group["name"].decode()
220:             if isinstance(group["name"], bytes)
221:             else group["name"]
222:             for group in initial_groups
223:         }
224:         expected_groups = {"doc-ocr-worker_group", "asr-worker_group"}
225:         if initial_stream_length != 0 or initial_group_names != expected_groups:
226:             await client.aclose()
227:             raise RuntimeError(
228:                 "Redis DB 1 has unexpected stream state: "
229:                 f"length={initial_stream_length}, groups={sorted(initial_group_names)}"
230:             )
231:         for group_name in initial_group_names:
232:             pending = await client.xpending(STREAM, group_name)
233:             if int(pending.get("pending", 0)) != 0:
234:                 await client.aclose()
235:                 raise RuntimeError(
236:                     f"Redis DB 1 group {group_name} has pending messages before the run"
237:                 )
238:     elif initial_dbsize != 0:
239:         await client.aclose()
240:         error = (
241:             "Redis DB 1 contains keys other than the two expected empty worker groups: "
242:             f"{sorted(key_names)}"
243:         )
244:         (output_dir / "invalid_upload_failure.json").write_text(
245:             json.dumps(
246:                 {
247:                     "started_at_utc": started_at,
248:                     "redis_db": 1,
249:                     "initial_dbsize": initial_dbsize,
250:                     "initial_redis_keys": sorted(key_names),
251:                     "initial_worker_groups": [
252:                         group["name"].decode()
253:                         if isinstance(group["name"], bytes)
254:                         else group["name"]
255:                         for group in initial_groups
256:                     ],
257:                     "error": error,
258:                 },
259:                 indent=2,
260:             ),
261:             encoding="utf-8",
262:         )
263:         raise RuntimeError(error)
264: 
265:     image_seed = 145982
266:     audio_seed = 285971
267:     image_bytes = random.Random(image_seed).randbytes(256)
268:     audio_bytes = random.Random(audio_seed).randbytes(256)
269:     image_path = payload_dir / "invalid_image.png"
270:     audio_path = payload_dir / "invalid_audio.wav"
271:     image_path.write_bytes(image_bytes)
272:     audio_path.write_bytes(audio_bytes)
273: 
274:     raw_jobs = []
275:     job_ids = set()
276:     auth_summary = {}
277:     http_429_count = 0
278:     password = uuid.uuid4().hex + "aA1!"
279:     email = f"verify-{run_id}@example.com"
280:     try:
281:         code, result = json_request(
282:             args.base_url,
283:             "/v1/auth/register",
284:             method="POST",
285:             payload={"email": email, "password": password},
286:         )
287:         auth_summary["register_status"] = code
288:         auth_summary["register_body"] = result
289:         if code != 200:
290:             raise RuntimeError(f"Scratch user registration returned HTTP {code}: {result}")
291: 
292:         code, result = json_request(
293:             args.base_url,
294:             "/v1/auth/login",
295:             method="POST",
296:             payload={"email": email, "password": password},
297:         )
298:         auth_summary["login_status"] = code
299:         auth_summary["login_has_access_token"] = bool(
300:             isinstance(result, dict) and result.get("access_token")
301:         )
302:         if code != 200 or not auth_summary["login_has_access_token"]:
303:             raise RuntimeError(f"Scratch user login failed with HTTP {code}")
304:         token = result["access_token"]
305: 
306:         code, result = json_request(
307:             args.base_url,
308:             "/v1/auth/api-keys",
309:             method="POST",
310:             payload={"rate_limit_per_min": args.rate_limit_per_min},
311:             headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"},
312:         )
313:         auth_summary["api_key_status"] = code
314:         auth_summary["api_key_rate_limit_per_min"] = (
315:             result.get("rate_limit_per_min") if isinstance(result, dict) else None
316:         )
317:         auth_summary["api_key_redacted"] = bool(
318:             isinstance(result, dict) and result.get("key")
319:         )
320:         if code != 200 or not auth_summary["api_key_redacted"]:
321:             auth_summary["api_key_error"] = result
322:             raise RuntimeError(f"API-key creation failed with HTTP {code}")
323:         api_key = result["key"]
324:         auth_headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}"}
325: 
326:         for model, filename, mime, payload in (
327:             ("doc-ocr", "invalid.png", "image/png", image_bytes),
328:             ("asr", "invalid.wav", "audio/wav", audio_bytes),
329:         ):
330:             for index in range(1, args.samples_per_model + 1):
331:                 boundary, body = multipart_body(filename, mime, payload)
332:                 submitted_at = utc_now()
333:                 started = time.perf_counter()
334:                 try:
335:                     status_code, _, response_body = http_request(
336:                         urljoin(
337:                             args.base_url.rstrip("/") + "/",
338:                             f"v1/infer/{model}",
339:                         ),
340:                         method="POST",
341:                         body=body,
342:                         headers={
343:                             "Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}",
344:                             "Content-Type": f"multipart/form-data; boundary={boundary}",
345:                         },
346:                     )
347:                     try:
348:                         response = json.loads(response_body)
349:                     except json.JSONDecodeError:
350:                         response = {"raw": response_body.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")}
351:                 except Exception as exc:
352:                     status_code = None
353:                     response = {"error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"}
354: 
355:                 if status_code == 429:
356:                     http_429_count += 1
357:                 job_id = response.get("job_id") if isinstance(response, dict) else None
358:                 job = {
359:                     "run_id": run_id,
360:                     "model": model,
361:                     "index": index,
362:                     "submit_time_utc": submitted_at,
363:                     "submit_http_status": status_code,
364:                     "job_id": job_id,
365:                     "submit_response": response,
366:                     "polls": 0,
367:                     "final_status": None,
368:                     "error": None,
369:                     "submit_to_terminal_ms": None,
370:                     "poll_interval_seconds": args.poll_interval_seconds,
371:                 }
372:                 append_jsonl(request_log, job.copy())
373:                 if not job_id:
374:                     raw_jobs.append(job)
375:                     continue
376:                 job_ids.add(job_id)
377: 
378:                 deadline = time.monotonic() + args.timeout_seconds
379:                 while time.monotonic() < deadline:
380:                     poll_at = utc_now()
381:                     elapsed_ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000
382:                     try:
383:                         poll_status, poll_result = json_request(
384:                             args.base_url,
385:                             f"/v1/jobs/{job_id}",
386:                             headers=auth_headers,
387:                         )
388:                     except Exception as exc:
389:                         poll_status = None
390:                         poll_result = {"error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"}
391:                     job["polls"] += 1
392:                     if poll_status == 429:
393:                         http_429_count += 1
394:                     current_status = (
395:                         poll_result.get("status")
396:                         if isinstance(poll_result, dict)
397:                         else None
398:                     )
399:                     current_error = (
400:                         poll_result.get("error")
401:                         if isinstance(poll_result, dict)
402:                         else None
403:                     )
404:                     append_jsonl(
405:                         poll_log,
406:                         {
407:                             "run_id": run_id,
408:                             "model": model,
409:                             "index": index,
410:                             "job_id": job_id,
411:                             "poll_index": job["polls"],
412:                             "poll_time_utc": poll_at,
413:                             "http_status": poll_status,
414:                             "status": current_status,
415:                             "error": current_error,
416:                             "elapsed_ms": elapsed_ms,
417:                         },
418:                     )
419:                     if current_status in TERMINAL:
420:                         job["final_status"] = current_status
421:                         job["error"] = current_error
422:                         job["submit_to_terminal_ms"] = (
423:                             time.perf_counter() - started
424:                         ) * 1000
425:                         break
426:                     time.sleep(args.poll_interval_seconds)
427: 
428:                 raw_jobs.append(job)
429: 
430:         for item in raw_jobs:
431:             append_jsonl(request_log, {"event": "completed", **item})
432: 
433:         model_summaries = {}
434:         for model in ("doc-ocr", "asr"):
435:             subset = [item for item in raw_jobs if item["model"] == model]
436:             failures = [
437:                 item["submit_to_terminal_ms"]
438:                 for item in subset
439:                 if item["final_status"] == "failed"
440:                 and item["submit_to_terminal_ms"] is not None
441:             ]
442:             model_summaries[model] = {
443:                 **latency_summary(failures),
444:                 "submitted": len(subset),
445:                 "failed": sum(item["final_status"] == "failed" for item in subset),
446:                 "success": sum(item["final_status"] == "success" for item in subset),
447:                 "not_terminal": sum(item["final_status"] is None for item in subset),
448:                 "error_texts": sorted(
449:                     {str(item["error"]) for item in subset if item.get("error")}
450:                 ),
451:             }
452: 
453:         cleanup = await cleanup_redis(
454:             client, job_ids, initial_keys, args.keep_worker_groups
455:         )
456:         final_keys = {key async for key in client.scan_iter()}
457:         remaining_keys = [
458:             key.decode() if isinstance(key, bytes) else key
459:             for key in sorted(final_keys, key=lambda value: str(value))
460:         ]
461:         summary = {
462:             "run_id": run_id,
463:             "started_at_utc": started_at,
464:             "ended_at_utc": utc_now(),
465:             "base_url": args.base_url,
466:             "redis_db": 1,
467:             "initial_dbsize": initial_dbsize,
468:             "final_dbsize": await client.dbsize(),
469:             "initial_redis_keys": sorted(key_names),
470:             "initial_worker_groups": [
471:                 group["name"].decode()
472:                 if isinstance(group["name"], bytes)
473:                 else group["name"]
474:                 for group in initial_groups
475:             ],
476:             "auth": auth_summary,
477:             "remaining_redis_keys": remaining_keys,
478:             "redis_cleanup": cleanup,
479:             "auth": auth_summary,
480:             "samples_per_model": args.samples_per_model,
481:             "poll_interval_seconds": args.poll_interval_seconds,
482:             "timeout_seconds": args.timeout_seconds,
483:             "http_429_count": http_429_count,
484:             "payloads": {
485:                 "invalid_image": {
486:                     "path": str(image_path),
487:                     "bytes": len(image_bytes),
488:                     "seed": image_seed,
489:                     "sha256": hashlib.sha256(image_bytes).hexdigest(),
490:                 },
491:                 "invalid_audio": {
492:                     "path": str(audio_path),
493:                     "bytes": len(audio_bytes),
494:                     "seed": audio_seed,
495:                     "sha256": hashlib.sha256(audio_bytes).hexdigest(),
496:                 },
497:             },
498:             "models": model_summaries,
499:             "jobs": raw_jobs,
500:         }
501:         (output_dir / "invalid_upload_summary.json").write_text(
502:             json.dumps(summary, indent=2, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8"
503:         )
504:         print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
505:         return (
506:             0
507:             if set(remaining_keys) == key_names and http_429_count == 0
508:             else 1
509:         )
510:     except Exception as exc:
511:         cleanup = {}
512:         cleanup_error = None
513:         try:
514:             cleanup = await cleanup_redis(
515:                 client, job_ids, initial_keys, args.keep_worker_groups
516:             )
517:         except Exception as cleanup_exc:
518:             cleanup_error = f"{type(cleanup_exc).__name__}: {cleanup_exc}"
519:         failure = {
520:             "run_id": run_id,
521:             "started_at_utc": started_at,
522:             "ended_at_utc": utc_now(),
523:             "base_url": args.base_url,
524:             "redis_db": 1,
525:             "initial_dbsize": initial_dbsize,
526:             "initial_redis_keys": sorted(key_names),
527:             "final_dbsize": await client.dbsize(),
528:             "http_429_count": http_429_count,
529:             "jobs_so_far": raw_jobs,
530:             "job_ids": sorted(job_ids),
531:             "redis_cleanup": cleanup,
532:             "redis_cleanup_error": cleanup_error,
533:             "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
534:         }
535:         (output_dir / "invalid_upload_summary.json").write_text(
536:             json.dumps(failure, indent=2, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8"
537:         )
538:         print(json.dumps(failure, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
539:         raise
540:     finally:
541:         # Credentials are intentionally kept in memory only.
542:         password = ""
543:         if client:
544:             await client.aclose()
545: 
546: 
547: def main():
548:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
549:     parser.add_argument("--base-url", default="http://host.docker.internal:8003")
550:     parser.add_argument("--redis-url", default="redis://redis:6379/1")
551:     parser.add_argument("--output-dir", required=True)
552:     parser.add_argument("--samples-per-model", type=int, default=10)
553:     parser.add_argument("--rate-limit-per-min", type=int, default=1000)
554:     parser.add_argument("--poll-interval-seconds", type=float, default=0.1)
555:     parser.add_argument("--timeout-seconds", type=float, default=180)
556:     parser.add_argument(
557:         "--keep-worker-groups",
558:         action="store_true",
559:         help="Leave the two isolated worker groups for caller cleanup after worker shutdown",
560:     )
561:     args = parser.parse_args()
562:     return __import__("asyncio").run(run(args))
563: 
564: 
565: if __name__ == "__main__":
566:     raise SystemExit(main())

[PY]  verification\queue_harness.py

  1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2: """Exercise both queue consumers against Redis DB 1 without loading model weights."""
  3: 
  4: from __future__ import annotations
  5: 
  6: import argparse
  7: import asyncio

  9: import json
 10: import logging

 17: from types import SimpleNamespace

 20: from redis.asyncio import Redis

 23: ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
 24: STREAM = "inference_jobs"
 25: DLQ = "inference_jobs_dlq"
 26: SEEDS = (20261123, 20261124, 20261125)

 29: def decode(value):
 30:     return value.decode() if isinstance(value, bytes) else value
 31: 
 32: 
 33: def decode_fields(fields):
 34:     return {decode(key): value for key, value in fields.items()}
 35: 
 36: 
 37: def append_jsonl(path: Path, value: dict) -> None:
 38:     with path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as output:
 39:         output.write(json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True) + "\n")

 42: def import_test_helper():
 43:     helper_path = ROOT / "tests" / "helpers.py"
 44:     spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("verification_test_helpers", helper_path)
 45:     if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
 46:         raise ImportError(f"Cannot load test helper from {helper_path}")
 47:     helper = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
 48:     spec.loader.exec_module(helper)
 49:     return helper

 52: def load_consumer(worker: str, sleep):
 53:     helper = import_test_helper()
 54:     if worker == "doc":
 55:         worker_name = "doc-ocr-worker"
 56:         rel_path = "workers/doc_ocr/consumer.py"
 57:         inference_stub = SimpleNamespace(extract_text=None)
 58:         inference_name = "extract_text"
 59:         error_text = "Invalid image data"
 60:     else:
 61:         worker_name = "asr-worker"
 62:         rel_path = "workers/asr/consumer.py"
 63:         inference_stub = SimpleNamespace(transcribe=None, _load_model=lambda: None)
 64:         inference_name = "transcribe"
 65:         error_text = "Invalid audio data"
 66: 
 67:     def unused_inference(_payload):
 68:         raise AssertionError("inference stub was not configured")
 69: 
 70:     setattr(inference_stub, inference_name, unused_inference)
 71:     previous_worker_name = os.environ.get("WORKER_NAME")
 72:     os.environ["WORKER_NAME"] = worker_name
 73:     try:
 74:         module = helper.load_module_from_path(
 75:             f"verification_{worker}_{uuid.uuid4().hex}",
 76:             rel_path,
 77:             stubs={
 78:                 "inference": inference_stub,
 79:                 "metrics": SimpleNamespace(log_metric=lambda *_args: None),
 80:             },
 81:         )
 82:     finally:
 83:         if previous_worker_name is None:
 84:             os.environ.pop("WORKER_NAME", None)
 85:         else:
 86:             os.environ["WORKER_NAME"] = previous_worker_name
 87: 
 88:     module.asyncio = SimpleNamespace(
 89:         sleep=sleep,
 90:         to_thread=asyncio.to_thread,
 91:         CancelledError=asyncio.CancelledError,
 92:     )
 93:     return module, inference_stub, inference_name, error_text

 95: 
 96: async def stream_length(client: Redis, key: str) -> int:
 97:     try:
 98:         return int(await client.xlen(key))
 99:     except Exception as exc:
100:         if "no such key" in str(exc).lower():
101:             return 0
102:         raise
103: 
104: 
105: async def pending_count(client: Redis, group: str) -> int:
106:     pending = await client.xpending(STREAM, group)
107:     return int(pending.get("pending", 0))
108: 
109: 
110: async def get_terminal_status(client: Redis, job_id: str):
111:     raw = await client.get(f"result:{job_id}")
112:     if raw is None:
113:         return None
114:     return json.loads(raw)
115: 
116: 
117: async def drain_group(client: Redis, module, group: str, consumer: str, deadline: float):
118:     while time.monotonic() < deadline:
119:         messages = await client.xreadgroup(
120:             group,
121:             consumer,
122:             {STREAM: ">"},
123:             count=50,
124:             block=100,
125:         )
126:         for _, entries in messages:
127:             for entry_id, fields in entries:
128:                 await module.process_entry(client, group, entry_id, fields)
129:         if not messages and await pending_count(client, group) == 0:
130:             return
131:     raise TimeoutError("Scenario did not drain its consumer group within the deadline")
132: 
133: 
134: async def count_dlq_jobs(client: Redis, job_ids: set[str]) -> int:
135:     count = 0
136:     for _, fields in await client.xrange(DLQ):
137:         decoded = decode_fields(fields)
138:         if decode(decoded.get("job_id", "")) in job_ids:
139:             count += 1
140:     return count
141: 
142: 
143: async def remove_empty_created_stream(client: Redis, key: str) -> None:
144:     if await stream_length(client, key) != 0:
145:         return
146:     try:
147:         groups = await client.xinfo_groups(key)
148:     except Exception as exc:
149:         if "no such key" in str(exc).lower():
150:             return
151:         raise
152:     if not groups:
153:         await client.delete(key)
154: 
155: 
156: async def cleanup_scenario(client: Redis, group: str, job_ids: set[str]) -> None:
157:     try:
158:         await client.xgroup_destroy(STREAM, group)
159:     except Exception as exc:
160:         if "no such key" not in str(exc).lower() and "no such consumer group" not in str(exc).lower():
161:             raise
162: 
163:     stream_ids = []
164:     for entry_id, fields in await client.xrange(STREAM):
165:         decoded = decode_fields(fields)
166:         if decode(decoded.get("job_id", "")) in job_ids:
167:             stream_ids.append(entry_id)
168:     if stream_ids:
169:         await client.xdel(STREAM, *stream_ids)
170: 
171:     dlq_ids = []
172:     for entry_id, fields in await client.xrange(DLQ):
173:         decoded = decode_fields(fields)
174:         if decode(decoded.get("job_id", "")) in job_ids:
175:             dlq_ids.append(entry_id)
176:     if dlq_ids:
177:         await client.xdel(DLQ, *dlq_ids)
178: 
179:     keys = []
180:     for job_id in job_ids:
181:         keys.extend(
182:             [
183:                 f"payload:{job_id}",
184:                 f"result:{job_id}",
185:                 f"job:{job_id}:status",
186:             ]
187:         )
188:     if keys:
189:         await client.delete(*keys)
190: 
191: 
192: async def run_scenario(
193:     *,
194:     client: Redis,
195:     module,
196:     inference_stub,
197:     inference_name: str,
198:     error_text: str,
199:     worker: str,
200:     run_id: str,
201:     scenario: str,
202:     job_count: int,
203:     event_path: Path,
204:     jobs_path: Path,
205:     mode: str,
206:     seed: int | None = None,
207:     real_sleep: bool = False,
208:     reclaim_idle_ms: int = 1000,
209: ) -> dict:
210:     scenario_id = f"{run_id}-{worker}-{scenario}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
211:     group = f"verification-{scenario_id}"
212:     consumer = f"verification-consumer-{scenario_id}"
213:     module.CONSUMER = consumer
214:     module.RECLAIM_IDLE_MS = reclaim_idle_ms
215:     job_ids = {f"{scenario_id}-{index:04d}" for index in range(job_count)}
216:     attempt_counts = defaultdict(int)
217:     sleep_delays = []
218:     current_job = None
219:     rng = random.Random(seed) if seed is not None else None
220:     original_sleep = asyncio.sleep
221: 
222:     async def record_sleep(delay):
223:         sleep_delays.append(float(delay))
224:         append_jsonl(
225:             event_path,
226:             {
227:                 "event": "sleep",
228:                 "scenario": scenario,
229:                 "worker": worker,
230:                 "job_id": current_job,
231:                 "delay_seconds": float(delay),
232:                 "real_time": real_sleep,
233:             },
234:         )
235:         if real_sleep:
236:             await original_sleep(delay)
237: 
238:     module.asyncio.sleep = record_sleep
239: 
240:     def infer(payload):
241:         nonlocal current_job
242:         current_job = decode(payload)
243:         attempt_counts[current_job] += 1
244:         attempt = attempt_counts[current_job]
245:         outcome = "success"
246:         error_type = None
247:         error_value = None
248: 
249:         if mode == "permanent":
250:             error = ValueError(error_text)
251:         elif mode == "always_transient":
252:             error = RuntimeError("Synthetic transient failure")
253:         elif mode == "fail_twice":
254:             error = RuntimeError("Synthetic transient failure") if attempt <= 2 else None
255:         elif mode == "random":
256:             draw = rng.random()
257:             if draw < 0.05:
258:                 error = ValueError(error_text)
259:             elif draw < 0.35:
260:                 error = RuntimeError("Synthetic transient failure")
261:             else:
262:                 error = None
263:         elif mode == "success":
264:             error = None
265:         else:
266:             raise ValueError(f"Unsupported mode: {mode}")
267: 
268:         if error is not None:
269:             outcome = "permanent_error" if isinstance(error, ValueError) else "transient_error"
270:             error_type = type(error).__name__
271:             error_value = str(error)
272:             append_jsonl(
273:                 event_path,
274:                 {
275:                     "event": "attempt",
276:                     "scenario": scenario,
277:                     "worker": worker,
278:                     "job_id": current_job,
279:                     "attempt": attempt,
280:                     "outcome": outcome,
281:                     "error_type": error_type,
282:                     "error": error_value,
283:                 },
284:             )
285:             raise error
286: 
287:         append_jsonl(
288:             event_path,
289:             {
290:                 "event": "attempt",
291:                 "scenario": scenario,
292:                 "worker": worker,
293:                 "job_id": current_job,
294:                 "attempt": attempt,
295:                 "outcome": outcome,
296:             },
297:         )
298:         return {"text": "verification success"}
299: 
300:     setattr(inference_stub, inference_name, infer)
301:     setattr(module, inference_name, infer)
302: 
303:     await client.xgroup_create(STREAM, group, id="$", mkstream=True)
304:     before_stream = await stream_length(client, STREAM)
305:     before_dlq = await stream_length(client, DLQ)
306:     append_jsonl(
307:         event_path,
308:         {
309:             "event": "scenario_start",
310:             "scenario": scenario,
311:             "worker": worker,
312:             "jobs": job_count,
313:             "seed": seed,
314:             "stream_before": before_stream,
315:             "dlq_before": before_dlq,
316:             "real_sleep": real_sleep,
317:             "reclaim_idle_ms": reclaim_idle_ms if scenario == "reclaim" else None,
318:         },
319:     )
320:     start = time.perf_counter()
321:     summary = {
322:         "scenario": scenario,
323:         "worker": worker,
324:         "seed": seed,
325:         "job_count": job_count,
326:         "real_sleep": real_sleep,
327:         "error": None,
328:     }
329: 
330:     try:
331:         if scenario == "reclaim":
332:             for job_id in sorted(job_ids):
333:                 await client.xadd(
334:                     STREAM,
335:                     {
336:                         "job_id": job_id,
337:                         "worker_name": module.WORKER_NAME,
338:                         "model_id": "verification",
339:                         "model_name": "doc-ocr" if worker == "doc" else "asr",
340:                         "model_version": "v1",
341:                         "retry_count": "0",
342:                         "payload": job_id,
343:                     },
344:                 )
345:             claimed = await client.xreadgroup(
346:                 group,
347:                 f"ghost-{scenario_id}",
348:                 {STREAM: ">"},
349:                 count=job_count,
350:             )
351:             pending_before_reclaim = await pending_count(client, group)
352:             await original_sleep((reclaim_idle_ms + 250) / 1000)
353:             await module.reclaim_stale(client, group)
354:             summary["ghost_delivered"] = sum(len(entries) for _, entries in claimed)
355:             summary["pending_before_reclaim"] = pending_before_reclaim
356:             await drain_group(
357:                 client,
358:                 module,
359:                 group,
360:                 consumer,
361:                 time.monotonic() + 180,
362:             )
363:         else:
364:             for job_id in sorted(job_ids):
365:                 await client.xadd(
366:                     STREAM,
367:                     {
368:                         "job_id": job_id,
369:                         "worker_name": module.WORKER_NAME,
370:                         "model_id": "verification",
371:                         "model_name": "doc-ocr" if worker == "doc" else "asr",
372:                         "model_version": "v1",
373:                         "retry_count": "0",
374:                         "payload": job_id,
375:                     },
376:                 )
377: 
378:             await drain_group(
379:                 client,
380:                 module,
381:                 group,
382:                 consumer,
383:                 time.monotonic() + 180,
384:             )
385: 
386:         terminal = {}
387:         for job_id in sorted(job_ids):
388:             status = await get_terminal_status(client, job_id)
389:             if status is not None:
390:                 terminal[job_id] = status
391:             append_jsonl(
392:                 jobs_path,
393:                 {
394:                     "scenario": scenario,
395:                     "worker": worker,
396:                     "seed": seed,
397:                     "job_id": job_id,
398:                     "attempts": attempt_counts[job_id],
399:                     "status": status.get("status") if status else None,
400:                     "error": status.get("error") if status else None,
401:                     "result": status.get("result") if status else None,
402:                 },
403:             )
404: 
405:         stream_after = await stream_length(client, STREAM)
406:         dlq_after = await stream_length(client, DLQ)
407:         dlq_count = await count_dlq_jobs(client, job_ids)
408:         pending_after = await pending_count(client, group)
409:         statuses = [value.get("status") for value in terminal.values()]
410:         summary.update(
411:             {
412:                 "success": statuses.count("success"),
413:                 "failed": statuses.count("failed"),
414:                 "nonterminal": job_count - len(terminal),
415:                 "total_attempts": sum(attempt_counts.values()),
416:                 "dlq_entries": dlq_count,
417:                 "pending_at_end": pending_after,
418:                 "stream_length_before": before_stream,
419:                 "stream_length_after": stream_after,
420:                 "stream_entries_added": stream_after - before_stream,
421:                 "dlq_length_before": before_dlq,
422:                 "dlq_length_after": dlq_after,
423:                 "sleep_delays_seconds": sleep_delays,
424:                 "sleep_total_seconds": sum(sleep_delays),
425:                 "wall_seconds": time.perf_counter() - start,
426:             }
427:         )
428:         summary["attempts_by_job"] = dict(attempt_counts)
429:         summary["terminal_error_texts"] = sorted(
430:             {str(value.get("error")) for value in terminal.values() if value.get("error")}
431:         )
432:         return summary
433:     except Exception as exc:
434:         summary["error"] = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
435:         summary["wall_seconds"] = time.perf_counter() - start
436:         raise
437:     finally:
438:         await cleanup_scenario(client, group, job_ids)
439:         append_jsonl(
440:             event_path,
441:             {
442:                 "event": "scenario_end",
443:                 "scenario": scenario,
444:                 "worker": worker,
445:                 "wall_seconds": summary.get("wall_seconds"),
446:                 "error": summary.get("error"),
447:             },
448:         )
449: 
450: 
451: async def run_all(redis_url: str, output_dir: Path, reclaim_idle_ms: int):
452:     parsed = urlparse(redis_url)
453:     if parsed.path.rstrip("/") != "/1":
454:         raise ValueError(f"Only Redis DB 1 is allowed; got URL path {parsed.path!r}")
455: 
456:     output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
457:     event_path = output_dir / "harness_events.jsonl"
458:     jobs_path = output_dir / "harness_jobs.jsonl"
459:     summary_path = output_dir / "harness_summary.json"
460:     run_id = uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
461:     client = Redis.from_url(redis_url, decode_responses=False)
462:     summary = {
463:         "run_id": run_id,
464:         "redis_url": redis_url,
465:         "redis_db": 1,
466:         "started_at_utc": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime()),
467:         "initial_dbsize": await client.dbsize(),
468:         "scenarios": [],
469:     }
470:     if summary["initial_dbsize"] != 0:
471:         await client.aclose()
472:         raise RuntimeError(
473:             f"Redis DB 1 is not empty ({summary['initial_dbsize']} keys); refusing to alter it"
474:         )
475: 
476:     root_logger = logging.getLogger()
477:     root_logger.setLevel(logging.WARNING)
478:     handler = logging.FileHandler(output_dir / "consumer.log", encoding="utf-8")
479:     handler.setFormatter(logging.Formatter("%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s %(message)s"))
480:     root_logger.addHandler(handler)
481: 
482:     failure_count = 0
483:     try:
484:         for worker in ("doc", "asr"):
485:             async def no_sleep(_delay):
486:                 return None
487: 
488:             module, inference_stub, inference_name, error_text = load_consumer(worker, no_sleep)
489:             cases = [
490:                 ("permanent_value_error", 1, "permanent", None, False),
491:                 ("always_transient_real_time", 1, "always_transient", None, True),
492:                 ("transient_twice_then_success", 1, "fail_twice", None, False),
493:                 *[
494:                     (f"random_500_seed_{seed}", 500, "random", seed, False)
495:                     for seed in SEEDS
496:                 ],
497:                 ("reclaim_20", 20, "success", None, False),
498:             ]
499:             for name, count, mode, seed, real_sleep in cases:
500:                 try:
501:                     scenario_result = await run_scenario(
502:                         client=client,
503:                         module=module,
504:                         inference_stub=inference_stub,
505:                         inference_name=inference_name,
506:                         error_text=error_text,
507:                         worker=worker,
508:                         run_id=run_id,
509:                         scenario="reclaim" if name == "reclaim_20" else name,
510:                         job_count=count,
511:                         event_path=event_path,
512:                         jobs_path=jobs_path,
513:                         mode=mode,
514:                         seed=seed,
515:                         real_sleep=real_sleep,
516:                         reclaim_idle_ms=reclaim_idle_ms,
517:                     )
518:                     scenario_result["scenario"] = name
519:                     summary["scenarios"].append(scenario_result)
520:                     append_jsonl(event_path, {"event": "scenario_summary", **scenario_result})
521:                 except Exception as exc:
522:                     failure_count += 1
523:                     failed = {
524:                         "scenario": name,
525:                         "worker": worker,
526:                         "seed": seed,
527:                         "job_count": count,
528:                         "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
529:                     }
530:                     summary["scenarios"].append(failed)
531:                     append_jsonl(event_path, {"event": "scenario_failure", **failed})
532:                 summary_path.write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")
533:     finally:
534:         await remove_empty_created_stream(client, STREAM)
535:         await remove_empty_created_stream(client, DLQ)
536:         summary["final_dbsize"] = await client.dbsize()
537:         summary["ended_at_utc"] = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime())
538:         summary["scenario_failures"] = failure_count
539:         summary_path.write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")
540:         root_logger.removeHandler(handler)
541:         handler.close()
542:         await client.aclose()
543: 
544:     print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
545:     if failure_count or summary["final_dbsize"] != 0:
546:         return 1
547:     return 0
548: 
549: 
550: def main():
551:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
552:     parser.add_argument("--redis-url", default="redis://redis:6379/1")
553:     parser.add_argument("--output-dir", required=True, type=Path)
554:     parser.add_argument("--reclaim-idle-ms", default=1000, type=int)
555:     args = parser.parse_args()
556:     return asyncio.run(run_all(args.redis_url, args.output_dir, args.reclaim_idle_ms))
557: 
558: 
559: if __name__ == "__main__":
560:     raise SystemExit(main())

  41: def utc_now() -> str:
  42:     return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")
  43: 
  44: 
  45: def write_json(path: Path, value) -> None:
  46:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  47:     path.write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
  48: 
  49: 
  50: def append_jsonl(path: Path, rows) -> None:
  51:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  52:     with path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as output:
  53:         for row in rows:
  54:             output.write(json.dumps(row, sort_keys=True) + "\n")
  55: 
  56: 
  57: def quantile(values: list[float], percentile: float):
  58:     ordered = sorted(values)
  59:     if not ordered:
  60:         return None
  61:     position = (len(ordered) - 1) * percentile
  62:     lower = int(position)
  63:     upper = min(lower + 1, len(ordered) - 1)
  64:     return ordered[lower] + (ordered[upper] - ordered[lower]) * (position - lower)
  65: 
  66: 
  67: def latency_summary(values: list[float]) -> dict:
  68:     if not values:
  69:         return {"n": 0, "median_ms": None, "p95_ms": None, "p99_ms": None, "max_ms": None, "spread_ms": None}
  70:     return {
  71:         "n": len(values),
  72:         "median_ms": float(statistics.median(values)),
  73:         "p95_ms": quantile(values, 0.95),
  74:         "p99_ms": quantile(values, 0.99),
  75:         "max_ms": max(values),
  76:         "spread_ms": max(values) - min(values),
  77:     }
  78: 
  79: 
  80: def parse_size(text: str) -> int:
  81:     units = {
  82:         "B": 1,
  83:         "KB": 1000,
  84:         "KIB": 1024,
  85:         "MB": 1000**2,
  86:         "MIB": 1024**2,
  87:         "GB": 1000**3,
  88:         "GIB": 1024**3,
  89:         "TB": 1000**4,
  90:         "TIB": 1024**4,
  91:     }
  92:     value = text.strip().upper()
  93:     for unit in sorted(units, key=len, reverse=True):
  94:         if value.endswith(unit):
  95:             return int(float(value[: -len(unit)].strip()) * units[unit])
  96:     return int(float(value))
  97: 
  98: 
  99: def redis_command(*args: str) -> str:
 100:     command = [
 101:         "docker",
 102:         "compose",
 103:         "exec",
 104:         "-T",
 105:         "redis",
 106:         "redis-cli",
 107:         "-n",
 108:         "1",
 109:         "--raw",
 110:         *map(str, args),
 111:     ]
 112:     result = subprocess.run(
 113:         command,
 114:         cwd=WORKTREE_ROOT,
 115:         capture_output=True,
 116:         text=True,
 117:         timeout=30,
 118:         check=False,
 119:     )
 120:     if result.returncode:
 121:         raise RuntimeError(f"Redis command failed ({result.returncode}): {result.stderr.strip()}")
 122:     return result.stdout.strip()
 123: 
 124: 
 125: def redis_snapshot() -> dict:
 126:     info = {}
 127:     for line in redis_command("INFO", "memory").splitlines():
 128:         if ":" in line:
 129:             key, value = line.split(":", 1)
 130:             info[key] = value
 131:     maxmemory = redis_command("CONFIG", "GET", "maxmemory").splitlines()
 132:     policy = redis_command("CONFIG", "GET", "maxmemory-policy").splitlines()
 133:     return {
 134:         "dbsize": int(redis_command("DBSIZE") or 0),
 135:         "used_memory_bytes": int(info.get("used_memory", "0")),
 136:         "used_memory_human": info.get("used_memory_human"),
 137:         "maxmemory_bytes": int(maxmemory[-1]) if len(maxmemory) > 1 else None,
 138:         "maxmemory_policy": policy[-1] if len(policy) > 1 else None,
 139:     }

 141: 
 142: def parse_docker_stats() -> dict:
 143:     command = [
 144:         "docker",
 145:         "stats",
 146:         "--no-stream",
 147:         "--format",
 148:         "{{.Name}}|{{.CPUPerc}}|{{.MemUsage}}",
 149:         "verify-registry-gateway",
 150:         "modelmesh-redis-1",
 151:         "modelmesh-postgres-1",
 152:         "modelmesh-doc-ocr-worker-1",
 153:         "modelmesh-asr-worker-1",
 154:     ]
 155:     result = subprocess.run(
 156:         command,
 157:         cwd=WORKTREE_ROOT,
 158:         capture_output=True,
 159:         text=True,
 160:         timeout=20,
 161:         check=False,
 162:     )
 163:     if result.returncode:
 164:         raise RuntimeError(f"docker stats failed: {result.stderr.strip()}")
 165:     rows = {}
 166:     for line in result.stdout.splitlines():
 167:         fields = line.strip().split("|")
 168:         if len(fields) != 3:
 169:             continue
 170:         name, cpu, memory = fields
 171:         used = memory.split("/", 1)[0].strip()
 172:         rows[name] = {
 173:             "cpu_percent": float(cpu.strip().rstrip("%")),
 174:             "memory_bytes": parse_size(used),
 175:             "memory_text": memory.strip(),
 176:         }
 177:     return rows

 180: def docker_container_id(name: str) -> str:
 181:     return subprocess.run(
 182:         ["docker", "inspect", "-f", "{{.Id}}", name],
 183:         cwd=WORKTREE_ROOT,
 184:         capture_output=True,
 185:         text=True,
 186:         timeout=20,
 187:         check=True,
 188:     ).stdout.strip()
