 190: 
 191: if os.name == "nt":
 192:     class _FileTime(ctypes.Structure):
 193:         _fields_ = [("low", ctypes.c_uint32), ("high", ctypes.c_uint32)]
 194: 
 195:     def _filetime_value(value) -> int:
 196:         return (value.high << 32) | value.low
 197: 
 198:     def _system_times():
 199:         idle = _FileTime()
 200:         kernel = _FileTime()
 201:         user = _FileTime()
 202:         ok = ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetSystemTimes(
 203:             ctypes.byref(idle), ctypes.byref(kernel), ctypes.byref(user)
 204:         )
 205:         if not ok:
 206:             return None
 207:         return _filetime_value(idle), _filetime_value(kernel), _filetime_value(user)
 208: else:
 209:     def _system_times():
 210:         return None
 211: 
 212: 
 213: class SystemCpuSampler:
 214:     def __init__(self):
 215:         self.previous = _system_times()
 216: 
 217:     def sample(self):
 218:         current = _system_times()
 219:         previous = self.previous
 220:         self.previous = current
 221:         if current is None or previous is None:
 222:             return None
 223:         idle_delta = current[0] - previous[0]
 224:         total_delta = (current[1] - previous[1]) + (current[2] - previous[2])
 225:         if total_delta <= 0:
 226:             return None
 227:         return max(0.0, min(100.0, (total_delta - idle_delta) * 100.0 / total_delta))
 228: 
 229: 
 230: class HttpClient:
 231:     def __init__(self, base_url: str):
 232:         parsed = urlsplit(base_url)
 233:         if parsed.scheme != "http" or not parsed.hostname:
 234:             raise ValueError("This runner expects an http:// gateway URL")
 235:         self.host = parsed.hostname
 236:         self.port = parsed.port or 80
 237:         self.local = threading.local()
 238: 
 239:     def _connection(self):
 240:         connection = getattr(self.local, "connection", None)
 241:         last_used = getattr(self.local, "last_used", None)
 242:         if (
 243:             connection is not None
 244:             and last_used is not None
 245:             and time.monotonic() - last_used > HTTP_KEEPALIVE_IDLE_SECONDS
 246:         ):
 247:             connection.close()
 248:             connection = None
 249:         if connection is None:
 250:             connection = http.client.HTTPConnection(self.host, self.port, timeout=20)
 251:             self.local.connection = connection
 252:         return connection
 253: 
 254:     def request_sync(self, method: str, path: str, body=None, headers=None) -> dict:
 255:         connection = self._connection()
 256:         started = time.perf_counter()
 257:         started_utc = utc_now()
 258:         try:
 259:             connection.request(method, path, body=body, headers=headers or {})
 260:             response = connection.getresponse()
 261:             payload = response.read()
 262:             completed = time.perf_counter()
 263:             self.local.last_used = time.monotonic()
 264:             try:
 265:                 body_json = json.loads(payload) if payload else None
 266:             except json.JSONDecodeError:
 267:                 body_json = None
 268:             return {
 269:                 "status": response.status,
 270:                 "body": body_json,
 271:                 "body_text": payload[:2048].decode("utf-8", errors="replace"),
 272:                 "started_utc": started_utc,
 273:                 "completed_utc": utc_now(),
 274:                 "_started_perf": started,
 275:                 "_completed_perf": completed,
 276:                 "latency_ms": (completed - started) * 1000.0,
 277:                 "error": None,
 278:             }
 279:         except (OSError, http.client.HTTPException, TimeoutError) as exc:
 280:             try:
 281:                 connection.close()
 282:             finally:
 283:                 self.local.connection = None
 284:                 self.local.last_used = time.monotonic()
 285:             completed = time.perf_counter()
 286:             return {
 287:                 "status": None,
 288:                 "body": None,
 289:                 "body_text": "",
 290:                 "started_utc": started_utc,
 291:                 "completed_utc": utc_now(),
 292:                 "_started_perf": started,
 293:                 "_completed_perf": completed,
 294:                 "latency_ms": (completed - started) * 1000.0,
 295:                 "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
 296:             }
 297: 
 298: 
 299: def multipart_body(payload: bytes, boundary: str) -> bytes:
 300:     return (
 301:         f"--{boundary}\r\n"
 302:         'Content-Disposition: form-data; name="file"; filename="verification.bin"\r\n'
 303:         "Content-Type: application/octet-stream\r\n\r\n"
 304:     ).encode() + payload + f"\r\n--{boundary}--\r\n".encode()
 305:

 307: def request_path(kind: str, model_name: str, version: str | None = None) -> str:
 308:     if kind == "registry":
 309:         return f"/v1/models/{model_name}?version=v1"
 310:     path = f"/v1/infer/{model_name}"
 311:     if version is not None:
 312:         path += f"?version={quote(version)}"
 313:     return path

 315: 
 316: def model_version(result: dict) -> str | None:
 317:     body = result.get("body")
 318:     if isinstance(body, dict):
 319:         return body.get("version")
 320:     return None
 321: 
 322: 
 323: def request_record(index: int, scheduled_perf: float, key_slot: int | None, result: dict) -> dict:
 324:     record = {
 325:         "index": index,
 326:         "key_slot": key_slot,
 327:         "scheduled_utc": None,
 328:         "request_started_utc": result["started_utc"],
 329:         "request_completed_utc": result["completed_utc"],
 330:         "latency_ms": result["latency_ms"],
 331:         "status": result["status"],
 332:         "response_version": model_version(result),
 333:         "job_id": result["body"].get("job_id") if isinstance(result.get("body"), dict) else None,
 334:         "response_body": result["body"] if result.get("body") is not None else result["body_text"],
 335:         "error": result["error"],
 336:         "_started_perf": result["_started_perf"],
 337:         "_completed_perf": result["_completed_perf"],
 338:         "_scheduled_perf": scheduled_perf,
 339:     }
 340:     record["schedule_lag_ms"] = (result["_started_perf"] - scheduled_perf) * 1000.0
 341:     return record
 342: 
 343: 
 344: def public_record(record: dict) -> dict:
 345:     return {key: value for key, value in record.items() if not key.startswith("_")}
 346: 
 347: 
 348: def summarize_records(records: list[dict]) -> dict:
 349:     histogram = collections.Counter(
 350:         str(row["status"]) if row["status"] is not None else "NETWORK_ERROR" for row in records
 351:     )
 352:     successful = [row["latency_ms"] for row in records if row["status"] == 200]
 353:     return {
 354:         "scheduled_requests": len(records),
 355:         "status_histogram": dict(sorted(histogram.items())),
 356:         "http_200_percent": (histogram.get("200", 0) * 100.0 / len(records)) if records else 0.0,
 357:         "valid_99_percent_rule": bool(records) and histogram.get("200", 0) / len(records) >= 0.99,
 358:         "latency_successful_http_200_ms": latency_summary(successful),
 359:         "schedule_lag_ms": latency_summary([row["schedule_lag_ms"] for row in records]),
 360:         "observed_rate_per_second": (
 361:             len(records) / max(
 362:                 1e-9,
 363:                 max(row["_started_perf"] for row in records)
 364:                 - min(row["_started_perf"] for row in records),
 365:             )
 366:         ) if len(records) > 1 else None,
 367:     }
 368: 
 369: 
 370: def parse_xrange_lines(output: str) -> list[tuple[str, dict[str, str]]]:
 371:     lines = [line for line in output.splitlines() if line != ""]
 372:     entries = []
 373:     index = 0
 374:     while index < len(lines):
 375:         entry_id = lines[index]
 376:         index += 1
 377:         fields = {}
 378:         while index + 1 < len(lines) and "-" not in lines[index]:
 379:             fields[lines[index]] = lines[index + 1]
 380:             index += 2
 381:         entries.append((entry_id, fields))
 382:     return entries

 384: 
 385: def cleanup_queue(model_names: set[str], api_key_ids: list[str], output_path: Path | None = None) -> dict:
 386:     output = redis_command("XRANGE", "inference_jobs", "-", "+")
 387:     matched = [
 388:         (entry_id, fields)
 389:         for entry_id, fields in parse_xrange_lines(output)
 390:         if fields.get("model_name") in model_names
 391:     ]
 392:     job_ids = sorted({fields["job_id"] for _, fields in matched if fields.get("job_id")})
 393:     for start in range(0, len(matched), 200):
 394:         batch = matched[start : start + 200]
 395:         redis_command("XDEL", "inference_jobs", *(entry_id for entry_id, _ in batch))
 396:     job_keys = []
 397:     for job_id in job_ids:
 398:         job_keys.extend(
 399:             [
 400:                 f"payload:{job_id}",
 401:                 f"result:{job_id}",
 402:                 f"job:{job_id}:status",
 403:                 f"job:{job_id}:owner",
 404:             ]
 405:         )
 406:     for start in range(0, len(job_keys), 400):
 407:         redis_command("DEL", *job_keys[start : start + 400])
 408:     rate_keys = [f"rl:{key_id}" for key_id in api_key_ids]
 409:     for start in range(0, len(rate_keys), 400):
 410:         redis_command("DEL", *rate_keys[start : start + 400])
 411:     stream_length = int(redis_command("XLEN", "inference_jobs") or 0)
 412:     if stream_length == 0:
 413:         exists = int(redis_command("EXISTS", "inference_jobs") or 0)
 414:         if exists:
 415:             groups_output = redis_command("XINFO", "GROUPS", "inference_jobs")
 416:             if not groups_output:
 417:                 redis_command("DEL", "inference_jobs")
 418:     result = {
 419:         "matched_stream_entries_deleted": len(matched),
 420:         "job_ids_found": len(job_ids),
 421:         "job_keys_deleted": len(job_keys),
 422:         "rate_limit_keys_deleted": len(rate_keys),
 423:         "redis_dbsize_after": int(redis_command("DBSIZE") or 0),
 424:     }
 425:     if output_path:
 426:         write_json(output_path, result)
 427:     return result
 428: 
 429: 
 430: async def sample_resources(path: Path, stop_event: asyncio.Event, abort_event: asyncio.Event, memory_cap: int):
 431:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 432:     cpu = SystemCpuSampler()
 433:     headers = [
 434:         "timestamp_utc",
 435:         "host_cpu_percent",
 436:         "gateway_cpu_percent",
 437:         "gateway_memory_bytes",
 438:         "redis_container_cpu_percent",
 439:         "redis_container_memory_bytes",
 440:         "postgres_container_cpu_percent",
 441:         "postgres_container_memory_bytes",
 442:         "doc_ocr_worker_cpu_percent",
 443:         "doc_ocr_worker_memory_bytes",
 444:         "asr_worker_cpu_percent",
 445:         "asr_worker_memory_bytes",
 446:         "redis_used_memory_bytes",
 447:         "redis_maxmemory_bytes",
 448:         "redis_maxmemory_policy",
 449:         "redis_dbsize",
 450:         "safety_cap_bytes",
 451:     ]
 452:     with path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as output:
 453:         writer = csv.DictWriter(output, fieldnames=headers)
 454:         writer.writeheader()
 455:         while not stop_event.is_set():
 456:             try:
 457:                 docker_stats, memory = await asyncio.gather(
 458:                     asyncio.to_thread(parse_docker_stats),
 459:                     asyncio.to_thread(redis_snapshot),
 460:                 )
 461:                 gateway = docker_stats.get("verify-registry-gateway", {})
 462:                 redis = docker_stats.get("modelmesh-redis-1", {})
 463:                 postgres = docker_stats.get("modelmesh-postgres-1", {})
 464:                 doc_ocr_worker = docker_stats.get("modelmesh-doc-ocr-worker-1", {})
 465:                 asr_worker = docker_stats.get("modelmesh-asr-worker-1", {})
 466:                 row = {
 467:                     "timestamp_utc": utc_now(),
 468:                     "host_cpu_percent": cpu.sample(),
 469:                     "gateway_cpu_percent": gateway.get("cpu_percent"),
 470:                     "gateway_memory_bytes": gateway.get("memory_bytes"),
 471:                     "redis_container_cpu_percent": redis.get("cpu_percent"),
 472:                     "redis_container_memory_bytes": redis.get("memory_bytes"),
 473:                     "postgres_container_cpu_percent": postgres.get("cpu_percent"),
 474:                     "postgres_container_memory_bytes": postgres.get("memory_bytes"),
 475:                     "doc_ocr_worker_cpu_percent": doc_ocr_worker.get("cpu_percent"),
 476:                     "doc_ocr_worker_memory_bytes": doc_ocr_worker.get("memory_bytes"),
 477:                     "asr_worker_cpu_percent": asr_worker.get("cpu_percent"),
 478:                     "asr_worker_memory_bytes": asr_worker.get("memory_bytes"),
 479:                     "redis_used_memory_bytes": memory["used_memory_bytes"],
 480:                     "redis_maxmemory_bytes": memory["maxmemory_bytes"],
 481:                     "redis_maxmemory_policy": memory["maxmemory_policy"],
 482:                     "redis_dbsize": memory["dbsize"],
 483:                     "safety_cap_bytes": memory_cap,
 484:                 }
 485:                 writer.writerow(row)
 486:                 output.flush()
 487:                 if memory["used_memory_bytes"] >= memory_cap:
 488:                     abort_event.set()
 489:                     writer.writerow(
 490:                         {
 491:                             "timestamp_utc": utc_now(),
 492:                             "redis_used_memory_bytes": memory["used_memory_bytes"],
 493:                             "safety_cap_bytes": memory_cap,
 494:                         }
 495:                     )
 496:                     output.flush()
 497:                     return
 498:             except Exception as exc:
 499:                 writer.writerow(
 500:                     {
 501:                         "timestamp_utc": utc_now(),
 502:                         "redis_maxmemory_policy": f"SAMPLER_ERROR: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
 503:                         "safety_cap_bytes": memory_cap,
 504:                     }
 505:                 )
 506:                 output.flush()
 507:                 raise
 508:             try:
 509:                 await asyncio.wait_for(stop_event.wait(), timeout=1.0)
 510:             except asyncio.TimeoutError:
 511:                 pass
 512: 
 513: 
 514: async def scheduled_load(
 515:     *,
 516:     client: HttpClient,
 517:     output_dir: Path,
 518:     label: str,
 519:     kind: str,
 520:     model_name: str,
 521:     keys: list[str],
 522:     rate: float,
 523:     duration: float,
 524:     payload: bytes,
 525:     version: str | None = None,
 526:     sample: bool = True,
 527:     memory_cap: int = REDIS_MEMORY_SAFETY_BYTES,
 528: ) -> dict:
 529:     output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 530:     request_path_value = request_path(kind, model_name, version)
 531:     boundary = "modelmesh-verification-boundary"
 532:     body = multipart_body(payload, boundary) if kind == "enqueue" else None
 533:     headers = {}
 534:     if kind == "enqueue":
 535:         headers = {"Content-Type": f"multipart/form-data; boundary={boundary}"}
 536: 
 537:     started_utc = utc_now()
 538:     start_perf = time.perf_counter()
 539:     start_wall = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
 540:     stop_sampling = asyncio.Event()
 541:     abort_scheduling = asyncio.Event()
 542:     sampler = None
 543:     if sample:
 544:         sampler = asyncio.create_task(
 545:             sample_resources(output_dir / "resource_samples.csv", stop_sampling, abort_scheduling, memory_cap)
 546:         )
 547:         await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
 548: 
 549:     semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(256)
 550:     tasks = []
 551: 
 552:     async def request_one(index: int, scheduled: float):
 553:         slot = index % len(keys) if kind == "enqueue" and keys else None
 554:         request_headers = dict(headers)
 555:         if slot is not None:
 556:             request_headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {keys[slot]}"
 557:         async with semaphore:
 558:             result = await asyncio.to_thread(
 559:                 client.request_sync,
 560:                 "POST" if kind == "enqueue" else "GET",
 561:                 request_path_value,
 562:                 body,
 563:                 request_headers,
 564:             )
 565:         record = request_record(index, scheduled, slot, result)
 566:         scheduled_at = start_wall.timestamp() + (scheduled - start_perf)
 567:         record["scheduled_utc"] = (
 568:             datetime.fromtimestamp(scheduled_at, timezone.utc)
 569:             .isoformat()
 570:             .replace("+00:00", "Z")
 571:         )
 572:         record["requested_version"] = version
 573:         return record
 574: 
 575:     planned_count = math.ceil(rate * duration)
 576:     for index in range(planned_count):
 577:         target = start_perf + index / rate
 578:         delay = target - time.perf_counter()
 579:         if delay > 0:
 580:             await asyncio.sleep(delay)
 581:         if abort_scheduling.is_set():
 582:             break
 583:         tasks.append(asyncio.create_task(request_one(index, target)))
 584: 
 585:     records = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)
 586:     stop_sampling.set()
 587:     if sampler:
 588:         await sampler
 589:     ended_utc = utc_now()
 590:     summary = summarize_records(records)
 591:     summary.update(
 592:         {
 593:             "label": label,
 594:             "kind": kind,
 595:             "model_name": model_name,
 596:             "requested_version": version,
 597:             "target_rate_per_second": rate,
 598:             "duration_seconds": duration,
 599:             "started_at_utc": started_utc,
 600:             "ended_at_utc": ended_utc,
 601:             "payload_bytes": len(payload) if kind == "enqueue" else None,
 602:             "aborted_for_redis_memory_safety": abort_scheduling.is_set(),
 603:             "status_codes_are_valid": summary["valid_99_percent_rule"] and not abort_scheduling.is_set(),
 604:             "redis_after_measurement_before_cleanup": redis_snapshot(),
 605:         }
 606:     )
 607:     append_jsonl(output_dir / "requests.jsonl", (public_record(row) for row in records))
 608:     write_json(output_dir / "summary.json", summary)
 609:     return {"summary": summary, "records": records}
 610: 
 611: 
 612: def response_json(client: HttpClient, method: str, path: str, payload=None, bearer=None) -> dict:
 613:     body = json.dumps(payload).encode() if payload is not None else None
 614:     headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"} if body is not None else {}
 615:     if bearer:
 616:         headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {bearer}"
 617:     return client.request_sync(method, path, body, headers)

 620: def assert_status(result: dict, expected: int, label: str):
 621:     if result["status"] != expected:
 622:         raise RuntimeError(
 623:             f"{label}: expected HTTP {expected}, got {result['status']}: {result.get('body_text')}"
 624:         )

 627: def register_model(client: HttpClient, admin_key: str, model: dict) -> str:
 628:     result = response_json(client, "POST", "/v1/models", model, admin_key)
 629:     assert_status(result, 200, f"register {model['name']}:{model['version']}")
 630:     return result["body"]["id"]
 631: 
 632: 
 633: def patch_model(client: HttpClient, admin_key: str, model_id: str, patch: dict) -> dict:
 634:     result = response_json(client, "PATCH", f"/v1/models/{model_id}", patch, admin_key)
 635:     assert_status(result, 200, f"patch model {model_id}")
 636:     return result
 637: 
 638: 
 639: def check_binomial_chi_square(successes: int, total: int, probability: float) -> dict:
 640:     if probability <= 0.0 or probability >= 1.0:
 641:         return {"chi_square": None, "df": 1, "p_value": None, "expected": None}
 642:     expected_yes = total * probability
 643:     expected_no = total * (1.0 - probability)
 644:     failures = total - successes
 645:     statistic = (successes - expected_yes) ** 2 / expected_yes + (failures - expected_no) ** 2 / expected_no
 646:     p_value = math.erfc(math.sqrt(statistic / 2.0))
 647:     return {
 648:         "chi_square": statistic,
 649:         "df": 1,
 650:         "p_value": p_value,
 651:         "expected": {"canary": expected_yes, "stable": expected_no},
 652:         "interpretation_alpha_0_05": "consistent_with_expected" if p_value >= 0.05 else "deviation_at_0.05",
 653:     }
 654: 
 655: 
 656: def summarize_versions(records: list[dict]) -> dict:
 657:     successful_count = sum(row["status"] == 200 for row in records)
 658:     counts = collections.Counter(
 659:         row["response_version"] for row in records if row["status"] == 200 and row.get("response_version")
 660:     )
 661:     return {
 662:         "version_histogram": dict(sorted(counts.items())),
 663:         "status_histogram": dict(
 664:             sorted(collections.Counter(
 665:                 str(row["status"]) if row["status"] is not None else "NETWORK_ERROR" for row in records
 666:             ).items())
 667:         ),
 668:         "total_requests": len(records),
 669:         "successful_http_200_requests": successful_count,
 670:         "latency_successful_http_200_ms": latency_summary(
 671:             [row["latency_ms"] for row in records if row["status"] == 200]
 672:         ),
 673:     }
 674: 
 675: 
 676: def parse_ws_frame(reader: asyncio.StreamReader, writer: asyncio.StreamWriter):
 677:     async def read():
 678:         while True:
 679:             first, second = await reader.readexactly(2)
 680:             opcode = first & 0x0F
 681:             length = second & 0x7F
 682:             if length == 126:
 683:                 length = struct.unpack("!H", await reader.readexactly(2))[0]
 684:             elif length == 127:
 685:                 length = struct.unpack("!Q", await reader.readexactly(8))[0]
 686:             mask = await reader.readexactly(4) if second & 0x80 else None
 687:             payload = await reader.readexactly(length)
 688:             if mask:
 689:                 payload = bytes(byte ^ mask[index % 4] for index, byte in enumerate(payload))
 690:             if opcode == 0x9:
 691:                 writer.write(encode_ws_frame(payload, 0xA))
 692:                 await writer.drain()
 693:                 continue
 694:             if opcode in (0x1, 0x2):
 695:                 return opcode, payload
 696:             if opcode == 0x8:
 697:                 raise ConnectionError("WebSocket closed before result")
 698:     return read()
 699: 
 700: 
 701: def encode_ws_frame(payload: bytes, opcode: int) -> bytes:
 702:     mask = secrets.token_bytes(4)
 703:     length = len(payload)
 704:     if length < 126:
 705:         header = bytes([0x80 | opcode, 0x80 | length])
 706:     elif length <= 0xFFFF:
 707:         header = bytes([0x80 | opcode, 0x80 | 126]) + struct.pack("!H", length)
 708:     else:
 709:         header = bytes([0x80 | opcode, 0x80 | 127]) + struct.pack("!Q", length)
 710:     masked = bytes(byte ^ mask[index % 4] for index, byte in enumerate(payload))
 711:     return header + mask + masked
 712: 
 713: 
 714: async def websocket_probe(base_url: str, api_key: str, send_payload: bytes | None):
 715:     parsed = urlsplit(base_url)
 716:     reader, writer = await asyncio.open_connection(parsed.hostname, parsed.port or 80)
 717:     key = base64.b64encode(secrets.token_bytes(16)).decode("ascii")
 718:     path = f"/v1/ws/infer/bench-ws?api_key={quote(api_key)}"
 719:     request = (
 720:         f"GET {path} HTTP/1.1\r\n"
 721:         f"Host: {parsed.hostname}:{parsed.port or 80}\r\n"
 722:         "Upgrade: websocket\r\n"
 723:         "Connection: Upgrade\r\n"
 724:         f"Sec-WebSocket-Key: {key}\r\n"
 725:         "Sec-WebSocket-Version: 13\r\n\r\n"
 726:     )
 727:     started = time.perf_counter()
 728:     started_utc = utc_now()
 729:     writer.write(request.encode("ascii"))
 730:     await writer.drain()
 731:     response_headers = await reader.readuntil(b"\r\n\r\n")
 732:     status_line = response_headers.split(b"\r\n", 1)[0].decode("ascii", errors="replace")
 733:     if " 101 " not in status_line:
 734:         writer.close()
 735:         await writer.wait_closed()
 736:         raise RuntimeError(f"WebSocket upgrade failed: {status_line}")
 737:     opcode, ready_bytes = await parse_ws_frame(reader, writer)
 738:     ready = json.loads(ready_bytes) if opcode == 0x1 else {"binary_ready": True}
 739:     result_message = None
 740:     if send_payload is not None:
 741:         writer.write(encode_ws_frame(send_payload, 0x2))
 742:         await writer.drain()
 743:         opcode, result_bytes = await asyncio.wait_for(parse_ws_frame(reader, writer), timeout=10)
 744:         result_message = json.loads(result_bytes) if opcode == 0x1 else {"binary_result": True}
 745:     completed = time.perf_counter()
 746:     writer.close()
 747:     await writer.wait_closed()
 748:     return {
 749:         "started_utc": started_utc,
 750:         "completed_utc": utc_now(),
 751:         "latency_ms": (completed - started) * 1000.0,
 752:         "ready": ready,
 753:         "result": result_message,
 754:         "_started_perf": started,
 755:         "_completed_perf": completed,
 756:     }
 757:

 805:     def run_sql(self, sql: str, database: str) -> str:
 806:         result = subprocess.run(
 807:             [
 808:                 "docker", "compose", "exec", "-T", "postgres", "psql",
 809:                 "-U", "user", "-d", database, "-v", "ON_ERROR_STOP=1", "-Atc", sql,
 810:             ],
 811:             cwd=WORKTREE_ROOT,
 812:             capture_output=True,
 813:             text=True,
 814:             timeout=30,
 815:             check=False,
 816:         )
 817:         if result.returncode:
 818:             raise RuntimeError(f"psql command failed: {result.stderr.strip()}")
 819:         return result.stdout.strip()

 821:     def setup_identity(self):
 822:         email = f"verify-registry-{uuid.uuid4().hex}@example.com"
 823:         password = secrets.token_urlsafe(24)
 824:         registered = response_json(
 825:             self.client, "POST", "/v1/auth/register", {"email": email, "password": password}
 826:         )
 827:         assert_status(registered, 200, "register scratch user")
 828:         login = response_json(
 829:             self.client, "POST", "/v1/auth/login", {"email": email, "password": password}
 830:         )
 831:         assert_status(login, 200, "login scratch user")
 832:         token = login["body"]["access_token"]
 833:         for _ in range(API_KEY_COUNT):
 834:             key_response = response_json(
 835:                 self.client,
 836:                 "POST",
 837:                 "/v1/auth/api-keys",
 838:                 {"rate_limit_per_min": API_KEY_RATE_LIMIT},
 839:                 token,
 840:             )
 841:             assert_status(key_response, 200, "create benchmark API key")
 842:             if key_response["body"]["rate_limit_per_min"] != API_KEY_RATE_LIMIT:
 843:                 raise RuntimeError("API returned an unexpected rate limit")
 844:             self.api_keys.append(key_response["body"]["key"])
 845:         hashes = [hashlib.sha256(value.encode()).hexdigest() for value in self.api_keys]
 846:         ids_query = (
 847:             "SELECT id::text || '|' || key_hash FROM api_keys WHERE key_hash IN ("
 848:             + ",".join(f"'{value}'" for value in hashes)
 849:             + ")"
 850:         )
 851:         rows = [line.split("|", 1) for line in self.run_sql(ids_query, self.args.database_name).splitlines()]
 852:         by_hash = {key_hash: key_id for key_id, key_hash in rows}
 853:         if len(by_hash) != API_KEY_COUNT or any(value not in by_hash for value in hashes):
 854:             raise RuntimeError(f"Expected {API_KEY_COUNT} distinct API key rows; found {len(by_hash)}")
 855:         self.api_key_ids = [by_hash[value] for value in hashes]
 856:         self.admin_key = self.api_keys[0]
 857:         admin_hash = hashes[0]
 858:         self.run_sql(
 859:             f"UPDATE api_keys SET is_admin = TRUE WHERE key_hash = '{admin_hash}'",
 860:             self.args.database_name,
 861:         )
 862:         is_admin = self.run_sql(
 863:             f"SELECT is_admin FROM api_keys WHERE key_hash = '{admin_hash}'",
 864:             self.args.database_name,
 865:         )
 866:         if is_admin.lower() not in {"t", "true"}:
 867:             raise RuntimeError("Scratch API key was not promoted to admin")
 868:         write_json(
 869:             self.raw_root / "setup" / "identity.json",
 870:             {
 871:                 "registered": registered["body"],
 872:                 "login_status": login["status"],
 873:                 "api_key_count": len(self.api_keys),
 874:                 "api_key_rate_limit_per_min": API_KEY_RATE_LIMIT,
 875:                 "api_key_ids": self.api_key_ids,
 876:                 "admin_key_slot": 0,
 877:                 "raw_keys_written": False,
 878:             },
 879:         )

 920:     async def run_part_a(self):
 921:         part_dir = self.raw_root / "part_a"
 922:         part_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 923:         payload = b"x" * PAYLOAD_BYTES
 924:         (part_dir / "payload.bin").write_bytes(payload)
 925:         payload_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest()
 926:         plan = []
 927:         for repetition in range(1, self.args.repetitions + 1):
 928:             for rate_index, rate in enumerate(self.args.rates):
 929:                 pair = [
 930:                     ("enqueue", "bench-null"),
 931:                     ("registry", "bench-null"),
 932:                 ]
 933:                 if (repetition + rate_index) % 2:
 934:                     pair.reverse()
 935:                 plan.extend((repetition, rate, kind, name) for kind, name in pair)
 936:         write_json(part_dir / "run_order.json", {"order": plan, "pair_order_alternates": True})
 937:         summaries = []
 938:         pooled = collections.defaultdict(
 939:             lambda: {
 940:                 "latencies": [],
 941:                 "run_medians": [],
 942:                 "status_histogram": collections.Counter(),
 943:                 "resource_rows": [],
 944:             }
 945:         )
 946:         stop_campaign = False
 947:         for repetition, rate, kind, model_name in plan:
 948:             if stop_campaign:
 949:                 break
 950:             label = f"{kind}_rate_{int(rate)}_rep_{repetition}"
 951:             output_dir = part_dir / label
 952:             baseline = redis_snapshot()
 953:             result = await scheduled_load(
 954:                 client=self.client,
 955:                 output_dir=output_dir,
 956:                 label=label,
 957:                 kind=kind,
 958:                 model_name=model_name,
 959:                 keys=self.api_keys,
 960:                 rate=rate,
 961:                 duration=self.args.duration,
 962:                 payload=payload,
 963:                 sample=True,
 964:                 memory_cap=self.args.memory_safety_bytes,
 965:             )
 966:             summary = result["summary"]
 967:             summary["redis_before_run"] = baseline
 968:             summary["payload_sha256"] = payload_sha256
 969:             resource_rows = []
 970:             resource_file = output_dir / "resource_samples.csv"
 971:             if resource_file.exists():
 972:                 with resource_file.open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as source:
 973:                     resource_rows = list(csv.DictReader(source))
 974:             resource_fields = (
 975:                 "host_cpu_percent",
 976:                 "gateway_cpu_percent",
 977:                 "redis_container_cpu_percent",
 978:                 "postgres_container_cpu_percent",
 979:                 "redis_used_memory_bytes",
 980:                 "gateway_memory_bytes",
 981:                 "redis_container_memory_bytes",
 982:                 "postgres_container_memory_bytes",
 983:                 "doc_ocr_worker_cpu_percent",
 984:                 "doc_ocr_worker_memory_bytes",
 985:                 "asr_worker_cpu_percent",
 986:                 "asr_worker_memory_bytes",
 987:             )
 988:             summary["resource_metrics"] = {}
 989:             for field in resource_fields:
 990:                 values = []
 991:                 for row in resource_rows:
 992:                     try:
 993:                         if row.get(field) not in (None, ""):
 994:                             values.append(float(row[field]))
 995:                     except ValueError:
 996:                         continue
 997:                 summary["resource_metrics"][field] = {
 998:                     "n": len(values),
 999:                     "mean": statistics.mean(values) if values else None,
1000:                     "p95": quantile(values, 0.95),
1001:                     "max": max(values) if values else None,
1002:                     "spread": max(values) - min(values) if values else None,
1003:                 }
1004:             group = pooled[(kind, rate)]
1005:             group["status_histogram"].update(summary["status_histogram"])
1006:             group["safety_aborted"] = (
1007:                 group.get("safety_aborted", False)
1008:                 or summary["aborted_for_redis_memory_safety"]
1009:             )
1010:             group["latencies"].extend(
1011:                 row["latency_ms"] for row in result["records"] if row["status"] == 200
1012:             )
1013:             run_median = summary["latency_successful_http_200_ms"]["median_ms"]
1014:             if run_median is not None:
1015:                 group["run_medians"].append(run_median)
1016:             group["resource_rows"].extend(resource_rows)
1017:             summary["cleanup"] = cleanup_queue(
1018:                 {"bench-null"}, self.api_key_ids, output_dir / "cleanup.json"
1019:             )
1020:             summary["redis_after_cleanup"] = redis_snapshot()
1021:             summaries.append(summary)
1022:             write_json(output_dir / "summary.json", summary)
1023:             append_jsonl(part_dir / "runs.jsonl", [summary])
1024:             if summary["aborted_for_redis_memory_safety"]:
1025:                 stop_campaign = True
1026:         aggregates = []
1027:         for (kind, rate), data in sorted(pooled.items(), key=lambda item: (item[0][0], item[0][1])):
1028:             cpu_summary = {}
1029:             for field in (
1030:                 "host_cpu_percent",
1031:                 "gateway_cpu_percent",
1032:                 "redis_container_cpu_percent",
1033:                 "postgres_container_cpu_percent",
1034:                 "doc_ocr_worker_cpu_percent",
1035:                 "doc_ocr_worker_memory_bytes",
1036:                 "asr_worker_cpu_percent",
1037:                 "asr_worker_memory_bytes",
1038:             ):
1039:                 values = []
1040:                 for row in data["resource_rows"]:
1041:                     try:
1042:                         if row.get(field) not in (None, ""):
1043:                             values.append(float(row[field]))
1044:                     except ValueError:
1045:                         continue
1046:                 cpu_summary[field] = {
1047:                     "n": len(values),
1048:                     "mean": statistics.mean(values) if values else None,
1049:                     "p95": quantile(values, 0.95),
1050:                     "max": max(values) if values else None,
1051:                     "spread": max(values) - min(values) if values else None,
1052:                 }
1053:             status_total = sum(data["status_histogram"].values())
1054:             aggregates.append(
1055:                 {
1056:                     "kind": kind,
1057:                     "target_rate_per_second": rate,
1058:                     "independent_runs_completed": len(data["run_medians"]),
1059:                     "pooled_successful_http_200_latency_ms": latency_summary(data["latencies"]),
1060:                     "run_median_distribution_ms": latency_summary(data["run_medians"]),
1061:                     "status_histogram": dict(sorted(data["status_histogram"].items())),
1062:                     "valid_99_percent_rule": (
1063:                         status_total > 0
1064:                         and data["status_histogram"].get("200", 0) / status_total >= 0.99
1065:                         and not data.get("safety_aborted", False)
1066:                     ),
1067:                     "cpu_samples": cpu_summary,
1068:                 }
1069:             )
1070:         write_json(
1071:             part_dir / "summary.json",
1072:             {
1073:                 "started_at_utc": summaries[0]["started_at_utc"] if summaries else None,
1074:                 "ended_at_utc": summaries[-1]["ended_at_utc"] if summaries else None,
1075:                 "runs": summaries,
1076:                 "aggregates_by_kind_and_rate": aggregates,
1077:                 "campaign_stopped_after_safety_abort": stop_campaign,
1078:             },
1079:         )
1080:         return summaries

1122:             "name": "bench-canary", "version": "v9", "worker_name": "nobody",
1123:             "protocol": "http", "endpoint": "http://nobody.invalid:9/infer",
1124:             "supports_streaming": False, "canary_percent": 0, "description": "verification middle version",
1125:         }
1126:         v10 = {
1127:             "name": "bench-canary", "version": "v10", "worker_name": "nobody",
1128:             "protocol": "http", "endpoint": "http://nobody.invalid:9/infer",
1129:             "supports_streaming": False, "canary_percent": 50, "description": "verification highest version",
1130:         }
1131:         self.models["bench-canary:v9"] = register_model(self.client, self.admin_key, v9)
1132:         self.models["bench-canary:v10"] = register_model(self.client, self.admin_key, v10)
1133:         self.model_definitions["bench-canary:v9"] = v9
1134:         self.model_definitions["bench-canary:v10"] = v10
1135:         write_json(
1136:             part_dir / "registered_versions.json",
1137:             {
1138:                 key: {"id": self.models[key], "definition": self.model_definitions[key]}
1139:                 for key in ("bench-canary:v1", "bench-canary:v2", "bench-canary:v9", "bench-canary:v10")
1140:             },
1141:         )
1142:         multi = await scheduled_load(
1143:             client=self.client,
1144:             output_dir=part_dir / "four_versions_percent_50",
1145:             label="four_versions_percent_50",
1146:             kind="enqueue",
1147:             model_name="bench-canary",
1148:             keys=self.api_keys,
1149:             rate=self.args.canary_rate,
1150:             duration=self.args.canary_samples / self.args.canary_rate,
1151:             payload=payload,
1152:             sample=False,
1153:         )
1154:         versions = summarize_versions(multi["records"])
1155:         multi_summary = {
1156:             **multi["summary"],
1157:             **versions,
1158:             "stable_version": "v1",
1159:             "canary_version": "v10",
1160:             "middle_versions_expected_zero": ["v2", "v9"],
1161:             "middle_versions_observed": {
1162:                 version: versions["version_histogram"].get(version, 0) for version in ("v2", "v9")
1163:             },
1164:             "chi_square_test_stable_vs_canary": check_binomial_chi_square(
1165:                 versions["version_histogram"].get("v10", 0),
1166:                 versions["successful_http_200_requests"],
1167:                 0.5,
1168:             ),
1169:         }
1170:         multi_summary["cleanup"] = cleanup_queue({"bench-canary"}, self.api_key_ids)
1171:         write_json(part_dir / "four_versions_percent_50" / "summary.json", multi_summary)
1172:         summaries.append(multi_summary)
1173: 
1174:         pin_results = {}
1175:         for version in ("v1", "v2", "v9", "v10"):
1176:             pin = await scheduled_load(
1177:                 client=self.client,
1178:                 output_dir=part_dir / f"pinned_{version}",
1179:                 label=f"pinned_{version}",
1180:                 kind="enqueue",
1181:                 model_name="bench-canary",
1182:                 keys=self.api_keys,
1183:                 rate=self.args.canary_rate,
1184:                 duration=50 / self.args.canary_rate,
1185:                 payload=payload,
1186:                 version=version,
1187:                 sample=False,
1188:             )
1189:             pin_counts = summarize_versions(pin["records"])
1190:             pin_results[version] = {
1191:                 **pin["summary"],
1192:                 **pin_counts,
1193:                 "all_responses_match_pin": (
1194:                     len(pin["records"]) > 0
1195:                     and all(
1196:                         row["status"] == 200 and row["response_version"] == version
1197:                         for row in pin["records"]
1198:                     )
1199:                 ),
1200:             }
1201:             pin_results[version]["cleanup"] = cleanup_queue({"bench-canary"}, self.api_key_ids)
1202:             write_json(part_dir / f"pinned_{version}" / "summary.json", pin_results[version])
1203:         write_json(
1204:             part_dir / "summary.json",
1205:             {"runs": summaries, "pinned_version_results": pin_results},
1206:         )
1207:         return summaries, pin_results
1208: 
1209:     async def wait_for_http_behavior(
1210:         self,
1211:         rows: list[dict],
1212:         start_after: float,
1213:         predicate,
1214:         timeout_seconds: float = 10.0,
1215:     ) -> dict:
1216:         deadline = time.perf_counter() + timeout_seconds
1217:         while time.perf_counter() < deadline:
1218:             candidates = [
1219:                 row for row in rows if row["_started_perf"] >= start_after and not row.get("_used_for_patch")
1220:             ]
1221:             for row in candidates:
1222:                 row["_used_for_patch"] = True
1223:                 if predicate(row):
1224:                     return row
1225:             await asyncio.sleep(0.002)
1226:         raise TimeoutError("No request observed the expected registry behavior after PATCH")
1227: 
1228:     async def continuous_http_loop(self, stop_event: asyncio.Event, records: list[dict]):
1229:         rate = HTTP_LOOP_RATE
1230:         start = time.perf_counter()
1231:         start_wall = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
1232:         index = 0
1233:         payload = b"phase-c-probe"
1234:         boundary = "modelmesh-phase-c-boundary"
1235:         body = multipart_body(payload, boundary)
1236:         while not stop_event.is_set():
1237:             target = start + index / rate
1238:             delay = target - time.perf_counter()
1239:             if delay > 0:
1240:                 try:
1241:                     await asyncio.wait_for(stop_event.wait(), timeout=delay)
1242:                     break
1243:                 except asyncio.TimeoutError:
1244:                     pass
1245:             if stop_event.is_set():
1246:                 break
1247:             slot = index % len(self.api_keys)
1248:             result = await asyncio.to_thread(
1249:                 self.client.request_sync,
1250:                 "POST",
1251:                 "/v1/infer/bench-canary",
1252:                 body,
1253:                 {
1254:                     "Content-Type": f"multipart/form-data; boundary={boundary}",
1255:                     "Authorization": f"Bearer {self.api_keys[slot]}",
1256:                 },
1257:             )
1258:             row = request_record(index, target, slot, result)
1259:             scheduled_at = start_wall.timestamp() + (target - start)
1260:             row["scheduled_utc"] = (
1261:                 datetime.fromtimestamp(scheduled_at, timezone.utc)
1262:                 .isoformat()
1263:                 .replace("+00:00", "Z")
1264:             )
1265:             records.append(row)
1266:             index += 1
1267: 
1268:     async def patch_and_observe(
1269:         self,
1270:         rows: list[dict],
1271:         patch_name: str,
1272:         model_id: str,
1273:         patch: dict,
1274:         predicate,
1275:         expected_behavior: str,
1276:     ) -> dict:
1277:         started_utc = utc_now()
1278:         response = await asyncio.to_thread(
1279:             response_json,
1280:             self.client,
1281:             "PATCH",
1282:             f"/v1/models/{model_id}",
1283:             patch,
1284:             self.admin_key,
1285:         )
1286:         response_perf = response["_completed_perf"]
1287:         assert_status(response, 200, f"PATCH {patch_name}")
1288:         first = await self.wait_for_http_behavior(rows, response_perf, predicate)
1289:         observation_delay_ms = (first["_completed_perf"] - response_perf) * 1000.0
1290:         return {
1291:             "name": patch_name,
1292:             "patch": patch,
1293:             "patch_started_at_utc": started_utc,
1294:             "patch_response_at_utc": response["completed_utc"],
1295:             "patch_latency_ms": response["latency_ms"],
1296:             "patch_response_latency_stats_ms": {
1297:                 "n": 1,
1298:                 "median_ms": response["latency_ms"],
1299:                 "p95_ms": response["latency_ms"],
1300:                 "spread_ms": 0.0,
1301:             },
1302:             "patch_status": response["status"],
1303:             "patch_response_body": response["body"],
1304:             "first_matching_request_index": first["index"],
1305:             "requests_started_after_patch_response_through_first_match": sum(
1306:                 1
1307:                 for row in rows
1308:                 if row["_started_perf"] >= response_perf and row["_started_perf"] <= first["_started_perf"]
1309:             ),
1310:             "first_matching_request_started_at_utc": first["request_started_utc"],
1311:             "first_matching_request_completed_at_utc": first["request_completed_utc"],
1312:             "first_matching_request_status": first["status"],
1313:             "first_matching_request_version": first["response_version"],
1314:             "expected_behavior": expected_behavior,
1315:             "patch_response_to_first_matching_request_ms": observation_delay_ms,
1316:             "patch_response_to_first_request_latency_stats": {
1317:                 "n": 1,
1318:                 "median_ms": observation_delay_ms,
1319:                 "p95_ms": observation_delay_ms,
1320:                 "spread_ms": 0.0,
1321:             },
1322:             "_patch_response_perf": response_perf,
1323:         }
1324: 
1325:     async def run_websocket_update(self, http_loop_rows: list[dict]) -> dict:
1326:         baseline = await websocket_probe(self.base_url, self.api_keys[0], None)
1327:         baseline.pop("_started_perf", None)
1328:         baseline.pop("_completed_perf", None)
1329:         started_utc = utc_now()
1330:         patch_response = await asyncio.to_thread(
1331:             response_json,
1332:             self.client,
1333:             "PATCH",
1334:             f"/v1/models/{self.models['bench-ws:v1']}",
1335:             {
1336:                 "protocol": "http",
1337:                 "endpoint": "http://verify-registry-endpoint:8765/probe/new",
1338:             },
1339:             self.admin_key,
1340:         )
1341:         assert_status(patch_response, 200, "PATCH WebSocket model protocol/endpoint")
1342:         patch_response_perf = patch_response["_completed_perf"]
1343: 
1344:         probe_records = []
1345:         first_new = None
1346:         start = time.perf_counter()
1347:         count = 40
1348:         for index in range(count):
1349:             target = start + index / HTTP_LOOP_RATE
1350:             delay = target - time.perf_counter()
1351:             if delay > 0:
1352:                 await asyncio.sleep(delay)
1353:             result = await websocket_probe(self.base_url, self.api_keys[index % len(self.api_keys)], b"ws-probe")
1354:             record = {
1355:                 "index": index,
1356:                 "started_utc": result["started_utc"],
1357:                 "completed_utc": result["completed_utc"],
1358:                 "latency_ms": result["latency_ms"],
1359:                 "ready": result["ready"],
1360:                 "result": result["result"],
1361:                 "_started_perf": result["_started_perf"],
1362:                 "_completed_perf": result["_completed_perf"],
1363:             }
1364:             probe_records.append(record)
1365:             if (
1366:                 first_new is None
1367:                 and result["_started_perf"] >= patch_response_perf
1368:                 and result["ready"].get("protocol") == "http"
1369:                 and isinstance(result["result"], dict)
1370:                 and isinstance(result["result"].get("result"), dict)
1371:                 and result["result"]["result"].get("endpoint_probe") == "/probe/new"
1372:             ):
1373:                 first_new = record
1374: 
1375:         endpoint_probe_logs = subprocess.run(
1376:             ["docker", "logs", "--timestamps", "verify-registry-endpoint"],
1377:             cwd=WORKTREE_ROOT,
1378:             capture_output=True,
1379:             text=True,
1380:             timeout=20,
1381:             check=False,
1382:         )
1383:         if endpoint_probe_logs.returncode:
1384:             raise RuntimeError(f"Could not capture endpoint probe logs: {endpoint_probe_logs.stderr}")
1385:         probe_dir = self.raw_root / "part_c" / "websocket_update"
1386:         probe_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
1387:         (probe_dir / "endpoint_probe.log").write_text(
1388:             endpoint_probe_logs.stdout + endpoint_probe_logs.stderr, encoding="utf-8"
1389:         )
1390:         append_jsonl(
1391:             probe_dir / "websocket_requests.jsonl",
1392:             (
1393:                 {
1394:                     key: value
1395:                     for key, value in row.items()
1396:                     if not key.startswith("_")
1397:                 }
1398:                 for row in probe_records
1399:             ),
1400:         )
1401:         if first_new is None:
1402:             raise RuntimeError("No WebSocket request observed the patched protocol and endpoint")
1403:         delay_ms = (first_new["_completed_perf"] - patch_response_perf) * 1000.0
1404:         return {
1405:             "baseline_websocket_ready": baseline,
1406:             "baseline_websocket_latency_stats_ms": {
1407:                 "n": 1,
1408:                 "median_ms": baseline["latency_ms"],
1409:                 "p95_ms": baseline["latency_ms"],
1410:                 "spread_ms": 0.0,
1411:             },
1412:             "patch": {
1413:                 "protocol": "http",
1414:                 "endpoint": "http://verify-registry-endpoint:8765/probe/new",
1415:             },
1416:             "patch_started_at_utc": started_utc,
1417:             "patch_response_at_utc": patch_response["completed_utc"],
1418:             "patch_response_status": patch_response["status"],
1419:             "patch_response_latency_ms": patch_response["latency_ms"],
1420:             "patch_response_latency_stats_ms": {
1421:                 "n": 1,
1422:                 "median_ms": patch_response["latency_ms"],
1423:                 "p95_ms": patch_response["latency_ms"],
1424:                 "spread_ms": 0.0,
1425:             },
1426:             "websocket_probe_rate_per_second": HTTP_LOOP_RATE,
1427:             "websocket_probe_requests": len(probe_records),
1428:             "websocket_request_latency_ms": latency_summary(
1429:                 [row["latency_ms"] for row in probe_records]
1430:             ),
1431:             "first_new_behavior": {
1432:                 "index": first_new["index"],
1433:                 "started_utc": first_new["started_utc"],
1434:                 "completed_utc": first_new["completed_utc"],
1435:                 "ready_protocol": first_new["ready"].get("protocol"),
1436:                 "endpoint_probe": first_new["result"]["result"].get("endpoint_probe"),
1437:                 "latency_ms": first_new["latency_ms"],
1438:                 "requests_after_patch_through_first_new_behavior": sum(
1439:                     1
1440:                     for row in probe_records
1441:                     if row["_started_perf"] >= patch_response_perf
1442:                     and row["_started_perf"] <= first_new["_started_perf"]
1443:                 ),
1444:                 "patch_response_to_first_new_behavior_ms": delay_ms,
1445:                 "patch_response_to_first_new_behavior_stats": {
1446:                     "n": 1,
1447:                     "median_ms": delay_ms,
1448:                     "p95_ms": delay_ms,
1449:                     "spread_ms": 0.0,
1450:                 },
1451:             },
1452:             "http_loop_requests_during_websocket_probe": sum(
1453:                 row["_started_perf"] >= patch_response_perf for row in http_loop_rows
1454:             ),
1455:         }
1456: 
1457:     async def run_part_c(self):
1458:         part_dir = self.raw_root / "part_c"
1459:         part_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
1460:         v1_id = self.models["bench-canary:v1"]
1461:         v2_id = self.models["bench-canary:v2"]
1462:         # Return to the intended initial live state before starting the loop.
1463:         for model_key, model_id in self.models.items():
1464:             if model_key.startswith("bench-canary:"):
1465:                 patch_model(self.client, self.admin_key, model_id, {"status": "disabled"})
1466:         patch_model(self.client, self.admin_key, v1_id, {"status": "active"})
1467:         patch_model(self.client, self.admin_key, v2_id, {"status": "active", "canary_percent": 0})
1468: 
1469:         stop = asyncio.Event()
1470:         rows = []
1471:         gateway_id_before = docker_container_id("verify-registry-gateway")
1472:         compose_gateway_id_before = docker_container_id("modelmesh-gateway-1")
1473:         loop_task = asyncio.create_task(self.continuous_http_loop(stop, rows))
1474:         patches = []
1475:         try:
1476:             await asyncio.sleep(2.0)
1477:             patches.append(
1478:                 await self.patch_and_observe(
1479:                     rows,
1480:                     "canary_percent_0_to_100",
1481:                     v2_id,
1482:                     {"canary_percent": 100},
1483:                     lambda row: row["status"] == 200 and row["response_version"] == "v2",
1484:                     "unpinned response version is v2",
1485:                 )
1486:             )
1487:             patches.append(
1488:                 await self.patch_and_observe(
1489:                     rows,
1490:                     "disable_v2",
1491:                     v2_id,
1492:                     {"status": "disabled"},
1493:                     lambda row: row["status"] == 200 and row["response_version"] == "v1",
1494:                     "v2 is disabled; only active version v1 serves",
1495:                 )
1496:             )
1497:             patches.append(
1498:                 await self.patch_and_observe(
1499:                     rows,
1500:                     "disable_v1_all_versions_disabled",
1501:                     v1_id,
1502:                     {"status": "disabled"},
1503:                     lambda row: row["status"] == 503,
1504:                     "no active version; gateway returns HTTP 503",
1505:                 )
1506:             )
1507:             patches.append(
1508:                 await self.patch_and_observe(
1509:                     rows,
1510:                     "re_enable_v1",
1511:                     v1_id,
1512:                     {"status": "active"},
1513:                     lambda row: row["status"] == 200 and row["response_version"] == "v1",
1514:                     "v1 is active and serves traffic",
1515:                 )
1516:             )
1517:             patches.append(
1518:                 await self.patch_and_observe(
1519:                     rows,
1520:                     "re_enable_v2",
1521:                     v2_id,
1522:                     {"status": "active"},
1523:                     lambda row: row["status"] == 200 and row["response_version"] == "v2",
1524:                     "v2 is active at 100% canary",
1525:                 )
1526:             )
1527:             websocket = await self.run_websocket_update(rows)
1528:             await asyncio.sleep(1.0)
1529:         finally:
1530:             stop.set()
1531:             await loop_task
1532:             append_jsonl(part_dir / "http_requests.jsonl", (public_record(row) for row in rows))
1533:         gateway_id_after = docker_container_id("verify-registry-gateway")
1534:         compose_gateway_id_after = docker_container_id("modelmesh-gateway-1")
1535:         cleanup = cleanup_queue({"bench-canary"}, self.api_key_ids)
1536:         summary = {
1537:             "started_at_utc": rows[0]["request_started_utc"] if rows else None,
1538:             "ended_at_utc": rows[-1]["request_completed_utc"] if rows else None,
1539:             "target_loop_rate_per_second": HTTP_LOOP_RATE,
1540:             "gateway_container_id_before": gateway_id_before,
1541:             "gateway_container_id_after": gateway_id_after,
1542:             "gateway_container_unchanged": gateway_id_before == gateway_id_after,
1543:             "compose_gateway_container_id_before": compose_gateway_id_before,
1544:             "compose_gateway_container_id_after": compose_gateway_id_after,
1545:             "compose_gateway_container_unchanged": (
1546:                 compose_gateway_id_before == compose_gateway_id_after
1547:             ),
1548:             "request_count": len(rows),
1549:             "observed_rate_per_second": (
1550:                 len(rows)
1551:                 / max(
1552:                     1e-9,
1553:                     rows[-1]["_completed_perf"] - rows[0]["_started_perf"],
1554:                 )
1555:             ) if len(rows) > 1 else None,
1556:             "status_histogram": dict(
1557:                 sorted(collections.Counter(
1558:                     str(row["status"]) if row["status"] is not None else "NETWORK_ERROR" for row in rows
1559:                 ).items())
1560:             ),
1561:             "latency_http_200_ms": latency_summary([row["latency_ms"] for row in rows if row["status"] == 200]),
1562:             "latency_503_ms": latency_summary([row["latency_ms"] for row in rows if row["status"] == 503]),
1563:             "version_histogram": dict(sorted(collections.Counter(
1564:                 row["response_version"] for row in rows if row["response_version"]
1565:             ).items())),
1566:             "patches": patches,
1567:             "websocket_protocol_endpoint_patch": websocket,
1568:             "cleanup": cleanup,
1569:         }
1570:         write_json(part_dir / "summary.json", summary)
1571:         return summary
1572: 
1573:     def cleanup_models(self) -> dict:
1574:         disabled = []
1575:         for model_key, model_id in self.models.items():
1576:             result = response_json(self.client, "DELETE", f"/v1/models/{model_id}", bearer=self.admin_key)
1577:             if result["status"] != 200:
1578:                 raise RuntimeError(
1579:                     f"Failed to disable {model_key}: HTTP {result['status']} {result['body_text']}"
1580:                 )
1581:             disabled.append(model_key)
1582:         states = []
1583:         for model_key in self.models:
1584:             name, version = model_key.split(":", 1)
1585:             result = response_json(
1586:                 self.client, "GET", f"/v1/models/{name}?version={quote(version)}"
1587:             )
1588:             assert_status(result, 200, f"verify disabled {model_key}")
1589:             states.append({"model": model_key, "status": result["body"]["status"]})
1590:         queue_cleanup = cleanup_queue(self.all_queue_models, self.api_key_ids)
1591:         final_redis = redis_snapshot()
1592:         if final_redis["dbsize"] != 0:
1593:             raise RuntimeError(f"Redis DB 1 still has {final_redis['dbsize']} key(s) after cleanup")
1594:         return {
1595:             "disabled_models": disabled,
1596:             "model_states": states,
1597:             "queue_cleanup": queue_cleanup,
1598:             "redis_db1_final": final_redis,
1599:         }
1600: 
1601:     async def run(self):
1602:         failure = None
1603:         report = {"metadata": self.run_metadata, "status": "running"}
1604:         write_json(self.raw_root / "campaign_start.json", report)
1605:         original_registry_sql = (
1606:             "SELECT COALESCE(json_agg(row_to_json(models_snapshot) ORDER BY name, version), '[]'::json) "
1607:             "FROM (SELECT name, version, worker_name, protocol, endpoint, status, "
1608:             "supports_streaming, canary_percent FROM models "
1609:             "WHERE name IN ('doc-ocr', 'asr')) AS models_snapshot"
1610:         )
1611:         try:
1612:             ready = response_json(self.client, "GET", "/ready")
1613:             assert_status(ready, 200, "scratch gateway readiness")
1614:             report["scratch_gateway_ready"] = ready["body"]
1615:             report["original_registry_before"] = json.loads(
1616:                 self.run_sql(original_registry_sql, "modelmesh") or "[]"
1617:             )
1618:             preflight = redis_snapshot()
1619:             if preflight["dbsize"] != 0:
1620:                 raise RuntimeError(
1621:                     f"Redis DB 1 is not empty before benchmark ({preflight['dbsize']} keys); refusing to continue"
1622:                 )
1623:             if preflight["maxmemory_bytes"] != 0 or preflight["maxmemory_policy"] != "noeviction":
1624:                 raise RuntimeError(
1625:                     "Redis memory policy differs from verified preflight: "
1626:                     f"{preflight['maxmemory_bytes']} / {preflight['maxmemory_policy']}"
1627:                 )
1628:             self.run_metadata["redis_preflight"] = preflight
1629:             self.run_metadata["redis_maxmemory_policy"] = preflight["maxmemory_policy"]
1630:             self.setup_identity()
1631:             self.setup_models()
1632:             part_a = await self.run_part_a()
1633:             part_b, pinned = await self.run_part_b()
1634:             part_c = await self.run_part_c()
1635:             report.update(
1636:                 {
1637:                     "status": "completed",
1638:                     "part_a": part_a,
1639:                     "part_b": part_b,
1640:                     "pinned": pinned,
1641:                     "part_c": part_c,
1642:                 }
1643:             )
1644:         except (KeyboardInterrupt, asyncio.CancelledError) as exc:
1645:             report.update({"status": "aborted", "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"})
1646:             raise
1647:         except Exception as exc:
1648:             failure = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
1649:             report.update({"status": "failed", "error": failure})
1650:         finally:
1651:             cleanup_status = None
1652:             if self.admin_key and self.models:
1653:                 try:
1654:                     cleanup_status = self.cleanup_models()
1655:                 except Exception as exc:
1656:                     cleanup_status = {"error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"}
1657:             report["cleanup"] = cleanup_status
1658:             if (
1659:                 isinstance(cleanup_status, dict)
1660:                 and "redis_db1_final" in cleanup_status
1661:                 and "redis_preflight" in self.run_metadata
1662:             ):
1663:                 cleanup_status["redis_used_memory_delta_bytes_vs_preflight"] = (
1664:                     cleanup_status["redis_db1_final"]["used_memory_bytes"]
1665:                     - self.run_metadata["redis_preflight"]["used_memory_bytes"]
1666:                 )
1667:             try:
1668:                 report["original_registry_after"] = json.loads(
1669:                     self.run_sql(original_registry_sql, "modelmesh") or "[]"
1670:                 )
1671:                 report["original_registry_unchanged"] = (
1672:                     report.get("original_registry_before") == report["original_registry_after"]
1673:                 )
1674:             except Exception as exc:
1675:                 report["original_registry_after"] = None
1676:                 report["original_registry_unchanged"] = False
1677:                 report["original_registry_check_error"] = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
1678:             report["ended_at_utc"] = utc_now()
1679:             write_json(self.raw_root / "campaign_summary.json", report)
1680:         if failure:
1681:             raise RuntimeError(failure)
1682:         return report
1683: 
1684: 
1685: async def main_async(args):
1686:     campaign = Campaign(args)
1687:     return await campaign.run()
1688: 
1689: 
1690: def main():
1691:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
1692:     parser.add_argument("--base-url", default="http://127.0.0.1:8003")
1693:     parser.add_argument("--raw-dir", required=True, type=Path)
1694:     parser.add_argument("--database-name", required=True)
1695:     parser.add_argument("--rates", nargs="+", type=float, default=[10.0, 50.0, 100.0])
1696:     parser.add_argument("--duration", type=float, default=60.0)
1697:     parser.add_argument("--repetitions", type=int, default=3)
1698:     parser.add_argument("--canary-samples", type=int, default=CANARY_SAMPLE_COUNT)
1699:     parser.add_argument("--canary-rate", type=float, default=CANARY_REQUEST_RATE)
1700:     parser.add_argument("--memory-safety-bytes", type=int, default=REDIS_MEMORY_SAFETY_BYTES)
1701:     args = parser.parse_args()
1702:     if (
1703:         any(rate <= 0 for rate in args.rates)
1704:         or args.duration <= 0
1705:         or args.repetitions <= 0
1706:         or args.canary_samples <= 0
1707:         or args.canary_rate <= 0
1708:     ):
1709:         parser.error("rates, duration, repetitions, canary sample count, and canary rate must be positive")
1710:     result = asyncio.run(main_async(args))
1711:     print(json.dumps(result, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
1712: 
1713: 
1714: if __name__ == "__main__":
1715:     main()

 10: | a   | Gateway reads model registry from Postgres on every request with no caching                              | TRUE                             | `gateway/registry.py:15-26` executes `SELECT * FROM models` for `get_model`; `gateway/registry.py:68-91` executes `SELECT * FROM models` for `list_versions`. `gateway/main.py:173-189` calls `get_model_with_canary` or `get_model` on each REST inference. `gateway/main.py:315-334` calls `get_model` on each WebSocket connection. No `cache`, `cached`, or `lru_cache` match exists under `gateway/*.py`.                                                                |
 11: | b   | REST `/v1/infer` uses canary selection; WebSocket does not                                               | TRUE                             | REST uses `get_model_with_canary(database, model_name)` when `version is None` in `gateway/main.py:177-184`. WebSocket reads `version` defaulting to `v1` and calls `registry.get_model(...)` in `gateway/main.py:315-334`; it has no canary branch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
 12: | c   | Registry `protocol`/`endpoint` used only by WebSocket, never REST queue                                  | TRUE                             | REST resolves a model and calls `enqueue_job(redis, model,...)` in `gateway/main.py:187-191`; `gateway/job_queue.py:32-58` stores `worker_name` and does not read `protocol` or `endpoint`. `gateway/main.py:89-105` routes by `protocol` and `endpoint` in `route_request`, and the only direct call is in the HTTP/gRPC branch of `ws_infer` at `gateway/main.py:332-377`.                                                                                                  |
 13: | d   | Canary picks highest numeric version as canary and lowest as stable                                      | TRUE                             | `gateway/main.py:99-115` says `canary = max(..., key=_vnum)` and `stable = min(..., key=_vnum)`; `_vnum` extracts the first digit sequence in `gateway/main.py:118-120`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

Runtime Redis package `redis.asyncio.Redis.pipeline` has `transaction: bool = True` in redis-py 5.0.4, and `test_gateway.py:61-70` verifies 429 on the third request and a 60-second bounded TTL. |
