[MD]  verification\runs\03_grpc_vs_http\README.md
================================================================================
  1: # WebSocket HTTP vs gRPC latency verification
  2: 
  3: ## Result
  4: 
  5: This benchmark does **not** substantiate a reliable gRPC speedup. The fixture and generated-speech comparisons all have run-cluster 95% confidence intervals that include zero. The page-like OCR comparison is not a valid five-run comparison: OCR failed for most requests, and only 2 of 5 runs had any successful measured requests in both arms. Every row therefore receives the conservative verdict **no measurable difference**.
  6: 
  7: | Payload          | Model / gRPC arm |            HTTP p50 (n; p95; spread) |           gRPC p50 (n; p95; spread) |      gRPC - HTTP p50 |               95% bootstrap CI (ms; %) | Mann-Whitney p | Independent paired runs | Runs favoring gRPC | Verdict                  |
  8: | ---------------- | ---------------- | -----------------------------------: | ----------------------------------: | -------------------: | -------------------------------------: | -------------: | ----------------------: | -----------------: | ------------------------ |
  9: | Existing image   | OCR / unary      |      356.45 ms (500; 459.37; 406.13) |    350.76 ms (500; 470.18; 3362.88) |    -5.69 ms (-1.60%) |     [-32.37, +30.59]; [-8.30%, +9.60%] |         0.3893 |                     5/5 |                3/5 | no measurable difference |
 10: | Page-like image  | OCR / unary      | 27683.42 ms (86; 30177.53; 28518.32) | 26200.42 ms (15; 27907.99; 4496.96) | -1483.00 ms (-5.36%) | [-1483.00, -1138.05]; [-5.36%, -4.31%] |       0.000342 |                     2/5 |              2/5\* | no measurable difference |
 11: | Existing audio   | ASR / stream     |      712.01 ms (500; 951.54; 475.78) |     698.39 ms (500; 940.13; 769.97) |   -13.62 ms (-1.91%) |      [-40.29, +2.60]; [-4.96%, +0.36%] |       0.001317 |                     5/5 |                4/5 | no measurable difference |
 12: | Existing audio   | ASR / unary      |      704.63 ms (500; 967.68; 595.95) |     691.97 ms (500; 962.52; 914.64) |   -12.66 ms (-1.80%) |     [-25.04, +10.47]; [-3.49%, +1.55%] |         0.1681 |                     5/5 |                3/5 | no measurable difference |
 13: | Generated speech | ASR / stream     |   1756.68 ms (500; 2437.42; 2836.74) |  1743.96 ms (500; 2404.32; 3112.30) |   -12.72 ms (-0.72%) |    [-24.17, +143.35]; [-1.40%, +7.56%] |         0.4621 |                     5/5 |                3/5 | no measurable difference |
 14: | Generated speech | ASR / unary      |   2086.45 ms (500; 3045.83; 5158.57) |  2054.86 ms (500; 3113.64; 3966.73) |   -31.59 ms (-1.51%) |     [-95.75, +28.88]; [-4.43%, +1.33%] |         0.4928 |                     5/5 |                3/5 | no measurable difference |
 15: 
 16: All latency values are milliseconds. Each arm's summary shows `p50 (successful request n; p95; spread)`, where spread is max minus min among successful measured requests. Difference is `gRPC p50 - HTTP p50`; a negative value means the observed gRPC p50 was lower. Full per-run p50, p95, mean, spread, success/attempt counts, and p50 differences are in [analysis.md](./analysis.md); machine-readable results and failed-request records are in [analysis.json](./analysis.json).
 17: 
 18: For the page-like OCR row, the reported pooled p50s and bootstrap interval use only successful observations from the two runs with successes in both arms (86 HTTP and 15 gRPC observations). Across all five attempted runs, there were 134 successful HTTP and 15 successful gRPC requests. Three runs had no successful gRPC page-OCR requests; thus `2/5*` means both paired runs favored gRPC, while the other three runs were not comparable. The interval excludes zero but is based on only two independent run clusters, alongside severe failures; it is not evidence for the requested five-run claim. The conservative verdict does not label this a speedup.
 19: 
 20: The Mann-Whitney values are two-sided asymptotic tests with tie correction on pooled request-level samples, not run-level tests. Requests within a run are not independent; interpret these p-values cautiously. The confidence intervals instead bootstrap whole run clusters (10,000 resamples). The OCR page comparison's interval has only two available clusters.
 21: 
 22: ## Method and environment
 23: 
 24: - Full Docker Compose stack was used, with a separate benchmark gateway on port 8003. `/ready` returned HTTP 200 before the run and after cleanup.
 25: - The benchmark gateway used an isolated scratch PostgreSQL database (`modelmesh_bench_20261122`) and Redis DB 1. The original model registry was not used for benchmark entries.
 26: - In-network endpoints: OCR HTTP `http://doc-ocr-worker:8001/infer`, OCR gRPC `doc-ocr-worker:50051`; ASR HTTP `http://asr-worker:8002/infer`, ASR gRPC `asr-worker:50051`.
 27: - Five separate benchmark processes were run. Each process made 20 warm-ups and then 100 measured requests per arm for each of six payload/protocol scenarios. Requests were sequential, with one WebSocket connection per request; the arm order was randomized separately within each scenario's warm-up and measured phases. The four inter-run pauses were each recorded as 60 seconds.
 28: - Orchestration was invoked as `powershell.exe -NoProfile -File verification\runs\03_grpc_vs_http\run_benchmark.ps1`; its setup and cleanup commands are in [run_benchmark.ps1](./run_benchmark.ps1), with the invocation and setup captures in [orchestration-start.json](./raw/setup/orchestration-start.json).
 29: - Each run logged 1,440 requests; 7,200 request rows total. There were 1,030 failures (851 measured, 179 warm-up), all in generated page-like OCR. No 429 responses were recorded; the benchmark key was configured for 1,000 requests/minute.
 30: - Timer: Python `time.perf_counter()`. Failed requests are excluded from latency statistics and retained in the raw CSVs.
 31: - Pre-run TTS, payload-size, scratch-database initialization, and command-probe anomalies are recorded in [setup-anomalies.txt](./raw/setup/setup-anomalies.txt); these did not send benchmark requests.
 32: - Host: Windows 11 Home, Docker Desktop; Docker server 29.9.2, Docker Compose 5.6.1, 16 daemon CPUs and about 7.58 GiB daemon memory. PostgreSQL 16.16 and Redis 7.4.12. No Compose CPU or memory limits were configured. No other host workload was continuously profiled (NOT VERIFIED). See [00_environment.md](../../00_environment.md).
 33: - Git commit for all five runs: `d6fd970b76ed5e0a747be709e0f62e7a009d31da`; the worktree was dirty throughout. Per-run exact commands, start/end timestamps, exit status, configuration, and anomalies are recorded in [run 01](./run-01/README.md), [run 02](./run-02/README.md), [run 03](./run-03/README.md), [run 04](./run-04/README.md), and [run 05](./run-05/README.md).
 34: 
 35: ### Payloads
 36: 
 37: | Payload              |       Size and format | Details                                                                                                                  |
 38: | -------------------- | --------------------: | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
 39: | Existing OCR fixture |      2,113 bytes, PNG | `test-image.png`, 360x169                                                                                                |
 40: | Existing ASR fixture |    149,650 bytes, WAV | `test-audio.wav`, 0.557 s, 44.1 kHz, stereo, 24-bit                                                                      |
 41: | Larger OCR payload   | 1,640,202 bytes, JPEG | `verification/payloads/page_like.jpg`, PIL-rendered page-like image, 2550x3300                                           |
 42: | Larger ASR payload   |  1,281,568 bytes, WAV | `verification/payloads/realistic_speech.wav`, synthetic Windows TTS generated offline, 29.059 s, 22.05 kHz, mono, 16-bit |
 43: 
 44: ## ASR gRPC stream behavior
 45: 
 46: The captured successful ASR gRPC-stream request received **one response message**, with `is_final=true`, `confidence=0.0`, and partial text `" Hello."`. The complete audio file was sent in **one WebSocket binary message**; this gateway does not perform incremental audio upload. The capture is retained in [asr_grpc_stream_sequence.json](./run-01/raw/asr_grpc_stream_sequence.json). The gateway implementation receives one `receive_bytes()` result and yields one final chunk in [gateway/main.py](../../../gateway/main.py#L351).
 47: 
 48: ## Failures and anomalies
 49: 
 50: The larger OCR payload made the benchmark unreliable for comparing protocols: only 134/500 HTTP and 15/500 gRPC measured requests succeeded. OCR worker child processes were observed being killed/restarted; a run-05 point-in-time sample showed 791.78% CPU and 2.682 GiB memory, while Docker reported `OOMKilled=false`. This does not establish the cause.
 51: 
 52: Five failed page-OCR requests have recorded elapsed times beyond the configured 90-second receive timeout; the maximum was 17,573,222 ms. The raw failure rows are retained and excluded from latency statistics. A host suspension or clock discontinuity is suspected but unverified. Run 05's wrapper metadata says no anomaly was reported by the benchmark process; the separately captured [runtime-anomalies.txt](./run-05/raw/runtime-anomalies.txt) documents the discrepancy. No rerun was performed to replace these results.
 53: 
 54: The analysis script itself required corrections after initial syntax and incomplete-run handling failures. Those analysis-only failures sent no application requests. The originally generated analysis files were overwritten and are not retained in full; this limitation and the observed failures are documented in [analysis-validation-history.md](./raw/analysis-validation-history.md). The corrected analysis execution is recorded in [analysis-validation-final.txt](./raw/analysis-validation-final.txt).
 55: 
 56: ## Cleanup and limits
 57: 
 58: - All five benchmark model entries, the scratch API-key state, the scratch user, the scratch database, and the temporary benchmark gateway were removed. The original models' protocols and endpoints were identical before and after cleanup.
 59: - Redis DB 1 had size 0 after cleanup; no Redis database was flushed.
 60: - The normal Compose stack was left running and `/ready` returned HTTP 200.
 61: - Optional step C (the no-op HTTP/gRPC echo server transport-overhead measurement) was **NOT RUN**.
 62: - These results compare end-to-end inference through this gateway and workers; they do not isolate transport overhead. They do not prove a transport-wide performance advantage.
 63: - The final worktree has an `.gitignore` change adding `verification/`; it was absent in the initial status snapshot, its origin is not established, and it was left untouched.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2: """Analyze the interleaved WebSocket benchmark without discarding failed runs."""
  3: 
  4: import csv
  5: import json
  6: import math
  7: import random
  8: import statistics
  9: from collections import defaultdict
 10: from pathlib import Path
 11: 
 12: 
 13: ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
 14: RUNS_DIR = ROOT
 15: BOOTSTRAP_RESAMPLES = 10_000
 16: SCENARIOS = {
 17:     "ocr_existing": ("Existing image", "OCR", "grpc_unary"),
 18:     "ocr_page": ("Page-like image", "OCR", "grpc_unary"),
 19:     "asr_existing_stream": ("Existing audio", "ASR (gRPC stream)", "grpc_stream"),
 20:     "asr_existing_unary": ("Existing audio", "ASR (gRPC unary)", "grpc_unary"),
 21:     "asr_speech_stream": ("Generated speech", "ASR (gRPC stream)", "grpc_stream"),
 22:     "asr_speech_unary": ("Generated speech", "ASR (gRPC unary)", "grpc_unary"),

 25: 
 26: def percentile(values, fraction):
 27:     ordered = sorted(values)
 28:     if not ordered:
 29:         return None
 30:     position = (len(ordered) - 1) * fraction
 31:     low = math.floor(position)
 32:     high = math.ceil(position)
 33:     if low == high:
 34:         return ordered[low]
 35:     return ordered[low] + (ordered[high] - ordered[low]) * (position - low)
 36: 
 37: 
 38: def summary(values):
 39:     if not values:
 40:         return {"n": 0, "median_ms": None, "p95_ms": None, "mean_ms": None, "spread_ms": None}
 41:     return {
 42:         "n": len(values),
 43:         "median_ms": statistics.median(values),
 44:         "p95_ms": percentile(values, 0.95),
 45:         "mean_ms": statistics.mean(values),
 46:         "spread_ms": max(values) - min(values),
 47:         "min_ms": min(values),
 48:         "max_ms": max(values),
 49:     }
 50: 
 51: 
 52: def mann_whitney_p_value(first, second):
 53:     """Two-sided asymptotic Mann-Whitney U with tie correction."""
 54:     n1, n2 = len(first), len(second)
 55:     if not n1 or not n2:
 56:         return None
 57:     combined = [(value, 0) for value in first] + [(value, 1) for value in second]
 58:     combined.sort(key=lambda item: item[0])
 59:     rank_sum_first = 0.0
 60:     tie_sum = 0.0
 61:     start = 0
 62:     while start < len(combined):
 63:         end = start + 1
 64:         while end < len(combined) and combined[end][0] == combined[start][0]:
 65:             end += 1
 66:         average_rank = ((start + 1) + end) / 2.0
 67:         for _, group in combined[start:end]:
 68:             if group == 0:
 69:                 rank_sum_first += average_rank
 70:         tie_count = end - start
 71:         tie_sum += tie_count**3 - tie_count
 72:         start = end
 73:     u1 = rank_sum_first - n1 * (n1 + 1) / 2.0
 74:     total = n1 + n2
 75:     variance = n1 * n2 / 12.0 * (
 76:         total + 1 - tie_sum / (total * (total - 1))
 77:     )
 78:     if variance <= 0:
 79:         return 1.0
 80:     z = (u1 - n1 * n2 / 2.0) / math.sqrt(variance)
 81:     return math.erfc(abs(z) / math.sqrt(2.0))
 82: 
 83: 
 84: def bootstrap_run_clusters(run_values, rng):
 85:     run_ids = sorted(run_values)
 86:     if len(run_ids) < 2:
 87:         return None
 88:     differences_ms = []
 89:     differences_pct = []
 90:     for _ in range(BOOTSTRAP_RESAMPLES):
 91:         selected = [rng.choice(run_ids) for _ in run_ids]
 92:         http = [
 93:             value
 94:             for run_id in selected
 95:             for value in run_values[run_id]["http"]
 96:         ]
 97:         grpc = [
 98:             value
 99:             for run_id in selected
100:             for value in run_values[run_id]["grpc"]
101:         ]
102:         http_p50 = statistics.median(http)
103:         difference = statistics.median(grpc) - http_p50
104:         differences_ms.append(difference)
105:         differences_pct.append(100 * difference / http_p50 if http_p50 else float("nan"))
106:     return {
107:         "resamples": BOOTSTRAP_RESAMPLES,
108:         "unit": "independent run cluster; complete within-run request samples retained",
109:         "difference_ms_ci95": [
110:             percentile(differences_ms, 0.025),
111:             percentile(differences_ms, 0.975),
112:         ],
113:         "difference_percent_ci95": [
114:             percentile(differences_pct, 0.025),
115:             percentile(differences_pct, 0.975),
116:         ],
117:     }
118: 
119: 
120: def read_data():
121:     observations = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list)))
122:     measured_counts = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(int)))
123:     failures = []
124:     run_metadata = {}
125:     for run_dir in sorted(RUNS_DIR.glob("run-*")):
126:         if not run_dir.is_dir():
127:             continue
128:         raw = run_dir / "raw"
129:         process_file = raw / "process.json"
130:         request_file = raw / "requests.csv"
131:         if process_file.exists():
132:             run_metadata[run_dir.name] = json.loads(process_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
133:         if not request_file.exists():
134:             continue
135:         with request_file.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as file:
136:             for row in csv.DictReader(file):
137:                 if row["phase"] != "measured":
138:                     continue
139:                 scenario = row["scenario"]
140:                 arm = row["arm"]
141:                 measured_counts[scenario][run_dir.name][arm] += 1
142:                 if row["success"].lower() == "true":
143:                     observations[scenario][run_dir.name][arm].append(float(row["latency_ms"]))
144:                 else:
145:                     failures.append(
146:                         {
147:                             "run": run_dir.name,
148:                             "scenario": scenario,
149:                             "arm": arm,
150:                             "payload": row["payload"],
151:                             "index": int(row["index"]),
152:                             "error": row["error"],
153:                         }
154:                     )
155:     return observations, measured_counts, failures, run_metadata
156: 
157: 
158: def main():
159:     observations, measured_counts, failures, run_metadata = read_data()
160:     result = {
161:         "method": {
162:             "bootstrap_resamples": BOOTSTRAP_RESAMPLES,
163:             "bootstrap_unit": "independent run cluster",
164:             "p95_method": "linear interpolation at the 95th percentile",
165:             "mann_whitney": "two-sided asymptotic U test with tie correction; request-level pooled samples",
166:             "failed_measured_requests_excluded_from_latency_statistics": True,
167:         },
168:         "runs": run_metadata,
169:         "failures": failures,
170:         "scenarios": {},
171:     }
172: 
173:     for scenario, (payload_label, model_label, grpc_arm) in SCENARIOS.items():
174:         all_run_ids = sorted(run_metadata)
175:         run_values = {
176:             run_id: {
177:                 "http": observations[scenario][run_id]["http"],
178:                 "grpc": observations[scenario][run_id][grpc_arm],
179:             }
180:             for run_id in all_run_ids
181:         }
182:         present = {
183:             run_id: arms
184:             for run_id, arms in run_values.items()
185:             if arms["http"] and arms["grpc"]
186:         }
187:         if not present:
188:             result["scenarios"][scenario] = {
189:                 "payload": payload_label,
190:                 "model": model_label,
191:                 "status": "NOT RUN",
192:                 "reason": "No run has successful measured requests for both HTTP and gRPC arms.",
193:                 "attempted_runs": len(all_run_ids),
194:             }
195:             continue
196: 
197:         http_all = [value for arms in present.values() for value in arms["http"]]
198:         grpc_all = [value for arms in present.values() for value in arms["grpc"]]
199:         http_stats = summary(http_all)
200:         grpc_stats = summary(grpc_all)
201:         delta_ms = grpc_stats["median_ms"] - http_stats["median_ms"]
202:         delta_pct = 100 * delta_ms / http_stats["median_ms"] if http_stats["median_ms"] else None
203:         run_results = {}
204:         favoured = 0
205:         clusters = {}
206:         for run_id, arms in sorted(run_values.items()):
207:             http_values = arms["http"]
208:             grpc_values = arms["grpc"]
209:             http_run = summary(http_values)
210:             grpc_run = summary(grpc_values)
211:             http_attempts = measured_counts[scenario][run_id]["http"]
212:             grpc_attempts = measured_counts[scenario][run_id][grpc_arm]
213:             http_run["attempted"] = http_attempts
214:             http_run["failures"] = http_attempts - len(http_values)
215:             grpc_run["attempted"] = grpc_attempts
216:             grpc_run["failures"] = grpc_attempts - len(grpc_values)
217:             if http_values and grpc_values:
218:                 run_delta = grpc_run["median_ms"] - http_run["median_ms"]
219:                 run_pct = 100 * run_delta / http_run["median_ms"] if http_run["median_ms"] else None
220:                 favoured += int(run_delta < 0)
221:                 clusters[run_id] = {"http": http_values, "grpc": grpc_values}
222:             else:
223:                 run_delta = None
224:                 run_pct = None
225:             run_results[run_id] = {
226:                 "http": http_run,
227:                 "grpc": grpc_run,
228:                 "difference_ms": run_delta,
229:                 "difference_percent": run_pct,
230:                 "favoured_grpc": run_delta < 0 if run_delta is not None else None,
231:             }
232:         ci = bootstrap_run_clusters(clusters, random.Random(300 + len(present)))
233:         ms_low, ms_high = ci["difference_ms_ci95"]
234:         if len(present) < len(all_run_ids):
235:             verdict = "no measurable difference"
236:         elif ms_high < 0:
237:             verdict = "gRPC faster (CI excludes 0)"
238:         elif ms_low > 0:
239:             verdict = "HTTP faster (CI excludes 0)"
240:         else:
241:             verdict = "no measurable difference"
242: 
243:         result["scenarios"][scenario] = {
244:             "payload": payload_label,
245:             "model": model_label,
246:             "grpc_arm": grpc_arm,
247:             "independent_runs_with_both_arms": len(present),
248:             "http_pooled": http_stats,
249:             "grpc_pooled": grpc_stats,
250:             "difference_ms": delta_ms,
251:             "difference_percent": delta_pct,
252:             "bootstrap_ci95": ci,
253:             "mann_whitney_u_p_value": mann_whitney_p_value(http_all, grpc_all),
254:             "runs_favouring_grpc": favoured,
255:             "attempted_independent_runs": len(all_run_ids),
256:             "runs_with_both_successful_arms": len(present),
257:             "per_run": run_results,
258:             "verdict": verdict,
259:         }
260: 
261:     output = ROOT / "analysis.json"
262:     output.write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
263:     lines = [
264:         "# Benchmark Analysis",
265:         "",
266:         "Successful measured requests only; failed requests remain listed in `analysis.json`.",
267:         "Bootstrap intervals resample independent run clusters (10,000 resamples), retaining all requests within each sampled run.",
268:         "Mann-Whitney p-values are two-sided asymptotic request-level pooled tests with tie correction.",
269:         "",
270:         "| Payload | Model | HTTP p50 (successful n; p95; spread) | gRPC p50 (successful n; p95; spread) | Difference | 95% CI (ms) | 95% CI (%) | U p-value | Runs favoring gRPC (paired/attempted) | Verdict |",
271:         "|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|",
272:     ]
273:     incomplete_notes = []
274:     for scenario, details in result["scenarios"].items():
275:         if details.get("status") == "NOT RUN":
276:             lines.append(
277:                 f"| {details['payload']} | {details['model']} | NOT RUN | NOT RUN | NOT RUN | NOT RUN | NOT RUN | NOT RUN | 0/{details.get('attempted_runs', 5)} | NOT RUN |"
278:             )
279:             continue
280:         http = details["http_pooled"]
281:         grpc = details["grpc_pooled"]
282:         ci = details["bootstrap_ci95"]
283:         lines.append(
284:             "| {payload} | {model} | {h50:.2f} ms (n={hn}; p95 {hp95:.2f}; spread {hs:.2f}) "
285:             "| {g50:.2f} ms (n={gn}; p95 {gp95:.2f}; spread {gs:.2f}) "
286:             "| {dpct:+.2f}% ({dms:+.2f} ms) | [{ml:+.2f}, {mh:+.2f}] ms "
287:             "| [{pl:+.2f}%, {ph:+.2f}%] | {pval:.4g} | {fav}/{runs} | {verdict} |".format(
288:                 payload=details["payload"],
289:                 model=details["model"],
290:                 h50=http["median_ms"],
291:                 hn=http["n"],
292:                 hp95=http["p95_ms"],
293:                 hs=http["spread_ms"],
294:                 g50=grpc["median_ms"],
295:                 gn=grpc["n"],
296:                 gp95=grpc["p95_ms"],
297:                 gs=grpc["spread_ms"],
298:                 dpct=details["difference_percent"],
299:                 dms=details["difference_ms"],
300:                 ml=ci["difference_ms_ci95"][0],
301:                 mh=ci["difference_ms_ci95"][1],
302:                 pl=ci["difference_percent_ci95"][0],
303:                 ph=ci["difference_percent_ci95"][1],
304:                 pval=details["mann_whitney_u_p_value"],
305:                 fav=details["runs_favouring_grpc"],
306:                 runs=details["attempted_independent_runs"],
307:                 verdict=details["verdict"],
308:             )
309:         )
310:         if details["runs_with_both_successful_arms"] < details["attempted_independent_runs"]:
311:             incomplete_notes.append(
312:                 f"- {details['payload']} / {details['model']}: only "
313:                 f"{details['runs_with_both_successful_arms']}/{details['attempted_independent_runs']} "
314:                 "attempted runs had successful measured requests for both arms."
315:             )
316:     if incomplete_notes:
317:         lines.extend(["", "Incomplete paired-run coverage:", *incomplete_notes])
318:     lines.extend(
319:         [
320:             "",
321:             "## Per-run statistics",
322:             "",
323:             "All latencies are in milliseconds. Spread is max minus min across successful measured requests.",
324:             "",
325:             "| Scenario | Run | HTTP successful/attempted; p50 / p95 / mean / spread | gRPC successful/attempted; p50 / p95 / mean / spread | Difference (ms; %) |",
326:             "|---|---|---:|---:|---:|",
327:         ]
328:     )
329:     for scenario, details in result["scenarios"].items():
330:         if details.get("status") == "NOT RUN":
331:             continue
332:         for run_id, run in details["per_run"].items():
333:             http = run["http"]
334:             grpc = run["grpc"]
335:             http_summary = (
336:                 f"{http['n']}/{http['attempted']}; "
337:                 f"{http['median_ms']:.2f} / {http['p95_ms']:.2f} / "
338:                 f"{http['mean_ms']:.2f} / {http['spread_ms']:.2f}"
339:                 if http["n"]
340:                 else f"0/{http['attempted']}; NOT AVAILABLE"
341:             )
342:             grpc_summary = (
343:                 f"{grpc['n']}/{grpc['attempted']}; "
344:                 f"{grpc['median_ms']:.2f} / {grpc['p95_ms']:.2f} / "
345:                 f"{grpc['mean_ms']:.2f} / {grpc['spread_ms']:.2f}"
346:                 if grpc["n"]
347:                 else f"0/{grpc['attempted']}; NOT AVAILABLE"
348:             )
349:             difference = (
350:                 f"{run['difference_ms']:+.2f}; {run['difference_percent']:+.2f}%"
351:                 if run["difference_ms"] is not None
352:                 else "NOT AVAILABLE"
353:             )
354:             lines.append(
355:                 f"| {scenario} | {run_id} | {http_summary} | {grpc_summary} | {difference} |"
356:             )
357:     (ROOT / "analysis.md").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
358:     print(f"Wrote {output} and {ROOT / 'analysis.md'}")
359:

362:     main()

 24: SCENARIOS = [
 25:     {
 26:         "scenario": "ocr_existing",
 27:         "payload": "ocr_existing",
 28:         "path": ROOT / "test-image.png",
 29:         "arms": {"http": "bench-ocr-http", "grpc_unary": "bench-ocr-grpc"},

 64: def utc_now():
 65:     return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
 66: 
 67: 
 68: def git_value(*args):
 69:     try:
 70:         return subprocess.check_output(
 71:             ["git", *args], cwd=ROOT, text=True, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT
 72:         ).strip()
 73:     except Exception as exc:
 74:         return f"UNAVAILABLE: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"

 77: def percentile(values, fraction):
 78:     ordered = sorted(values)
 79:     if not ordered:
 80:         return None
 81:     position = (len(ordered) - 1) * fraction
 82:     low = math.floor(position)
 83:     high = math.ceil(position)
 84:     if low == high:
 85:         return ordered[low]
 86:     return ordered[low] + (ordered[high] - ordered[low]) * (position - low)

 89: def stats(values):
 90:     if not values:
 91:         return {"n": 0, "median_ms": None, "p95_ms": None, "mean_ms": None, "spread_ms": None}
 92:     return {
 93:         "n": len(values),
 94:         "median_ms": statistics.median(values),
 95:         "p95_ms": percentile(values, 0.95),
 96:         "mean_ms": statistics.mean(values),
 97:         "spread_ms": max(values) - min(values),
 98:         "min_ms": min(values),
 99:         "max_ms": max(values),
100:     }

103: async def one_request(base_ws, api_key, model_name, payload, capture_sequence):
104:     uri = f"{base_ws}/v1/ws/infer/{model_name}?api_key={api_key}&version=v1"
105:     started = time.perf_counter()
106:     ready = None
107:     messages = []
108:     success = False
109:     error = ""
110:     try:
111:         async with websockets.connect(
112:             uri,
113:             open_timeout=10,
114:             close_timeout=5,
115:             ping_interval=None,
116:             max_size=MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES + 1024 * 1024,
117:         ) as ws:
118:             ready = json.loads(await asyncio.wait_for(ws.recv(), timeout=20))
119:             if ready.get("status") != "ready":
120:                 raise RuntimeError(f"not ready: {ready}")
121:             await ws.send(payload)
122:             while True:
123:                 raw = await asyncio.wait_for(ws.recv(), timeout=90)
124:                 message = json.loads(raw)
125:                 messages.append(message)
126:                 if message.get("error"):
127:                     raise RuntimeError(str(message["error"]))
128:                 if message.get("is_final") or "result" in message:
129:                     success = True
130:                     break
131:     except Exception as exc:
132:         error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}".replace(api_key, "[REDACTED]")
133: 
134:     elapsed_ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000
135:     capture = None
136:     if capture_sequence:
137:         capture = {
138:             "ready_message": ready,
139:             "response_messages": messages,
140:             "response_message_count": len(messages),
141:             "is_final_flags": [message.get("is_final") for message in messages],
142:             "confidence_values": [message.get("confidence") for message in messages],
143:             "success": success,
144:             "error": error,
145:             "whole_payload_sent_as_one_websocket_binary_message": True,
146:             "note": (
147:                 "The gateway source consumes one receive_bytes() result and yields "
148:                 "one final gRPC InferenceChunk for this request."
149:             ),
150:         }
151:     return elapsed_ms, success, error, capture
152: 
153: 
154: async def run(args):
155:     api_key = os.environ.get("MODELMESH_BENCH_KEY")
156:     if not api_key:
157:         raise RuntimeError("MODELMESH_BENCH_KEY environment variable is not set")
158: 
159:     raw_dir = Path(args.run_dir).resolve() / "raw"
160:     raw_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
161:     request_path = raw_dir / "requests.csv"
162:     capture_path = raw_dir / "asr_grpc_stream_sequence.json"
163:     started_at = utc_now()
164:     wall_started = time.perf_counter()
165:     rng = random.Random(args.seed)
166:     missing_payloads = []
167:     selected_scenarios = []
168:     for scenario in SCENARIOS:
169:         if scenario["path"].is_file():
170:             selected_scenarios.append(scenario)
171:         else:
172:             missing_payloads.append(
173:                 {"payload": scenario["payload"], "path": str(scenario["path"])}
174:             )
175: 
176:     counts = {}
177:     successes = {}
178:     failures = {}
179:     rate_limit_failures = 0
180:     capture_written = capture_path.exists()
181:     fields = [
182:         "run",
183:         "scenario",
184:         "arm",
185:         "payload",
186:         "phase",
187:         "index",
188:         "latency_ms",
189:         "success",
190:         "error",
191:     ]
192: 
193:     with request_path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as request_file:
194:         writer = csv.DictWriter(request_file, fieldnames=fields)
195:         writer.writeheader()
196:         request_file.flush()
197: 
198:         for scenario in selected_scenarios:
199:             payload_bytes = scenario["path"].read_bytes()
200:             if len(payload_bytes) > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES:
201:                 raise ValueError(
202:                     f"{scenario['path']} is {len(payload_bytes)} bytes, "
203:                     f"above the configured {MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES}-byte upload limit"
204:                 )
205:             arm_names = list(scenario["arms"])
206:             for phase, per_arm in (("warmup", args.warmups), ("measured", args.requests)):
207:                 order = [arm for arm in arm_names for _ in range(per_arm)]
208:                 rng.shuffle(order)
209:                 for arm in order:
210:                     key = (scenario["scenario"], arm, phase)
211:                     counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
212:                     index = counts[key]
213:                     is_capture = (
214:                         args.capture_stream_sequence
215:                         and not capture_written
216:                         and scenario["scenario"] == "asr_existing_stream"
217:                         and arm == "grpc_stream"
218:                     )
219:                     latency_ms, success, error, capture = await one_request(
220:                         args.base_ws,
221:                         api_key,
222:                         scenario["arms"][arm],
223:                         payload_bytes,
224:                         is_capture,
225:                     )
226:                     row = {
227:                         "run": args.run_id,
228:                         "scenario": scenario["scenario"],
229:                         "arm": arm,
230:                         "payload": scenario["payload"],
231:                         "phase": phase,
232:                         "index": index,
233:                         "latency_ms": f"{latency_ms:.6f}",
234:                         "success": str(success).lower(),
235:                         "error": error,
236:                     }
237:                     writer.writerow(row)
238:                     request_file.flush()
239: 
240:                     bucket = successes if success else failures
241:                     bucket[key] = bucket.get(key, 0) + 1
242:                     if "429" in error or "rate limit" in error.lower():
243:                         rate_limit_failures += 1
244:                     if capture is not None:
245:                         capture_path.write_text(
246:                             json.dumps(capture, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
247:                         )
248:                         capture_written = True
249:                     print(
250:                         f"{args.run_id},{scenario['scenario']},{phase},{arm},"
251:                         f"{index},{latency_ms:.3f}ms,success={success}"
252:                         + (f",error={error}" if error else ""),
253:                         flush=True,
254:                     )
255: 
256:     with request_path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as request_file:
257:         rows = list(csv.DictReader(request_file))
258: 
259:     measured = {}
260:     for scenario in selected_scenarios:
261:         for arm in scenario["arms"]:
262:             key = (scenario["scenario"], arm, "measured")
263:             measured[f"{scenario['scenario']}::{arm}"] = {
264:                 **stats(
265:                     [
266:                         float(row["latency_ms"])
267:                         for row in rows
268:                         if row["scenario"] == scenario["scenario"]
269:                         and row["arm"] == arm
270:                         and row["phase"] == "measured"
271:                         and row["success"] == "true"
272:                     ]
273:                 ),
274:                 "successes": successes.get(key, 0),
275:                 "failures": failures.get(key, 0),
276:             }
277: 
278:     process = {
279:         "run_id": args.run_id,
280:         "started_at_utc": started_at,
281:         "ended_at_utc": utc_now(),
282:         "wall_duration_seconds": time.perf_counter() - wall_started,
283:         "git_commit": git_value("rev-parse", "HEAD"),
284:         "git_status_porcelain": git_value("status", "--porcelain"),
285:         "worktree_dirty": bool(git_value("status", "--porcelain")),
286:         "python": sys.version,
287:         "platform": platform.platform(),
288:         "command": (
289:             "python bench.py --run-id {0} --run-dir {1} --seed {2} "
290:             "--warmups {3} --requests {4} --base-ws {5}{6}"
291:         ).format(
292:             args.run_id,
293:             args.run_dir,
294:             args.seed,
295:             args.warmups,
296:             args.requests,
297:             args.base_ws,
298:             " --capture-stream-sequence" if args.capture_stream_sequence else "",
299:         ),
300:         "secret_handling": "API key supplied in MODELMESH_BENCH_KEY environment variable; not recorded.",
301:         "request_counts": {
302:             "warmup_per_arm": args.warmups,
303:             "measured_per_arm": args.requests,
304:         },
305:         "scenarios": [
306:             {
307:                 "scenario": scenario["scenario"],
308:                 "payload": scenario["payload"],
309:                 "path": str(scenario["path"]),
310:                 "payload_bytes": scenario["path"].stat().st_size,
311:                 "arms": scenario["arms"],
312:             }
313:             for scenario in selected_scenarios
314:         ],
315:         "missing_payloads": missing_payloads,
316:         "measured_arm_summaries": measured,
317:         "rate_limit_failures_429": rate_limit_failures,
318:         "request_failure_count": sum(failures.values()),
319:         "sequence_capture_written": capture_written,
320:     }
321:     (raw_dir / "process.json").write_text(json.dumps(process, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
322:     print(json.dumps(process, indent=2), flush=True)
323: 
324: 
325: def main():
326:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
327:     parser.add_argument("--run-id", required=True)
328:     parser.add_argument("--run-dir", required=True)
329:     parser.add_argument("--seed", required=True, type=int)
330:     parser.add_argument("--base-ws", default="ws://localhost:8003")
331:     parser.add_argument("--warmups", type=int, default=20)
332:     parser.add_argument("--requests", type=int, default=100)
333:     parser.add_argument("--capture-stream-sequence", action="store_true")
334:     args = parser.parse_args()
335:     asyncio.run(run(args))
336: 
337: 
338: if __name__ == "__main__":
339:     main()
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 34: 
 35: ## Payload deviation and worker processing time
 36: 
 37: The requested setup described a small image with an expected processing time of about 2 seconds per job. This initial series instead used the generated 1280×960 image (135,371 bytes); the repository image preserved as `verification/payloads/queue_crash_source_fixture.png` is 360×169 (2,113 bytes). All successful worker processing durations parsed from these eight runs' logs have n=480, median=6,053.5 ms, p95=8,927.55 ms, and spread=9,148 ms (max minus min). Therefore these results do not establish behavior for the requested small-image workload. The corrected fixture series is reported separately.
 38: 
 39: Every trial's raw submissions, polling observations, worker logs, kill/restart evidence, queue snapshots, and per-job outcomes are in its `raw/` directory. `per_job.csv` lists final status, submit-to-terminal time, and count of `Processing job <id>` log lines.
 40:

 85: ### 12_queue_double_sigkill

 87: - Job `1615b4d6-6080-4d96-b0c9-b21b745136b3`: final status `success`, Processing log count 2.
 88:   - `2026-11-23T04:17:36.562227372Z INFO:__main__:Processing job 1615b4d6-6080-4d96-b0c9-b21b745136b3`
 89:   - `2026-11-23T04:17:52.379881334Z INFO:__main__:Processing job 1615b4d6-6080-4d96-b0c9-b21b745136b3`
 90:   - `2026-11-23T04:17:58.963505151Z INFO:__main__:Job 1615b4d6-6080-4d96-b0c9-b21b745136b3 completed successfully (6581ms)`
 91: - Job `9519b3f8-554e-4659-ba8a-e2f6f837214f`: final status `success`, Processing log count 2.
 92:   - `2026-11-23T04:17:44.224854237Z INFO:__main__:Processing job 9519b3f8-554e-4659-ba8a-e2f6f837214f`
 93:   - `2026-11-23T04:18:36.712975255Z INFO:__main__:Processing job 9519b3f8-554e-4659-ba8a-e2f6f837214f`
 94:   - `2026-11-23T04:18:44.761748312Z INFO:__main__:Job 9519b3f8-554e-4659-ba8a-e2f6f837214f completed successfully (8060ms)`
 95: 
 96: 
 97: ## Verdict
 98: 
 99: Across 7 trial(s) with a recorded SIGKILL, the runner sent SIGKILL successfully for 8/8 kill attempt(s). 420/420 accepted jobs succeeded; 0 failed, 0 were still non-terminal, 0 were never visible, and 8 job IDs had more than one `Processing job` log entry.
100: The worker-down trial separately accepted 60/60 jobs while stopped; 60 succeeded after the worker was started.
101: What may be claimed is limited to these trial counts on this local Compose setup and this OCR fixture. The evidence does not justify saying the queue eliminated message-delivery failures in general, nor does a repeated processing log alone prove duplicate results were delivered to a caller.
102: 
103: No percentage performance comparison is made. The time-to-terminal summaries report n, median, p95, and spread as required by the verification rules. Kill-to-last-completion has n=1 for each SIGKILL trial; its p95 and spread are single-observation summaries. The worker-down trial has no kill-to-last value.
104: 
105: ## Method and deviations
106: 
107: - Five trials used RECLAIM_IDLE_MS=15000, intentionally overriding the Compose default of 300000. One trial used the default 300000. One separate trial submitted jobs with the benchmark worker stopped, then started it. One separate trial killed the consumer twice on successive processing jobs.
108: - Each trial attempted 60 POSTs, records each response/job ID, polls every accepted job until terminal or the specified deadline, and parses the isolated benchmark worker's Docker logs for processing attempts.
109: - The benchmark gateway used a scratch PostgreSQL database and Redis DB 1. The regular Compose stack uses its original database and Redis DB 0 and was not used for benchmark job traffic.
110: - The normal Compose stack was initially stopped; its gateway was restarting due to unavailable PostgreSQL DNS. That failed preflight and the subsequent successful `docker compose up -d` recovery are documented under `raw/setup/`. The stack was ready before any trial.
111: - The cleanup field named `normal_gateway_ready_http_status` actually queried the benchmark URL `http://localhost:8003/ready` after the benchmark gateway had been removed, so its connection refusal is not evidence that the normal Compose gateway was down. A later direct check of `http://localhost:8000/ready` at 2026-11-23T04:35:32.703554+00:00 returned HTTP 200 (`{"status":"ready"}`), with the full Compose stack up.
112: - Two prior setup-only attempts stopped before any POST job was submitted: one used a reserved `.invalid` email domain, and one failed to detect a consumer-group log emitted on stderr. Both attempts and their cleanup evidence are preserved in `setup-attempt-01/` and `setup-attempt-02/`; neither was a queue trial or was repeated.
113: - All artifacts are local. Raw API key material and the generated JWT secret were not saved.
114: - Optional limitation: the observations do not test SIGKILL of the whole Docker container; the consumer Python process was SIGKILLed inside a separate worker container, allowing its entrypoint and Docker restart policy to react.

  69: def utc_now():
  70:     return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="milliseconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")

  73: def write_json(path, value):
  74:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  75:     path.write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=2, ensure_ascii=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
  76: 
  77: 
  78: def append_jsonl(path, value):
  79:     with path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
  80:         stream.write(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=True) + "\n")
  81:         stream.flush()

  84: def run_cmd(args, timeout=60, check=False):
  85:     result = subprocess.run(
  86:         [str(arg) for arg in args],
  87:         cwd=REPO,
  88:         capture_output=True,
  89:         text=True,
  90:         encoding="utf-8",
  91:         errors="replace",
  92:         timeout=timeout,
  93:         shell=False,
  94:     )
  95:     if check and result.returncode:
  96:         raise RuntimeError(
  97:             f"Command failed ({result.returncode}): {args}\n"
  98:             f"stdout={result.stdout[-3000:]}\nstderr={result.stderr[-3000:]}"
  99:         )
 100:     return result

 108:     return run_cmd(["docker", "compose", *args], timeout=timeout, check=check)

 114:             "exec", "-T", "postgres", "psql", "-v", "ON_ERROR_STOP=1",
 115:             "-U", "user", "-d", database, "-Atc", sql,

 117:         timeout=60,

 123:     result = compose(
 124:         ["exec", "-T", "redis", "redis-cli", "--raw", "-n", "1", *args],
 125:         timeout=60,
 126:         check=check,
 127:     )
 128:     return result.stdout.strip()

 131: def redis_json(*args):
 132:     result = compose(
 133:         ["exec", "-T", "redis", "redis-cli", "--json", "-n", "1", *args],
 134:         timeout=60,
 135:         check=False,
 136:     )
 137:     text = result.stdout.strip()
 138:     if result.returncode or text.startswith("error:"):
 139:         return None, text or result.stderr.strip()
 140:     try:
 141:         return json.loads(text), ""
 142:     except json.JSONDecodeError:
 143:         return None, text

 146: def http_request(method, path, headers=None, body=None, timeout=20):
 147:     request = urllib.request.Request(
 148:         BASE_URL + path,
 149:         data=body,
 150:         headers=headers or {},
 151:         method=method,
 152:     )
 153:     try:
 154:         with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:
 155:             data = response.read()
 156:             return response.status, data.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
 157:     except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
 158:         data = error.read()
 159:         return error.code, data.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
 160: 
 161: 
 162: def json_request(method, path, value=None, headers=None):
 163:     body = None if value is None else json.dumps(value).encode("utf-8")
 164:     merged = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
 165:     merged.update(headers or {})
 166:     status, text = http_request(method, path, merged, body)
 167:     try:
 168:         data = json.loads(text) if text else {}
 169:     except json.JSONDecodeError:
 170:         data = {"body": text}
 171:     return status, data

 174: def multipart_body(filename, payload):
 175:     boundary = "----ModelMeshQueueCrash" + uuid.uuid4().hex
 176:     header = (
 177:         f"--{boundary}\r\n"
 178:         f'Content-Disposition: form-data; name="file"; filename="{filename}"\r\n'
 179:         "Content-Type: image/png\r\n\r\n"
 180:     ).encode("ascii")
 181:     body = header + payload + f"\r\n--{boundary}--\r\n".encode("ascii")
 182:     return boundary, body

 185: def submit_one(index, payload):
 186:     boundary, body = multipart_body(FIXTURE.name, payload)
 187:     started_utc = utc_now()
 188:     started_mono = time.monotonic()
 189:     try:
 190:         status, text = http_request(
 191:             "POST",
 192:             "/v1/infer/doc-ocr",
 193:             {
 194:                 "Authorization": f"Bearer {API_KEY}",
 195:                 "Content-Type": f"multipart/form-data; boundary={boundary}",
 196:             },
 197:             body,
 198:             timeout=60,
 199:         )
 200:     except Exception as error:
 201:         status, text = 0, f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}"
 202:     try:
 203:         data = json.loads(text) if text else {}
 204:     except json.JSONDecodeError:
 205:         data = {"body": text}
 206:     return {
 207:         "index": index,
 208:         "submit_started_utc": started_utc,
 209:         "submit_started_monotonic": started_mono,
 210:         "response_status": status,
 211:         "job_id": data.get("job_id"),
 212:         "response": data,
 213:         "submit_finished_utc": utc_now(),
 214:         "submit_finished_monotonic": time.monotonic(),
 215:     }

 218: async def submit_jobs(count, payload):
 219:     semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(20)
 220: 
 221:     async def one(index):
 222:         async with semaphore:
 223:             return await asyncio.to_thread(submit_one, index, payload)
 224: 
 225:     return await asyncio.gather(*(one(index) for index in range(1, count + 1)))

 228: def get_status(job_id):
 229:     try:
 230:         status, text = http_request(
 231:             "GET",
 232:             f"/v1/jobs/{job_id}",
 233:             {"Authorization": f"Bearer {API_KEY}"},
 234:             timeout=20,
 235:         )
 236:     except Exception as error:
 237:         return {
 238:             "http_status": 0,
 239:             "body": {"error": f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}"},
 240:             "observed_utc": utc_now(),
 241:         }
 242:     try:
 243:         data = json.loads(text) if text else {}
 244:     except json.JSONDecodeError:
 245:         data = {"body": text}
 246:     return {"http_status": status, "body": data, "observed_utc": utc_now()}

 249: async def poll_all(jobs, log_path, round_number):
 250:     async def one(job):
 251:         return job, await asyncio.to_thread(get_status, job["job_id"])
 252: 
 253:     results = await asyncio.gather(*(one(job) for job in jobs))
 254:     for job, result in results:
 255:         code = result["http_status"]
 256:         body = result["body"]
 257:         status = body.get("status") if code == 200 and isinstance(body, dict) else None
 258:         job["last_http_status"] = code
 259:         job["last_status"] = status
 260:         job["last_observed_utc"] = result["observed_utc"]
 261:         job["last_observed_monotonic"] = time.monotonic()
 262:         job["last_body"] = body
 263:         if code == 404:
 264:             job["not_found_count"] += 1
 265:         elif code == 200:
 266:             job["ever_visible"] = True
 267:             if status and job["first_visible_utc"] is None:
 268:                 job["first_visible_utc"] = result["observed_utc"]
 269:             if status in ("success", "failed") and job["terminal_monotonic"] is None:
 270:                 job["terminal_monotonic"] = job["last_observed_monotonic"]
 271:                 job["terminal_utc"] = result["observed_utc"]
 272:                 job["terminal_status"] = status
 273:                 job["terminal_body"] = body
 274:         append_jsonl(
 275:             log_path,
 276:             {
 277:                 "round": round_number,
 278:                 "job_id": job["job_id"],
 279:                 "http_status": code,
 280:                 "job_status": status,
 281:                 "observed_utc": result["observed_utc"],
 282:                 "body": body if code != 200 or status == "failed" else None,
 283:             },
 284:         )
 285:     return results

 288: def queue_snapshot(label, raw_dir):
 289:     raw_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 290:     stream_len = redis("XLEN", STREAM)
 291:     pending_output = redis("XPENDING", STREAM, GROUP, check=False)
 292:     dlq_len = redis("XLEN", DLQ)
 293:     try:
 294:         pending_count = int(pending_output.splitlines()[0]) if pending_output else 0
 295:     except ValueError:
 296:         pending_count = None
 297:     value = {
 298:         "label": label,
 299:         "captured_at_utc": utc_now(),
 300:         "redis_database": 1,
 301:         "stream_length": int(stream_len) if stream_len.isdigit() else None,
 302:         "pending_count": pending_count,
 303:         "pending_raw": pending_output,
 304:         "dlq_length": int(dlq_len) if dlq_len.isdigit() else None,
 305:     }
 306:     write_json(raw_dir / f"queue-{label}.json", value)
 307:     return value

 310: def inspect_worker():
 311:     result = docker(
 312:         ["inspect", WORKER_CONTAINER, "--format", "{{json .State}}|{{.RestartCount}}|{{.HostConfig.RestartPolicy.Name}}"],
 313:         timeout=15,
 314:         check=False,
 315:     )
 316:     if result.returncode:
 317:         return {"exists": False, "error": result.stderr.strip() or result.stdout.strip()}
 318:     text = result.stdout.strip()
 319:     state_text, restart_count, policy = text.split("|", 2)
 320:     return {
 321:         "exists": True,
 322:         "state": json.loads(state_text),
 323:         "restart_count": int(restart_count),
 324:         "restart_policy": policy,
 325:     }
 326: 
 327: 
 328: def consumer_process_check(container=WORKER_CONTAINER):
 329:     code = (
 330:         "import os\n"
 331:         "matches=[]\n"
 332:         "for p in os.listdir('/proc'):\n"
 333:         "    if not p.isdigit(): continue\n"
 334:         "    try:\n"
 335:         "        command=open('/proc/'+p+'/cmdline','rb').read().split(b'\\0')\n"
 336:         "        if len(command)>1 and command[1]==b'consumer.py': matches.append(int(p))\n"
 337:         "    except OSError: pass\n"
 338:         "print(','.join(map(str,matches)))\n"
 339:         "raise SystemExit(0 if matches else 1)\n"
 340:     )
 341:     result = docker(["exec", container, "python", "-c", code], timeout=15, check=False)
 342:     if result.returncode:
 343:         return []
 344:     return [int(value) for value in result.stdout.strip().split(",") if value.strip().isdigit()]
 345:

 347: def start_worker(idle_ms, raw_dir, reason):
 348:     current = inspect_worker()
 349:     if current.get("exists"):
 350:         if current["state"].get("Running"):
 351:             return current
 352:         docker(["rm", "-f", WORKER_CONTAINER], timeout=30, check=False)
 353:     command = [
 354:         "run", "-d", "--name", WORKER_CONTAINER, "--network", NETWORK,
 355:         "--restart", "on-failure:10",
 356:         "-e", "REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379/1",
 357:         "-e", f"RECLAIM_IDLE_MS={idle_ms}",
 358:         "-e", f"WORKER_NAME={WORKER_NAME}",
 359:         "-e", "STREAM_MAXLEN=10000",
 360:         WORKER_IMAGE,
 361:     ]
 362:     result = docker(command, timeout=90, check=True)
 363:     write_json(
 364:         raw_dir / f"worker-start-{reason}.json",
 365:         {
 366:             "timestamp_utc": utc_now(),
 367:             "command": ["docker", *command],
 368:             "container_id": result.stdout.strip(),
 369:             "reclaim_idle_ms": idle_ms,
 370:             "redis_url": "redis://redis:6379/1",
 371:             "restart_policy": "on-failure:10",
 372:         },
 373:     )
 374:     deadline = time.monotonic() + 120
 375:     while time.monotonic() < deadline:
 376:         state = inspect_worker()
 377:         if state.get("exists") and state["state"].get("Running"):
 378:             log_result = docker(["logs", WORKER_CONTAINER], timeout=15, check=False)
 379:             logs = log_result.stdout + log_result.stderr
 380:             if "consumer group" in logs.lower():
 381:                 return state
 382:         time.sleep(1)
 383:     raise RuntimeError(f"Benchmark worker failed to initialize for {reason}: {inspect_worker()}")
 384: 
 385: 
 386: def kill_consumer(raw_dir, ordinal):
 387:     before = inspect_worker()
 388:     pids = consumer_process_check()
 389:     event = {
 390:         "kill_number": ordinal,
 391:         "requested_at_utc": utc_now(),
 392:         "requested_at_monotonic": time.monotonic(),
 393:         "container_before": before,
 394:         "consumer_pids_before": pids,
 395:         "signal": "SIGKILL",
 396:         "command": "docker exec modelmesh-queue-bench-worker python -c <procfs exact argv consumer.py match; os.kill(pid, SIGKILL)>",
 397:     }
 398:     if not pids:
 399:         event["result"] = "FAILED: no consumer.py PID found"
 400:         write_json(raw_dir / f"kill-{ordinal}.json", event)
 401:         return event
 402:     code = (
 403:         "import os,signal; "
 404:         f"pid={pids[0]}; "
 405:         "c=open('/proc/%d/cmdline'%pid,'rb').read().split(b'\\0'); "
 406:         "assert len(c)>1 and c[1]==b'consumer.py', c; "
 407:         "os.kill(pid,signal.SIGKILL); print('SIGKILL sent to consumer pid %d'%pid)"
 408:     )
 409:     result = docker(["exec", WORKER_CONTAINER, "python", "-c", code], timeout=15, check=False)
 410:     event["command_exit_code"] = result.returncode
 411:     event["stdout"] = result.stdout.strip()
 412:     event["stderr"] = result.stderr.strip()
 413:     event["result"] = "SIGKILL_SENT" if result.returncode == 0 else "FAILED"
 414:     event["sent_at_utc"] = utc_now()
 415:     event["sent_at_monotonic"] = time.monotonic()
 416:     write_json(raw_dir / f"kill-{ordinal}.json", event)
 417:     return event
 418: 
 419: 
 420: def await_consumer_after_kill(raw_dir, ordinal, baseline_restarts):
 421:     deadline = time.monotonic() + 90
 422:     auto_restarted = False
 423:     while time.monotonic() < deadline:
 424:         state = inspect_worker()
 425:         if state.get("exists") and state["state"].get("Running"):
 426:             if consumer_process_check():
 427:                 auto_restarted = state["restart_count"] > baseline_restarts
 428:                 event = {
 429:                     "kill_number": ordinal,
 430:                     "observed_at_utc": utc_now(),
 431:                     "auto_restarted": auto_restarted,
 432:                     "manual_start_used": False,
 433:                     "container": state,
 434:                 }
 435:                 write_json(raw_dir / f"restart-{ordinal}.json", event)
 436:                 return event
 437:         time.sleep(1)
 438:     manual = docker(["start", WORKER_CONTAINER], timeout=30, check=False)
 439:     event = {
 440:         "kill_number": ordinal,
 441:         "observed_at_utc": utc_now(),
 442:         "auto_restarted": False,
 443:         "manual_start_used": manual.returncode == 0,
 444:         "manual_start_exit_code": manual.returncode,
 445:         "manual_start_stdout": manual.stdout.strip(),
 446:         "manual_start_stderr": manual.stderr.strip(),
 447:         "container": inspect_worker(),
 448:     }
 449:     write_json(raw_dir / f"restart-{ordinal}.json", event)
 450:     deadline = time.monotonic() + 60
 451:     while time.monotonic() < deadline:
 452:         if consumer_process_check():
 453:             return event
 454:         time.sleep(1)
 455:     event["consumer_available_after_manual_start"] = False
 456:     write_json(raw_dir / f"restart-{ordinal}.json", event)
 457:     return event
