[PY]  verification\runs\15_retry_backoff_dlq_corrected\raw\historical_source\asr_consumer.py
================================================================================
  1: import asyncio
  2: import json
  3: import logging
  4: import os
  5: import time
  6: from redis.asyncio import Redis
  7: from inference import transcribe
  8: 
  9: logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
 10: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
 11: 
 12: WORKER_NAME = "asr-worker"
 13: REDIS_URL = os.getenv("REDIS_URL", "redis://redis:6379")
 14: MAX_RETRIES = 3
 15: RETRY_DELAY_BASE = 2  # seconds
 16: PAYLOAD_TTL = int(os.getenv("PAYLOAD_TTL_SECONDS", "3600"))
 17: STREAM_MAXLEN = int(os.getenv("STREAM_MAXLEN", "10000"))
 18: 
 19: 
 20: async def update_job_status(redis: Redis, job_id: str, status: str, result: dict = None, error: str = None):
 21:     """Update job status in Redis."""
 22:     data = {"job_id": job_id, "status": status}
 23: 
 24:     if result:
 25:         data["result"] = result
 26: 
 27:     if error:
 28:         data["error"] = error
 29: 
 30:     if status in ("success", "failed"):
 31:         await redis.setex(f"result:{job_id}", 7200, json.dumps(data))
 32:         await redis.delete(f"job:{job_id}:status")
 33:     else:
 34:         await redis.setex(f"job:{job_id}:status", 3600, json.dumps(data))
 35: 
 36: 
 37: async def handle_job(redis: Redis, job_data: dict, entry_id: str) -> bool:
 38:     """
 39:     Process a single job.
 40: 
 41:     Returns:
 42:         bool: True if successful, False if should retry
 43:     """
 44:     job_id = job_data["job_id"]
 45:     payload = job_data.get("payload") or await redis.get(f"payload:{job_id}")
 46:     if payload is None:
 47:         raise Exception("payload expired")
 48: 
 49:     try:
 50:         logger.info(f"Processing job {job_id}")
 51:         await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "processing")
 52: 
 53:         # Run inference
 54:         result = transcribe(payload)
 55: 
 56:         # Store result
 57:         await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "success", result=result)
 58:         logger.info(f"Job {job_id} completed successfully")
 59:         return True
 60: 
 61:     except Exception as e:
 62:         logger.error(f"Job {job_id} failed: {e}")
 63:         return False
 64: 
 65: 
 66: async def handle_failure(redis: Redis, job_data: dict, entry_id: str, group: str, error: Exception):
 67:     """Handle job failure with retry logic and dead-letter queue."""
 68:     job_id = job_data["job_id"]
 69:     retry_count = int(job_data.get("retry_count", 0))
 70: 
 71:     if retry_count < MAX_RETRIES:
 72:         # Retry with exponential backoff
 73:         retry_count += 1
 74:         delay = RETRY_DELAY_BASE ** retry_count
 75:         logger.warning(f"Job {job_id} retry {retry_count}/{MAX_RETRIES} after {delay}s")
 76: 
 77:         await asyncio.sleep(delay)
 78: 
 79:         # Re-enqueue with updated retry count
 80:         job_data["retry_count"] = str(retry_count)
 81:         await redis.xadd(
 82:             "inference_jobs",
 83:             job_data,
 84:             maxlen=STREAM_MAXLEN,
 85:             approximate=True,
 86:         )
 87: 
 88:         # Acknowledge original message
 89:         await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, entry_id)
 90:     else:
 91:         # Move to dead-letter queue
 92:         logger.error(f"Job {job_id} exceeded max retries, moving to DLQ")
 93:         job_data["dlq_reason"] = str(error)
 94:         job_data["dlq_timestamp"] = str(int(time.time()))
 95:         await redis.xadd(
 96:             "inference_jobs_dlq",
 97:             job_data,
 98:             maxlen=1000,
 99:             approximate=True,
100:         )
101: 
102:         # Mark as failed
103:         await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "failed", error=str(error))
104: 
105:         # Acknowledge original message
106:         await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, entry_id)
107: 
108: 
109: async def log_consumer_lag(redis: Redis, group: str):
110:     """Log consumer lag metrics for monitoring."""
111:     try:
112:         stream_length = await redis.xlen("inference_jobs")
113:         pending_info = await redis.xpending("inference_jobs", group)
114: 
115:         if pending_info:
116:             pending_count = pending_info["pending"]
117:             logger.info(f"[{WORKER_NAME}] Stream length: {stream_length}, Pending: {pending_count}")
118:             if stream_length > STREAM_MAXLEN * 0.8:
119:                 logger.warning(
120:                     f"[{WORKER_NAME}] Stream length {stream_length} exceeds 80% of limit {STREAM_MAXLEN}"
121:                 )
122:     except Exception as e:
123:         logger.debug(f"Could not fetch lag metrics: {e}")
124: 
125: 
126: async def consume_loop():
127:     """Main consumer loop for Redis Streams."""
128:     redis = Redis.from_url(REDIS_URL, decode_responses=False)
129:     group = f"{WORKER_NAME}_group"
130: 
131:     try:
132:         # Create consumer group if it doesn't exist
133:         try:
134:             await redis.xgroup_create("inference_jobs", group, id="0", mkstream=True)
135:             logger.info(f"Created consumer group: {group}")
136:         except Exception:
137:             logger.info(f"Consumer group {group} already exists")
138: 
139:         logger.info(f"Starting consumer loop for {WORKER_NAME}")
140:         lag_counter = 0
141: 
142:         while True:
143:             try:
144:                 # Read messages from the stream
145:                 messages = await redis.xreadgroup(
146:                     group,
147:                     f"{WORKER_NAME}-1",
148:                     {"inference_jobs": ">"},
149:                     count=1,
150:                     block=5000
151:                 )
152: 
153:                 # Log consumer lag periodically (every 10 iterations)
154:                 lag_counter += 1
155:                 if lag_counter >= 10:
156:                     await log_consumer_lag(redis, group)
157:                     lag_counter = 0
158: 
159:                 if not messages:
160:                     continue
161: 
162:                 for stream, entries in messages:
163:                     for entry_id, fields in entries:
164:                         # Decode fields
165:                         job_data = {
166:                             k.decode() if isinstance(k, bytes) else k:
167:                             v.decode() if isinstance(v, bytes) and k.decode() != "payload" else v
168:                             for k, v in fields.items()
169:                         }
170: 
171:                 # Note: we filter by worker_name, not model status. A job enqueued before
172:                 # the model was disabled will still process to completion. Disabling a model
173:                 # stops new jobs from being accepted, not in-flight ones.
174:                 # Filter by worker_name
175:                         if job_data.get("worker_name") != WORKER_NAME:
176:                             logger.debug(f"Skipping job for different worker: {job_data.get('worker_name')}")
177:                             await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, entry_id)
178:                             continue
179: 
180:                         # Process job
181:                         success = await handle_job(redis, job_data, entry_id)
182: 
183:                         if success:
184:                             # Acknowledge successful processing
185:                             await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, entry_id)
186:                         else:
187:                             # Handle failure with retry logic
188:                             await handle_failure(redis, job_data, entry_id, group, Exception("Processing failed"))
189: 
190:             except asyncio.CancelledError:
191:                 logger.info("Consumer loop cancelled")
192:                 break
193:             except Exception as e:
194:                 logger.error(f"Error in consumer loop: {e}")
195:                 await asyncio.sleep(1)
196: 
197:     finally:
198:         await redis.close()
199: 
200: 
201: if __name__ == "__main__":
202:     asyncio.run(consume_loop())
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  1: import asyncio
  2: import json
  3: import logging
  4: import os
  5: import time
  6: from redis.asyncio import Redis
  7: from inference import extract_text
  8: from metrics import log_metric
  9: 
 10: logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
 11: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
 12: 
 13: WORKER_NAME = "doc-ocr-worker"
 14: REDIS_URL = os.getenv("REDIS_URL", "redis://redis:6379")
 15: MAX_RETRIES = 3
 16: RETRY_DELAY_BASE = 2  # seconds
 17: PAYLOAD_TTL = int(os.getenv("PAYLOAD_TTL_SECONDS", "3600"))
 18: STREAM_MAXLEN = int(os.getenv("STREAM_MAXLEN", "10000"))
 19: 
 20: 
 21: async def update_job_status(redis: Redis, job_id: str, status: str, result: dict = None, error: str = None):
 22:     """Update job status in Redis."""
 23:     data = {"job_id": job_id, "status": status}
 24: 
 25:     if result:
 26:         data["result"] = result
 27: 
 28:     if error:
 29:         data["error"] = error
 30: 
 31:     if status in ("success", "failed"):
 32:         await redis.setex(f"result:{job_id}", 7200, json.dumps(data))
 33:         await redis.delete(f"job:{job_id}:status")
 34:     else:
 35:         await redis.setex(f"job:{job_id}:status", 3600, json.dumps(data))
 36: 
 37: 
 38: async def handle_job(redis: Redis, job_data: dict, entry_id: str) -> bool:
 39:     job_id = job_data["job_id"]
 40:     payload = job_data.get("payload") or await redis.get(f"payload:{job_id}")
 41:     if payload is None:
 42:         raise Exception("payload expired")
 43:     model_id = job_data.get("model_id", "")
 44:     model_name = job_data.get("model_name", "")
 45:     model_version = job_data.get("model_version", "")
 46: 
 47:     try:
 48:         logger.info(f"Processing job {job_id}")
 49:         await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "processing")
 50: 
 51:         start = time.perf_counter()
 52:         result = extract_text(payload)
 53:         latency_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000)
 54: 
 55:         await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "success", result=result)
 56:         log_metric(job_id, model_id, model_name, model_version, WORKER_NAME, "success", latency_ms)
 57:         logger.info(f"Job {job_id} completed successfully ({latency_ms}ms)")
 58:         return True
 59: 
 60:     except Exception as e:
 61:         logger.error(f"Job {job_id} failed: {e}")
 62:         log_metric(job_id, model_id, model_name, model_version, WORKER_NAME, "error", 0)
 63:         return False
 64: 
 65: 
 66: async def handle_failure(redis: Redis, job_data: dict, entry_id: str, group: str, error: Exception):
 67:     """Handle job failure with retry logic and dead-letter queue."""

 69:     retry_count = int(job_data.get("retry_count", 0))
 70: 
 71:     if retry_count < MAX_RETRIES:
 72:         # Retry with exponential backoff
 73:         retry_count += 1
 74:         delay = RETRY_DELAY_BASE ** retry_count
 75:         logger.warning(f"Job {job_id} retry {retry_count}/{MAX_RETRIES} after {delay}s")
 76: 
 77:         await asyncio.sleep(delay)
 78: 
 79:         # Re-enqueue with updated retry count
 80:         job_data["retry_count"] = str(retry_count)
 81:         await redis.xadd(
 82:             "inference_jobs",
 83:             job_data,
 84:             maxlen=STREAM_MAXLEN,
 85:             approximate=True,
 86:         )
 87: 
 88:         # Acknowledge original message
 89:         await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, entry_id)
 90:     else:
 91:         # Move to dead-letter queue

 97:             job_data,

154:                 lag_counter += 1
155:                 if lag_counter >= 10:
156:                     await log_consumer_lag(redis, group)
157:                     lag_counter = 0
158: 
159:                 if not messages:
160:                     continue

162:                 for stream, entries in messages:
163:                     for entry_id, fields in entries:
164:                         # Decode fields
165:                         job_data = {

167:                             v.decode() if isinstance(v, bytes) and k.decode() != "payload" else v
168:                             for k, v in fields.items()
169:                         }
170: 
171:                         # Note: we filter by worker_name, not model status. A job enqueued before
172:                 # the model was disabled will still process to completion. Disabling a model
173:                 # stops new jobs from being accepted, not in-flight ones.
174:                 # Filter by worker_name
175:                         if job_data.get("worker_name") != WORKER_NAME:
176:                             logger.debug(f"Skipping job for different worker: {job_data.get('worker_name')}")
177:                             await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, entry_id)
178:                             continue
179: 
180:                         # Process job
181:                         success = await handle_job(redis, job_data, entry_id)
182: 
183:                         if success:
184:                             # Acknowledge successful processing
185:                             await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, entry_id)
186:                         else:
187:                             # Handle failure with retry logic
188:                             await handle_failure(redis, job_data, entry_id, group, Exception("Processing failed"))
189: 
190:             except asyncio.CancelledError:
191:                 logger.info("Consumer loop cancelled")
192:                 break
193:             except Exception as e:
194:                 logger.error(f"Error in consumer loop: {e}")
195:                 await asyncio.sleep(1)
196: 
197:     finally:
198:         await redis.close()
199: 
200: 
201: if __name__ == "__main__":
202:     asyncio.run(consume_loop())
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[MD]  verification\runs\19_registry_latency_canary\README.md

  1: # Registry latency, canary, and live-update verification
  2: 
  3: **Scope:** local single-machine measurements from a temporary scratch gateway built from the current worktree, attached to the running Docker Compose network. The request generator ran on the same Windows host as Docker Desktop. These results are not a multi-host or production capacity claim.
  4: 
  5: ## Environment and run
  6: 
  7: - Windows 11, Docker Desktop; Intel Core i5-1340P, 12 physical / 16 logical cores; 16,025 MB host RAM.
  8: - Docker Engine 29.9.2; daemon reported 16 CPUs and 8,139,243,520 bytes memory. Compose/PostgreSQL/Redis versions are recorded in [00_environment.md](../../00_environment.md). No Compose container CPU or memory limits were set.
  9: - The full Compose stack was running; `/ready` returned 200 before and after the benchmark. The scratch gateway used the temporary database `modelmesh_registry_verify_20261123_19` and Redis DB 1. Redis DB 1 started empty; Redis reported `maxmemory=0`, `noeviction`.
 10: - Other processes in the preflight snapshot included VS Code (`Code`), `copilot-runtime`, Docker Desktop/backend, and Task Manager. They were not continuously profiled.
 11: - Commit `d6fd970b76ed5e0a747be709e0f62e7a009d31da`; worktree dirty. Pre-existing changes were not reverted or edited by this benchmark.
 12: - Exact run: `python verification\registry_canary_bench.py --raw-dir verification\runs\19_registry_latency_canary\raw --database-name modelmesh_registry_verify_20261123_19 --base-url http://127.0.0.1:8003 --rates 10 50 100 --duration 60 --repetitions 3 --canary-samples 2000 --canary-rate 100`
 13: - Start `2026-11-23T07:23:42.6050362Z`; end `2026-11-23T07:49:30.0919146Z`; exit code 0.
 14: 
 15: The scratch gateway was built from the current worktree and used the same app source, but was intentionally isolated from the existing gateway database and Redis DB 0. The regular Compose gateway remained running with the same container ID throughout Part C. The load generator shared the host with Docker Desktop, so the latency/CPU numbers below are local figures.
 16: 
 17: ## Part A — enqueue and registry-only latency
 18: 
 19: Each target rate ran three separate 60-second enqueue runs and three 60-second `GET /v1/models/bench-null?version=v1` reference runs. Enqueue used a 102,400-byte payload and 20 round-robin API keys, each set to 1,000 requests/minute (the accepted maximum). Actual observed request rates ranged from 10.02–10.03, 50.05–50.07, and 100.06–100.11 requests/s, respectively. All 18 runs were valid: 100% HTTP 200; no 401, 422, 429, or 500 responses. Pooled latency includes all successful requests across the three repetitions; per-run distributions are in the raw JSON.
 20: 
 21: | Path | Target req/s | Runs | Pooled n | p50 ms | p95 ms | p99 ms | max ms | spread ms | Statuses |
 22: |---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
 23: | Enqueue | 10 | 3 | 1,800 | 17.81 | 80.87 | 169.06 | 1,107.40 | 1,102.94 | 1,800 × 200 |
 24: | Enqueue | 50 | 3 | 9,000 | 15.35 | 44.90 | 268.90 | 786.87 | 783.06 | 9,000 × 200 |
 25: | Enqueue | 100 | 3 | 18,000 | 22.09 | 76.39 | 218.53 | 751.42 | 746.96 | 18,000 × 200 |
 26: | Registry GET | 10 | 3 | 1,800 | 6.79 | 30.51 | 65.17 | 138.79 | 136.59 | 1,800 × 200 |
 27: | Registry GET | 50 | 3 | 9,000 | 6.94 | 12.11 | 26.10 | 87.68 | 85.91 | 9,000 × 200 |
 28: | Registry GET | 100 | 3 | 18,000 | 8.29 | 23.44 | 72.78 | 321.76 | 319.87 | 18,000 × 200 |
 29: 
 30: CPU samples below are sampled means / p95 percentages across the three runs (sample counts 58–60 per row). These are not CPU allocations or isolated load-generator CPU.
 31: 
 32: | Path | Target req/s | Host CPU mean / p95 | Gateway CPU mean / p95 | Redis CPU mean / p95 | PostgreSQL CPU mean / p95 |
 33: |---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
 34: | Enqueue | 10 | 18.82% / 33.41% | 8.41% / 10.72% | 5.76% / 12.29% | 2.83% / 10.71% |
 35: | Enqueue | 50 | 26.90% / 55.96% | 34.97% / 43.79% | 10.20% / 17.60% | 6.25% / 13.06% |
 36: | Enqueue | 100 | 24.01% / 26.66% | 53.91% / 65.70% | 13.01% / 18.17% | 9.58% / 16.60% |

 39: | Registry GET | 100 | 20.55% / 27.56% | 19.26% / 25.61% | 6.32% / 11.58% | 6.38% / 15.58% |
 40: 
 41: Redis sampled peak `used_memory` for enqueue was 71,271,872 bytes at 10 req/s, 348,838,048 bytes at 50 req/s, and 689,315,352 bytes at 100 req/s. All stayed below the 900,000,000-byte safety threshold. Each Part A run cleaned the `bench-null` stream entries, job payload/status keys, and test rate-limit keys; Redis DB 1 returned to zero keys after every run. OCR/ASR worker CPU remained low in these dummy-worker tests (sampled maxima 2.34% and 0.80%, respectively).
 42: 
 43: **Part A verdict:** All 18 required 60-second runs completed with 100% HTTP 200 and actual rates close to target. The enqueue path shows substantial tail outliers (including the measured 1,107.40 ms maximum); no outliers were discarded.
 44: 
 45: ## Part B — canary selection and pinning
 46: 
 47: Each unpinned sample used 2,000 successful requests. The p-values are Pearson chi-square goodness-of-fit tests against the configured canary fraction (df=1, alpha=0.05).
 48: 
 49: | Versions | v2/v10 canary % | Expected stable / canary | Observed stable / canary | p-value | Statuses |
 50: |---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
 51: | v1 / v2 | 10% | 1,800 / 200 | 1,781 / 219 | 0.1567 | 2,000 × 200 |
 52: | v1 / v2 | 30% | 1,400 / 600 | 1,410 / 590 | 0.6256 | 2,000 × 200 |
 53: | v1 / v2 | 50% | 1,000 / 1,000 | 1,002 / 998 | 0.9287 | 2,000 × 200 |
 54: | v1 / v2 / v9 / v10 | 50% on v10 | 1,000 / 1,000 | 981 / 1,019 | 0.3955 | 2,000 × 200 |
 55: 
 56: In the four-version sample, v2 and v9 each received zero requests. Explicit `?version=` pinning returned the requested version for every request: v1 50/50, v2 50/50, v9 50/50, and v10 50/50. Each pinned request returned HTTP 200.
 57: 
 58: **Part B verdict:** The observed proportions were statistically consistent with 10%, 30%, and 50% canary selection at alpha 0.05; the highest-numbered version pair received traffic, the two middle versions received none, and all tested version pins were honored.
 59: 
 60: ## Part C — live registry changes without redeploy
 61: 
 62: The continuous HTTP loop observed 108 requests at 20.09 req/s (107 HTTP 200; one expected HTTP 503 while both canary versions were disabled). For each PATCH, delay is from PATCH response completion to the first response demonstrating the new behavior; each is one observation (`n=1`), so p50=p95 and spread is zero.
 63: 
 64: | PATCH | First observed behavior | Requests after PATCH response through observation | Delay ms (n=1; p50 / p95 / spread) |
 65: |---|---|---:|---:|
 66: | canary 0 → 100 | v2 returned | 1 | 15.82 / 15.82 / 0 |
 67: | Disable v2 | v1 returned | 1 | 42.17 / 42.17 / 0 |
 68: | Disable v1 (both disabled) | HTTP 503 | 1 | 52.44 / 52.44 / 0 |
 69: | Re-enable v1 | v1 returned | 1 | 40.73 / 40.73 / 0 |
 70: | Re-enable v2 | v2 returned | 1 | 11.68 / 11.68 / 0 |
 71: | WebSocket model protocol/endpoint PATCH | `ready.protocol=http`; backend returned `/probe/new` | 1 WebSocket request | 48.50 / 48.50 / 0 |
 72: 
 73: The WebSocket check sent 40 probes at a target 20 req/s. WebSocket request latency was `n=40`, p50 35.16 ms, p95 44.90 ms, p99 50.01 ms, max 51.61 ms, spread 30.60 ms. Its pre-PATCH ready handshake reported `protocol=grpc` (`n=1`, p50 23.69 ms, p95 23.69 ms, spread 0 ms); the first post-PATCH request reported `protocol=http` and `/probe/new`. The HTTP loop continued during the WebSocket probes (39 loop requests started after the endpoint PATCH response).
 74: 
 75: Both the temporary scratch gateway and the existing Compose gateway retained their container IDs throughout Part C. The original Compose registry snapshot was identical before and after the campaign (`original_registry_unchanged=true`).
 76: 
 77: All six PATCH calls returned HTTP 200. No 429 response occurred in Part C.
 78: 
 79: **Part C verdict:** All five HTTP registry transitions and the WebSocket protocol/endpoint change were observed without restarting either gateway container; the recorded delays are single observations on this local host, not a propagation guarantee.
 80: 
 81: ## Cleanup and limitations
 82: 
 83: All six scratch benchmark models were disabled, Redis DB 1 was empty, the scratch database and temporary containers were removed, and the original Compose `/ready` endpoint remained HTTP 200. Redis process-wide `used_memory` was 1,830,072 bytes before and 1,871,912 bytes after external cleanup (+41,840 bytes); DB 1 had zero keys, but process memory was not byte-identical to preflight. Redis settings were `maxmemory=0` and `noeviction`.
 84: 
 85: These are local single-machine numbers. Host process activity was only sampled before the run, not continuously monitored. The enqueue model used a fixed 100 KiB payload and a worker no consumer processes; these measurements isolate enqueue/auth/registry/Redis behavior and do not measure worker completion latency or production capacity.
 86: 
 87: Earlier failed harness pilots and the reduced successful pilot are preserved, but excluded: [run 16](../16_registry_canary_pilot/README.md), [run 17](../17_registry_canary_pilot_retry/README.md), [run 18](../18_registry_canary_pilot_complete/README.md). Request-level evidence and commands for this campaign are in [raw/](./raw/).

 17: ## Exact commands
 18: 
 19: ```powershell
 20: docker compose ps
 21: curl.exe -sS -o NUL -w "%{http_code}\n" http://127.0.0.1:8000/ready
 22: docker build -t modelmesh-gateway:registry-bench -f gateway\Dockerfile .
 23: docker compose exec -T postgres psql -U user -d modelmesh -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -c "CREATE DATABASE modelmesh_registry_verify_20261123_19"
 24: Get-Content -Raw db\init.sql | docker compose exec -T postgres psql -U user -d modelmesh_registry_verify_20261123_19 -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1
 25: docker run -d --name verify-registry-gateway --network modelmesh_default -p 8003:8000 -e DATABASE_URL=postgresql://user:pass@postgres/modelmesh_registry_verify_20261123_19 -e REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379/1 -e JWT_SECRET=verification-only-secret -e MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES=10485760 -e PAYLOAD_TTL_SECONDS=3600 -e STREAM_MAXLEN=10000 modelmesh-gateway:registry-bench
 26: docker create --name verify-registry-endpoint --network modelmesh_default modelmesh-gateway:registry-bench python -u /tmp/http_endpoint_probe.py
 27: docker cp verification\http_endpoint_probe.py verify-registry-endpoint:/tmp/http_endpoint_probe.py
 28: docker start verify-registry-endpoint
 29: python verification\registry_canary_bench.py --raw-dir verification\runs\19_registry_latency_canary\raw --database-name modelmesh_registry_verify_20261123_19 --base-url http://127.0.0.1:8003 --rates 10 50 100 --duration 60 --repetitions 3 --canary-samples 2000 --canary-rate 100
 30: docker logs --timestamps verify-registry-gateway
 31: docker logs --timestamps verify-registry-endpoint
 32: docker rm -f verify-registry-endpoint verify-registry-gateway
 33: docker compose exec -T postgres psql -U user -d modelmesh -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -c "DROP DATABASE modelmesh_registry_verify_20261123_19 WITH (FORCE)"
 34: docker image rm modelmesh-gateway:registry-bench
 35: ```
 36: 
 37: The image build was performed before this full campaign and reused; its build output and ID are in the session history and `container_ids.txt`. The benchmark command and timestamps are also captured in `execution.txt`.
 38: 
 39: ## Evidence and cleanup
 40: 
 41: - Request-level records, per-run summaries, run order, exact 100 KiB payload, resource CSVs, Part B statistical results, and Part C request records are under this `raw/` directory.
 42: - `gateway.log` and `endpoint_probe.log` preserve container output. `stdout.log` contains the completed campaign JSON; `stderr.log` is empty.
 43: - The runner disabled all six temporary benchmark models, deleted 20 Redis rate-limit keys, and verified Redis DB 1 had zero keys. The external cleanup removed the temporary gateway/probe containers and scratch database; the main Compose `/ready` endpoint remained HTTP 200.
 44: - The runner operated on Redis DB 1 only. Final host checks recorded Redis DB 0 `DBSIZE=2`, Redis DB 1 `DBSIZE=0`, and Compose `/ready` HTTP 200 in `final_host_checks.txt`.
 45: - Original Compose registry snapshot before/after is in `campaign_summary.json`; equality was `true`.
 46: - Redis DB 1 was empty after cleanup. Redis process-wide `used_memory` was 1,830,072 bytes at preflight, 1,900,024 bytes after the runner cleanup, and 1,871,912 bytes after removing the temporary resources. The last value is 41,840 bytes above preflight, so it was close but not byte-identical; these are process-wide values, not DB1-only memory.
 47: - Earlier harness failures and the reduced successful smoke pilot remain preserved under runs 16–18. None of their measurements are included in run 19 results.

[PY]  verification\scripts\generate_page_payload.py

  1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
  2: """Render a deterministic page-like OCR payload for the verification benchmark."""
  3: 
  4: from pathlib import Path
  5: import random
  6: 
  7: from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
  8: 
  9: 
 10: ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
 11: OUTPUT = ROOT / "verification" / "payloads" / "page_like.jpg"
 12: WIDTH, HEIGHT = 2550, 3300
 13: 
 14: 
 15: def find_font():
 16:     candidates = [
 17:         Path(r"C:\Windows\Fonts\arial.ttf"),
 18:         Path(r"C:\Windows\Fonts\arialbd.ttf"),
 19:     ]
 20:     for candidate in candidates:
 21:         if candidate.exists():
 22:             return candidate
 23:     return None
 24: 
 25: 
 26: def main():
 27:     OUTPUT.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 28:     rng = random.Random(20261122)
 29:     base = Image.new("L", (WIDTH, HEIGHT), 250)
 30:     noise = Image.effect_noise((WIDTH, HEIGHT), 9).convert("L")
 31:     page = Image.blend(base, noise, 0.22).convert("RGB")
 32:     draw = ImageDraw.Draw(page)
 33:     font_path = find_font()
 34: 
 35:     def font(size, bold=False):
 36:         path = Path(r"C:\Windows\Fonts\arialbd.ttf" if bold else r"C:\Windows\Fonts\arial.ttf")
 37:         if path.exists():
 38:             return ImageFont.truetype(str(path), size)
 39:         return ImageFont.load_default()
 40: 
 41:     margin = 180
 42:     title = font(84, True)
 43:     heading = font(48, True)
 44:     body = font(31)
 45:     small = font(26)
 46:     ink = (28, 36, 44)
 47:     muted = (82, 91, 101)
 48:     draw.text((margin, 130), "NORTHWIND OPERATIONS", fill=ink, font=heading)
 49:     draw.text((WIDTH - 620, 145), "SERVICE REVIEW  |  2026", fill=muted, font=small)
 50:     draw.line((margin, 225, WIDTH - margin, 225), fill=(120, 130, 138), width=4)
 51:     draw.text((margin, 300), "Quarterly Service Summary", fill=ink, font=title)
 52:     draw.text((margin, 440), "Reference: NW-26-Q3-0417     Prepared: 22 November 2026", fill=muted, font=body)
 53: 
 54:     y = 560
 55:     paragraphs = [
 56:         "This page summarizes operational activity across the regional document and speech processing service.",
 57:         "The review compares submitted volume, processing time, and quality checks for the reporting period.",
 58:         "All figures below are illustrative verification data created locally for an OCR payload-size test.",
 59:     ]
 60:     for paragraph in paragraphs:
 61:         draw.text((margin, y), paragraph, fill=ink, font=body)
 62:         y += 58
 63: 
 64:     for section in range(1, 7):
 65:         y += 36
 66:         draw.text((margin, y), f"{section}. Regional Processing Detail", fill=ink, font=heading)
 67:         y += 78
 68:         for row in range(6):
 69:             number = (section - 1) * 6 + row + 1
 70:             day = 3 + (number * 3) % 25
 71:             volume = 840 + ((number * 137) % 1700)
 72:             median = 420 + ((number * 83) % 950)
 73:             confidence = 0.91 + (number % 8) * 0.01
 74:             sentence = (
 75:                 f"Entry {number:02d} - Region {chr(65 + (number % 5))}: "
 76:                 f"{volume:,} documents reviewed on 20{26:02d}-11-{day:02d}; "
 77:                 f"median service time {median} ms; quality score {confidence:.2f}."
 78:             )
 79:             draw.text((margin + 18, y), sentence, fill=ink, font=body)
 80:             y += 51
 81:             if y > HEIGHT - 330:
 82:                 break
 83:         if section == 2:
 84:             y += 20
 85:             table_top = y
 86:             col_x = [margin, margin + 700, margin + 1370, margin + 1930]
 87:             headers = ["Service", "Requests", "Median (ms)", "Status"]
 88:             values = [
 89:                 ["Document OCR", "18,420", "612", "Within target"],
 90:                 ["Speech ASR", "9,815", "1,204", "Within target"],
 91:                 ["Queue delivery", "28,235", "84", "Reviewed"],
 92:             ]
 93:             for i, header in enumerate(headers):
 94:                 draw.rectangle((col_x[i], table_top, col_x[i] + 550, table_top + 62), outline=(80, 90, 100), width=2)
 95:                 draw.text((col_x[i] + 10, table_top + 12), header, fill=ink, font=small)
 96:             for r, value_row in enumerate(values, 1):
 97:                 for i, value in enumerate(value_row):
 98:                     top = table_top + r * 62
 99:                     draw.rectangle((col_x[i], top, col_x[i] + 550, top + 62), outline=(120, 130, 138), width=1)
100:                     draw.text((col_x[i] + 10, top + 13), value, fill=ink, font=small)
101:             y = table_top + 280
102:         if y > HEIGHT - 250:
103:             break
104:     draw.line((margin, HEIGHT - 190, WIDTH - margin, HEIGHT - 190), fill=(120, 130, 138), width=3)
105:     draw.text((margin, HEIGHT - 155), "Northwind Operations - Internal review copy", fill=muted, font=small)
106:     draw.text((WIDTH - 450, HEIGHT - 155), f"Page 1 of {rng.randint(4, 8)}", fill=muted, font=small)
107: 
108:     page.save(OUTPUT, format="JPEG", quality=90, subsampling=0, optimize=True, dpi=(300, 300))
109:     print(
110:         f"output={OUTPUT}\nsize_bytes={OUTPUT.stat().st_size}\n"
111:         f"dimensions={WIDTH}x{HEIGHT}\nformat=JPEG quality=90 dpi=300\n"
112:         f"font={'Arial' if font_path else 'Pillow default'}"
113:     )
114: 
115: 
116: if __name__ == "__main__":
117:     main()

[PY]  workers\asr\consumer.py
================================================================================
  1: import asyncio
  2: import json
  3: import logging
  4: import os
  5: import time
  6: from redis.asyncio import Redis
  7: from inference import transcribe, _load_model
  8: from metrics import log_metric
  9: 
 10: logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
 11: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
 12: 
 13: WORKER_NAME = os.getenv("WORKER_NAME", "asr-worker")
 14: CONSUMER = f"{WORKER_NAME}-{os.getenv('HOSTNAME', '1')}"
 15: RECLAIM_IDLE_MS = int(os.getenv("RECLAIM_IDLE_MS", "300000"))
 16: REDIS_URL = os.getenv("REDIS_URL", "redis://redis:6379")
 17: MAX_RETRIES = 3
 18: RETRY_DELAY_BASE = 2
 19: STREAM_MAXLEN = int(os.getenv("STREAM_MAXLEN", "10000"))
 20: MAINTENANCE_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 30.0
 21: 
 22: 
 23: class PermanentError(Exception):
 24:     """Input that retrying cannot fix."""
 25: 
 26: 
 27: async def update_job_status(redis: Redis, job_id: str, status: str, result: dict = None, error: str = None):
 28:     data = {"job_id": job_id, "status": status}
 29:     if result:
 30:         data["result"] = result
 31:     if error:
 32:         data["error"] = error
 33:     if status in ("success", "failed"):
 34:         await redis.setex(f"result:{job_id}", 7200, json.dumps(data))
 35:         await redis.delete(f"job:{job_id}:status")
 36:     else:
 37:         await redis.setex(f"job:{job_id}:status", 3600, json.dumps(data))
 38: 
 39: 
 40: def decode_fields(fields):
 41:     decoded = {}
 42:     for key, value in fields.items():
 43:         k = key.decode() if isinstance(key, bytes) else key
 44:         if k == "payload":
 45:             decoded[k] = value
 46:         elif isinstance(value, bytes):
 47:             decoded[k] = value.decode()
 48:         else:
 49:             decoded[k] = value
 50:     return decoded
 51: 
 52: 
 53: async def handle_job(redis: Redis, job_data: dict) -> tuple[Exception | None, int]:
 54:     start = time.monotonic()
 55:     job_id = job_data["job_id"]
 56:     try:
 57:         logger.info(f"Processing job {job_id}")
 58:         await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "processing")
 59:         payload = job_data.get("payload") or await redis.get(f"payload:{job_id}")
 60:         if payload is None:
 61:             return PermanentError("payload expired"), int((time.monotonic() - start) * 1000)
 62:         inference_start = time.monotonic()
 63:         result = transcribe(payload)
 64:         elapsed_ms = int((time.monotonic() - inference_start) * 1000)
 65:         await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "success", result=result)
 66:         logger.info(f"Job {job_id} completed successfully ({elapsed_ms}ms)")
 67:         return None, int((time.monotonic() - start) * 1000)
 68:     except PermanentError as exc:
 69:         return exc, int((time.monotonic() - start) * 1000)
 70:     except ValueError as exc:
 71:         return PermanentError(str(exc)), int((time.monotonic() - start) * 1000)
 72:     except Exception as exc:
 73:         return exc, int((time.monotonic() - start) * 1000)
 74: 
 75: 
 76: async def fail_permanently(redis, job_data, entry_id, group, error, elapsed_ms):
 77:     job_id = job_data["job_id"]
 78:     await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "failed", error=str(error))
 79:     await redis.xadd(
 80:         "inference_jobs_dlq",
 81:         {"job_id": job_id, "reason": str(error), "elapsed_ms": str(elapsed_ms)},
 82:         maxlen=1000,
 83:         approximate=True,
 84:     )
 85:     await asyncio.to_thread(
 86:         log_metric,
 87:         job_id,
 88:         job_data.get("model_id", ""),
 89:         job_data.get("model_name", ""),
 90:         job_data.get("model_version", ""),
 91:         WORKER_NAME,
 92:         "error",
 93:         elapsed_ms,
 94:     )
 95:     await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, entry_id)
 96: 
 97: 
 98: async def handle_failure(redis, job_data, entry_id, group, error, elapsed_ms):
 99:     job_id = job_data["job_id"]
100:     retry_count = int(job_data.get("retry_count", 0))
101:     if retry_count < MAX_RETRIES:
102:         retry_count += 1
103:         delay = RETRY_DELAY_BASE ** retry_count
104:         await asyncio.sleep(delay)
105:         job_data["retry_count"] = str(retry_count)
106:         await redis.xadd(
107:             "inference_jobs",
108:             job_data,
109:             maxlen=STREAM_MAXLEN,
110:             approximate=True,
111:         )
112:         await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, entry_id)
113:     else:
114:         await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "failed", error=str(error))
115:         await redis.xadd(
116:             "inference_jobs_dlq",
117:             {"job_id": job_id, "reason": str(error), "elapsed_ms": str(elapsed_ms)},
118:             maxlen=1000,
119:             approximate=True,
120:         )
121:         await asyncio.to_thread(
122:             log_metric,
123:             job_id,
124:             job_data.get("model_id", ""),
125:             job_data.get("model_name", ""),
126:             job_data.get("model_version", ""),
127:             WORKER_NAME,
128:             "error",
129:             elapsed_ms,
130:         )
131:         await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, entry_id)
132: 
133: 
134: async def process_entry(redis, group, entry_id, fields):
135:     job_data = decode_fields(fields)
136:     if job_data.get("worker_name") != WORKER_NAME:
137:         await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, entry_id)
138:         return
139:     error, elapsed_ms = await handle_job(redis, job_data)
140:     if error is None:
141:         await asyncio.to_thread(
142:             log_metric,
143:             job_data["job_id"],
144:             job_data.get("model_id", ""),
145:             job_data.get("model_name", ""),
146:             job_data.get("model_version", ""),
147:             WORKER_NAME,
148:             "success",
149:             elapsed_ms,
150:         )
151:         await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, entry_id)
152:     elif isinstance(error, PermanentError):
153:         await fail_permanently(redis, job_data, entry_id, group, error, elapsed_ms)
154:     else:
155:         await handle_failure(redis, job_data, entry_id, group, error, elapsed_ms)
156: 
157: 
158: async def log_consumer_lag(redis, group):
159:     try:
160:         stream_length = await redis.xlen("inference_jobs")
161:         pending_info = await redis.xpending("inference_jobs", group)
162:         if pending_info:
163:             pending_count = pending_info["pending"]
164:             logger.info(f"[{WORKER_NAME}] Stream length: {stream_length}, Pending: {pending_count}")
165:             if stream_length > STREAM_MAXLEN * 0.8:
166:                 logger.warning(
167:                     f"[{WORKER_NAME}] Stream length {stream_length} exceeds 80% of limit {STREAM_MAXLEN}"
168:                 )
169:     except Exception as exc:
170:         logger.debug(f"Could not fetch lag metrics: {exc}")
171: 
172: 
173: async def reclaim_stale(redis, group):
174:     next_id = "0-0"
175:     for _ in range(100):
176:         res = await redis.xautoclaim(
177:             "inference_jobs",
178:             group,
179:             CONSUMER,
180:             RECLAIM_IDLE_MS,
181:             start_id=next_id,
182:             count=10,
183:         )
184:         next_id = res[0]
185:         entries = res[1]
186:         if len(res) > 2 and res[2]:
187:             await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, *res[2])
188:         for entry_id, fields in entries:
189:             await process_entry(redis, group, entry_id, fields)
190:         if next_id in (b"0-0", "0-0"):
191:             break
192: 
193: 
194: async def consume_loop():
195:     _load_model()
196:     redis = Redis.from_url(REDIS_URL, decode_responses=False)
197:     group = f"{WORKER_NAME}_group"
198:     try:
199:         try:
200:             await redis.xgroup_create("inference_jobs", group, id="0", mkstream=True)
201:             logger.info(f"Created consumer group: {group}")
202:         except Exception:
203:             logger.info(f"Consumer group {group} already exists")
204:         logger.info(f"Starting consumer loop for {WORKER_NAME}")
205:         await reclaim_stale(redis, group)
206:         last_maintenance = time.monotonic()
207:         while True:
208:             try:
209:                 messages = await redis.xreadgroup(
210:                     group,
211:                     CONSUMER,
212:                     {"inference_jobs": ">"},
213:                     count=1,
214:                     block=5000,
215:                 )
216:                 now = time.monotonic()
217:                 if now - last_maintenance >= MAINTENANCE_INTERVAL_SECONDS:
218:                     await log_consumer_lag(redis, group)
219:                     await reclaim_stale(redis, group)
220:                     last_maintenance = time.monotonic()
221:                 if not messages:
222:                     continue
223:                 for _, entries in messages:
224:                     for entry_id, fields in entries:
225:                         await process_entry(redis, group, entry_id, fields)
226:             except asyncio.CancelledError:
227:                 logger.info("Consumer loop cancelled")
228:                 break
229:             except Exception as exc:
230:                 logger.error(f"Error in consumer loop: {exc}")
231:                 await asyncio.sleep(1)
232:     finally:
233:         await redis.close()
234: 
235: 
236: if __name__ == "__main__":
237:     asyncio.run(consume_loop())
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[SH]  workers\asr\entrypoint.sh
================================================================================
  1: #!/bin/bash
  2: set -e
  3: cd /app
  4: python3 -m grpc_tools.protoc -I. --python_out=. --pyi_out=. --grpc_python_out=. inference.proto
  5: 
  6: uvicorn main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8002 &
  7: python grpc_server.py &
  8: python consumer.py &
  9: 
 10: trap 'kill $(jobs -p) 2>/dev/null; exit 0' TERM INT
 11: wait -n || true
 12: kill $(jobs -p) 2>/dev/null || true
 13: exit 1
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[PY]  workers\asr\grpc_server.py
================================================================================
  1: import grpc
  2: import json
  3: import time
  4: import logging
  5: import io
  6: import os
  7: from concurrent import futures
  8: import inference_pb2
  9: import inference_pb2_grpc
 10: from inference import transcribe, _load_model
 11: 
 12: logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
 13: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
 14: MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = int(os.getenv("MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES", "10485760"))
 15: MAX_MSG = MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES + 1048576
 16: GRPC_MAX_WORKERS = int(os.getenv("GRPC_MAX_WORKERS", "10"))
 17: 
 18: 
 19: class InferenceWorkerServicer(inference_pb2_grpc.InferenceWorkerServicer):
 20:     def Predict(self, request, context):
 21:         """Unary RPC for single inference request."""
 22:         start = time.perf_counter()
 23: 
 24:         try:
 25:             logger.info(f"gRPC Predict called for model: {request.model_name}")
 26: 
 27:             # Run inference
 28:             result = transcribe(request.payload)
 29: 
 30:             latency_ms = (time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000
 31: 
 32:             return inference_pb2.InferenceResponse(
 33:                 result_json=json.dumps(result),
 34:                 latency_ms=latency_ms
 35:             )
 36:         except Exception as e:
 37:             logger.error(f"Prediction failed: {e}")
 38:             context.set_code(grpc.StatusCode.INTERNAL)
 39:             context.set_details(str(e))
 40:             return inference_pb2.InferenceResponse()
 41: 
 42:     def PredictStream(self, request_iterator, context):
 43:         """
 44:         Bidirectional streaming RPC for progressive ASR inference.
 45: 
 46:         Receives audio chunks, processes them incrementally, and yields
 47:         partial transcription results as they become available.
 48:         """
 49:         logger.info("gRPC PredictStream called - bidirectional streaming")
 50: 
 51:         try:
 52:             # Collect chunks
 53:             chunks = []
 54:             for chunk_msg in request_iterator:
 55:                 logger.debug(f"Received chunk {chunk_msg.sequence}, size: {len(chunk_msg.chunk)}")
 56:                 chunks.append((chunk_msg.sequence, chunk_msg.chunk, chunk_msg.is_final))
 57: 
 58:                 # Process chunk immediately for streaming
 59:                 if len(chunk_msg.chunk) > 0:
 60:                     try:
 61:                         # Transcribe this chunk
 62:                         result = transcribe(chunk_msg.chunk)
 63: 
 64:                         # Yield partial result
 65:                         yield inference_pb2.InferenceResult(
 66:                             partial_text=result.get("text", ""),
 67:                             is_final=chunk_msg.is_final,
 68:                             confidence=result.get("confidence", 0.0)
 69:                         )
 70: 
 71:                         logger.info(f"Streamed partial result for chunk {chunk_msg.sequence}")
 72: 
 73:                         # If final chunk, we're done
 74:                         if chunk_msg.is_final:
 75:                             break
 76: 
 77:                     except Exception as e:
 78:                         logger.error(f"Failed to process chunk {chunk_msg.sequence}: {e}")
 79:                         yield inference_pb2.InferenceResult(
 80:                             partial_text=f"[Error: {str(e)}]",
 81:                             is_final=chunk_msg.is_final,
 82:                             confidence=0.0
 83:                         )
 84: 
 85:         except Exception as e:
 86:             logger.error(f"Stream processing failed: {e}")
 87:             context.set_code(grpc.StatusCode.INTERNAL)
 88:             context.set_details(str(e))
 89: 
 90: 
 91: def serve(port=50051):
 92:     """Start the gRPC server."""
 93:     _load_model()
 94:     server = grpc.server(
 95:         futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=GRPC_MAX_WORKERS),
 96:         options=[
 97:             ("grpc.max_send_message_length", MAX_MSG),
 98:             ("grpc.max_receive_message_length", MAX_MSG),
 99:         ],
100:     )
101:     inference_pb2_grpc.add_InferenceWorkerServicer_to_server(
102:         InferenceWorkerServicer(), server
103:     )
104:     server.add_insecure_port(f"[::]:{port}")
105:     server.start()
106:     logger.info(f"gRPC server started on port {port}")
107:     server.wait_for_termination()
108: 
109: 
110: if __name__ == "__main__":
111:     serve()
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[PY]  workers\asr\inference.py
================================================================================
  1: import os
  2: import tempfile
  3: import threading
  4: import whisper
  5: 
  6: _model = None
  7: _lock = threading.Lock()
  8: 
  9: 
 10: def _load_model():
 11:     global _model
 12:     with _lock:
 13:         if _model is None:
 14:             model_size = os.getenv("WHISPER_MODEL", "tiny")
 15:             _model = whisper.load_model(model_size)
 16:     return _model
 17: 
 18: 
 19: def transcribe(audio_bytes: bytes) -> dict:
 20:     model = _load_model()
 21:     with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=".wav", delete=False) as f:
 22:         f.write(audio_bytes)
 23:         tmp_path = f.name
 24:     try:
 25:         result = model.transcribe(tmp_path)
 26:     except RuntimeError as exc:
 27:         if str(exc).startswith("Failed to load audio"):
 28:             raise ValueError("Invalid audio data") from exc
 29:         raise
 30:     finally:
 31:         os.unlink(tmp_path)
 32:     return {"text": result["text"]}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[PY]  workers\asr\kafka_consumer.py
================================================================================
  1: # DORMANT: not imported by the gateway and not deployed. See README.
  2: import asyncio
  3: import json
  4: import logging
  5: import os
  6: from aiokafka import AIOKafkaConsumer
  7: from redis.asyncio import Redis
  8: from inference import transcribe
  9: 
 10: logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
 11: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
 12: 
 13: WORKER_NAME = "asr-worker"
 14: KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS = os.getenv("KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS", "kafka:9092")
 15: REDIS_URL = os.getenv("REDIS_URL", "redis://redis:6379")
 16: 
 17: 
 18: async def update_job_status(redis: Redis, job_id: str, status: str, result: dict = None, error: str = None):
 19:     """Update job status in Redis."""
 20:     data = {"job_id": job_id, "status": status}
 21: 
 22:     if result:
 23:         data["result"] = result
 24: 
 25:     if error:
 26:         data["error"] = error
 27: 
 28:     if status in ("success", "failed"):
 29:         await redis.setex(f"result:{job_id}", 7200, json.dumps(data))
 30:         await redis.delete(f"job:{job_id}:status")
 31:     else:
 32:         await redis.setex(f"job:{job_id}:status", 3600, json.dumps(data))
 33: 
 34: 
 35: async def handle_job(redis: Redis, job_data: dict) -> bool:
 36:     """
 37:     Process a single job.
 38: 
 39:     Returns:
 40:         bool: True if successful, False if failed
 41:     """
 42:     job_id = job_data["job_id"]
 43:     payload_hex = job_data["payload"]
 44: 
 45:     try:
 46:         logger.info(f"Processing job {job_id}")
 47:         await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "processing")
 48: 
 49:         # Decode hex payload back to bytes
 50:         payload = bytes.fromhex(payload_hex)
 51: 
 52:         # Run inference
 53:         result = transcribe(payload)
 54: 
 55:         # Store result
 56:         await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "success", result=result)
 57:         logger.info(f"Job {job_id} completed successfully")
 58:         return True
 59: 
 60:     except Exception as e:
 61:         logger.error(f"Job {job_id} failed: {e}")
 62:         await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "failed", error=str(e))
 63:         return False
 64: 
 65: 
 66: async def consume_loop():
 67:     """Main consumer loop for Kafka."""
 68:     redis = Redis.from_url(REDIS_URL, decode_responses=False)
 69: 
 70:     consumer = AIOKafkaConsumer(
 71:         "inference-jobs",
 72:         bootstrap_servers=KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS,
 73:         group_id=f"{WORKER_NAME}-workers",
 74:         value_deserializer=lambda m: json.loads(m.decode("utf-8")),
 75:         auto_offset_reset="earliest",
 76:         enable_auto_commit=True
 77:     )
 78: 
 79:     try:
 80:         await consumer.start()
 81:         logger.info(f"Started Kafka consumer for {WORKER_NAME}")
 82: 
 83:         async for msg in consumer:
 84:             try:
 85:                 job_data = msg.value
 86: 
 87:                 # Filter by worker_name
 88:                 if job_data.get("worker_name") != WORKER_NAME:
 89:                     logger.debug(f"Skipping job for different worker: {job_data.get('worker_name')}")
 90:                     continue
 91: 
 92:                 # Process job
 93:                 await handle_job(redis, job_data)
 94: 
 95:             except Exception as e:
 96:                 logger.error(f"Error processing message: {e}")
 97: 
 98:     except asyncio.CancelledError:
 99:         logger.info("Kafka consumer cancelled")
100:     finally:
101:         await consumer.stop()
102:         await redis.close()
103: 
104: 
105: if __name__ == "__main__":
106:     asyncio.run(consume_loop())
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[PY]  workers\asr\main.py
================================================================================
  1: from fastapi import FastAPI, UploadFile, File, HTTPException
  2: from inference import transcribe, _load_model
  3: 
  4: AUDIO_MIME_TYPES = {
  5:     "audio/wav", "audio/wave", "audio/x-wav",
  6:     "audio/mpeg", "audio/mp3", "audio/ogg",
  7:     "audio/webm", "audio/flac", "audio/x-flac",
  8:     "application/octet-stream",
  9: }
 10: 
 11: app = FastAPI(title="ASR Worker", version="1.0.0")
 12: 
 13: 
 14: @app.on_event("startup")
 15: async def startup():
 16:     _load_model()
 17: 
 18: 
 19: @app.post("/infer")
 20: async def infer(file: UploadFile = File(...)):
 21:     if file.content_type and file.content_type not in AUDIO_MIME_TYPES:
 22:         raise HTTPException(400, f"Unexpected content type: {file.content_type}")
 23:     audio_bytes = await file.read()
 24:     try:
 25:         result = transcribe(audio_bytes)
 26:     except ValueError as exc:
 27:         raise HTTPException(400, str(exc)) from exc
 28:     return result
 29: 
 30: 
 31: @app.get("/health")
 32: def health():
 33:     return {"status": "ok"}

[PY]  workers\asr\metrics.py
================================================================================
  1: import logging
  2: import os
  3: import time
  4: 
  5: log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
  6: 
  7: _client = None
  8: _skip_until = 0.0
  9: 
 10: 
 11: def _get_client():
 12:     global _client
 13:     if _client is None:
 14:         import clickhouse_connect
 15: 
 16:         _client = clickhouse_connect.get_client(
 17:             host=os.environ["CLICKHOUSE_HOST"],
 18:             port=int(os.getenv("CLICKHOUSE_PORT", "8123")),
 19:             connect_timeout=2,
 20:             send_receive_timeout=5,
 21:         )
 22:     return _client
 23: 
 24: 
 25: def log_metric(
 26:     request_id: str,
 27:     model_id: str,
 28:     model_name: str,
 29:     model_version: str,
 30:     worker_name: str,
 31:     status: str,
 32:     latency_ms: int,
 33: ):
 34:     """Write one metric row without surfacing ClickHouse failures to workers."""
 35:     global _client, _skip_until
 36:     if not os.getenv("CLICKHOUSE_HOST"):
 37:         return
 38:     now = time.monotonic()
 39:     if now < _skip_until:
 40:         return
 41:     try:
 42:         _get_client().insert(
 43:             "request_metrics",
 44:             [[
 45:                 request_id,
 46:                 model_id,
 47:                 model_name,
 48:                 model_version,
 49:                 worker_name,
 50:                 status,
 51:                 int(latency_ms),
 52:             ]],
 53:             column_names=[
 54:                 "request_id",
 55:                 "model_id",
 56:                 "model_name",
 57:                 "model_version",
 58:                 "worker_name",
 59:                 "status",
 60:                 "latency_ms",
 61:             ],
 62:         )
 63:     except Exception as exc:
 64:         _client = None
 65:         _skip_until = time.monotonic() + 60.0
 66:         log.warning("metric insert failed (skipping for 60s): %s", exc)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[PY]  workers\doc_ocr\consumer.py
================================================================================
  1: import asyncio
  2: import json
  3: import logging
  4: import os
  5: import time
  6: from redis.asyncio import Redis
  7: from inference import extract_text
  8: from metrics import log_metric
  9: 
 10: logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
 11: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
 12: 
 13: WORKER_NAME = os.getenv("WORKER_NAME", "doc-ocr-worker")
 14: CONSUMER = f"{WORKER_NAME}-{os.getenv('HOSTNAME', '1')}"
 15: RECLAIM_IDLE_MS = int(os.getenv("RECLAIM_IDLE_MS", "300000"))
 16: REDIS_URL = os.getenv("REDIS_URL", "redis://redis:6379")
 17: MAX_RETRIES = 3
 18: RETRY_DELAY_BASE = 2
 19: STREAM_MAXLEN = int(os.getenv("STREAM_MAXLEN", "10000"))
 20: MAINTENANCE_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 30.0
 21: 
 22: 
 23: class PermanentError(Exception):
 24:     """Input that retrying cannot fix."""
 25: 
 26: 
 27: async def update_job_status(redis: Redis, job_id: str, status: str, result: dict = None, error: str = None):
 28:     data = {"job_id": job_id, "status": status}
 29:     if result:
 30:         data["result"] = result
 31:     if error:
 32:         data["error"] = error
 33: 
 34:     if status in ("success", "failed"):
 35:         await redis.setex(f"result:{job_id}", 7200, json.dumps(data))
 36:         await redis.delete(f"job:{job_id}:status")
 37:     else:
 38:         await redis.setex(f"job:{job_id}:status", 3600, json.dumps(data))
 39: 
 40: 
 41: def decode_fields(fields):
 42:     decoded = {}
 43:     for key, value in fields.items():
 44:         k = key.decode() if isinstance(key, bytes) else key
 45:         if k == "payload":
 46:             decoded[k] = value
 47:         elif isinstance(value, bytes):
 48:             decoded[k] = value.decode()
 49:         else:
 50:             decoded[k] = value
 51:     return decoded
 52: 
 53: 
 54: async def handle_job(redis: Redis, job_data: dict) -> tuple[Exception | None, int]:
 55:     start = time.monotonic()
 56:     job_id = job_data["job_id"]
 57: 
 58:     try:
 59:         logger.info(f"Processing job {job_id}")
 60:         await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "processing")
 61:         payload = job_data.get("payload") or await redis.get(f"payload:{job_id}")
 62:         if payload is None:
 63:             return PermanentError("payload expired"), int((time.monotonic() - start) * 1000)
 64: 
 65:         inference_start = time.monotonic()
 66:         result = extract_text(payload)
 67:         elapsed_ms = int((time.monotonic() - inference_start) * 1000)
 68:         await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "success", result=result)
 69:         logger.info(f"Job {job_id} completed successfully ({elapsed_ms}ms)")
 70:         return None, int((time.monotonic() - start) * 1000)
 71:     except PermanentError as exc:
 72:         return exc, int((time.monotonic() - start) * 1000)
 73:     except ValueError as exc:
 74:         permanent = PermanentError(str(exc))
 75:         return permanent, int((time.monotonic() - start) * 1000)
 76:     except Exception as exc:
 77:         return exc, int((time.monotonic() - start) * 1000)
 78: 
 79: 
 80: async def fail_permanently(redis, job_data, entry_id, group, error, elapsed_ms):
 81:     job_id = job_data["job_id"]
 82:     logger.error(f"Job {job_id} permanently failed: {error}")
 83:     await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "failed", error=str(error))
 84:     await redis.xadd(
 85:         "inference_jobs_dlq",
 86:         {
 87:             "job_id": job_id,
 88:             "reason": str(error),
 89:             "elapsed_ms": str(elapsed_ms),
 90:         },
 91:         maxlen=1000,
 92:         approximate=True,
 93:     )
 94:     await asyncio.to_thread(
 95:         log_metric,
 96:         job_id,
 97:         job_data.get("model_id", ""),
 98:         job_data.get("model_name", ""),
 99:         job_data.get("model_version", ""),
100:         WORKER_NAME,
101:         "error",
102:         elapsed_ms,
103:     )
104:     await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, entry_id)
105: 
106: 
107: async def handle_failure(redis, job_data, entry_id, group, error, elapsed_ms):
108:     job_id = job_data["job_id"]
109:     retry_count = int(job_data.get("retry_count", 0))
110: 
111:     if retry_count < MAX_RETRIES:
112:         retry_count += 1
113:         delay = RETRY_DELAY_BASE ** retry_count
114:         logger.warning(f"Job {job_id} retry {retry_count}/{MAX_RETRIES} after {delay}s")
115:         await asyncio.sleep(delay)
116:         job_data["retry_count"] = str(retry_count)
117:         await redis.xadd(
118:             "inference_jobs",
119:             job_data,
120:             maxlen=STREAM_MAXLEN,
121:             approximate=True,
122:         )
123:         await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, entry_id)
124:     else:
125:         logger.error(f"Job {job_id} exceeded max retries, moving to DLQ")
126:         await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "failed", error=str(error))
127:         await redis.xadd(
128:             "inference_jobs_dlq",
129:             {
130:                 "job_id": job_id,
131:                 "reason": str(error),
132:                 "elapsed_ms": str(elapsed_ms),
133:             },
134:             maxlen=1000,
135:             approximate=True,
136:         )
137:         await asyncio.to_thread(
138:             log_metric,
139:             job_id,
140:             job_data.get("model_id", ""),
141:             job_data.get("model_name", ""),
142:             job_data.get("model_version", ""),
143:             WORKER_NAME,
144:             "error",
145:             elapsed_ms,
146:         )
147:         await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, entry_id)
148: 
149: 
150: async def process_entry(redis, group, entry_id, fields):
151:     job_data = decode_fields(fields)
152:     if job_data.get("worker_name") != WORKER_NAME:
153:         await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, entry_id)
154:         return
155:     error, elapsed_ms = await handle_job(redis, job_data)
156:     if error is None:
157:         await asyncio.to_thread(
158:             log_metric,
159:             job_data["job_id"],
160:             job_data.get("model_id", ""),
161:             job_data.get("model_name", ""),
162:             job_data.get("model_version", ""),
163:             WORKER_NAME,
164:             "success",
165:             elapsed_ms,
166:         )
167:         await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, entry_id)
168:     elif isinstance(error, PermanentError):
169:         await fail_permanently(redis, job_data, entry_id, group, error, elapsed_ms)
170:     else:
171:         await handle_failure(redis, job_data, entry_id, group, error, elapsed_ms)
172: 
173: 
174: async def log_consumer_lag(redis, group):
175:     try:
176:         stream_length = await redis.xlen("inference_jobs")
177:         pending_info = await redis.xpending("inference_jobs", group)
178:         if pending_info:
179:             pending_count = pending_info["pending"]
180:             logger.info(f"[{WORKER_NAME}] Stream length: {stream_length}, Pending: {pending_count}")
181:             if stream_length > STREAM_MAXLEN * 0.8:
182:                 logger.warning(
183:                     f"[{WORKER_NAME}] Stream length {stream_length} exceeds 80% of limit {STREAM_MAXLEN}"
184:                 )
185:     except Exception as exc:
186:         logger.debug(f"Could not fetch lag metrics: {exc}")
187: 
188: 
189: async def reclaim_stale(redis, group):
190:     next_id = "0-0"
191:     for _ in range(100):
192:         res = await redis.xautoclaim(
193:             "inference_jobs",
194:             group,
195:             CONSUMER,
196:             RECLAIM_IDLE_MS,
197:             start_id=next_id,
198:             count=10,
199:         )
200:         next_id = res[0]
201:         entries = res[1]
202:         if len(res) > 2 and res[2]:
203:             await redis.xack("inference_jobs", group, *res[2])
204:         for entry_id, fields in entries:
205:             await process_entry(redis, group, entry_id, fields)
206:         if next_id in (b"0-0", "0-0"):
207:             break
208: 
209: 
210: async def consume_loop():
211:     redis = Redis.from_url(REDIS_URL, decode_responses=False)
212:     group = f"{WORKER_NAME}_group"
213:     try:
214:         try:
215:             await redis.xgroup_create("inference_jobs", group, id="0", mkstream=True)
216:             logger.info(f"Created consumer group: {group}")
217:         except Exception:
218:             logger.info(f"Consumer group {group} already exists")
219: 
220:         logger.info(f"Starting consumer loop for {WORKER_NAME}")
221:         await reclaim_stale(redis, group)
222:         last_maintenance = time.monotonic()
223:         while True:
224:             try:
225:                 messages = await redis.xreadgroup(
226:                     group,
227:                     CONSUMER,
228:                     {"inference_jobs": ">"},
229:                     count=1,
230:                     block=5000,
231:                 )
232:                 now = time.monotonic()
233:                 if now - last_maintenance >= MAINTENANCE_INTERVAL_SECONDS:
234:                     await log_consumer_lag(redis, group)
235:                     await reclaim_stale(redis, group)
236:                     last_maintenance = time.monotonic()
237:                 if not messages:
238:                     continue
239:                 for _, entries in messages:
240:                     for entry_id, fields in entries:
241:                         await process_entry(redis, group, entry_id, fields)
242:             except asyncio.CancelledError:
243:                 logger.info("Consumer loop cancelled")
244:                 break
245:             except Exception as exc:
246:                 logger.error(f"Error in consumer loop: {exc}")
247:                 await asyncio.sleep(1)
248:     finally:
249:         await redis.close()
250: 
251: 
252: if __name__ == "__main__":
253:     asyncio.run(consume_loop())
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[SH]  workers\doc_ocr\entrypoint.sh
================================================================================
  1: #!/bin/bash
  2: set -e
  3: cd /app
  4: python3 -m grpc_tools.protoc -I. --python_out=. --pyi_out=. --grpc_python_out=. inference.proto
  5: 
  6: uvicorn main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8001 &
  7: python grpc_server.py &
  8: python consumer.py &
  9: 
 10: trap 'kill $(jobs -p) 2>/dev/null; exit 0' TERM INT
 11: wait -n || true
 12: kill $(jobs -p) 2>/dev/null || true

[PY]  workers\doc_ocr\grpc_server.py
================================================================================
  1: import grpc
  2: import json
  3: import time
  4: import logging
  5: import os
  6: from concurrent import futures
  7: import inference_pb2
  8: import inference_pb2_grpc
  9: from inference import extract_text
 10: 
 11: logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
 12: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
 13: MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = int(os.getenv("MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES", "10485760"))
 14: MAX_MSG = MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES + 1048576
 15: GRPC_MAX_WORKERS = int(os.getenv("GRPC_MAX_WORKERS", "10"))
 16: 
 17: 
 18: class InferenceWorkerServicer(inference_pb2_grpc.InferenceWorkerServicer):
 19:     def Predict(self, request, context):
 20:         """Unary RPC for single inference request."""
 21:         start = time.perf_counter()
 22: 
 23:         try:
 24:             logger.info(f"gRPC Predict called for model: {request.model_name}")
 25: 
 26:             # Run inference
 27:             result = extract_text(request.payload)
 28: 
 29:             latency_ms = (time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000
 30: 
 31:             return inference_pb2.InferenceResponse(
 32:                 result_json=json.dumps(result),
 33:                 latency_ms=latency_ms
 34:             )
 35:         except Exception as e:
 36:             logger.error(f"Prediction failed: {e}")
 37:             context.set_code(grpc.StatusCode.INTERNAL)
 38:             context.set_details(str(e))
 39:             return inference_pb2.InferenceResponse()
 40: 
 41: 
 42: def serve(port=50051):
 43:     """Start the gRPC server."""
 44:     server = grpc.server(
 45:         futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=GRPC_MAX_WORKERS),
 46:         options=[
 47:             ("grpc.max_send_message_length", MAX_MSG),
 48:             ("grpc.max_receive_message_length", MAX_MSG),
 49:         ],
 50:     )
 51:     inference_pb2_grpc.add_InferenceWorkerServicer_to_server(
 52:         InferenceWorkerServicer(), server
 53:     )
 54:     server.add_insecure_port(f"[::]:{port}")
 55:     server.start()
 56:     logger.info(f"gRPC server started on port {port}")
 57:     server.wait_for_termination()
 58: 
 59: 
 60: if __name__ == "__main__":
 61:     serve()
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[PY]  workers\doc_ocr\inference.py
================================================================================
  1: import easyocr
  2: import numpy as np
  3: import cv2
  4: 
  5: # Load once at module import, not per-request
  6: reader = easyocr.Reader(['en'], gpu=False)
  7: 
  8: 
  9: def extract_text(image_bytes: bytes) -> dict:
 10:     """
 11:     Extract text from image bytes using EasyOCR.
 12:     Returns dict with extracted text, avg confidence, and line count.
 13:     """
 14:     nparr = np.frombuffer(image_bytes, np.uint8)
 15:     img = cv2.imdecode(nparr, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
 16: 
 17:     if img is None:
 18:         raise ValueError("Invalid image data")
 19: 
 20:     results = reader.readtext(img)
 21: 
 22:     lines = [text for (_, text, _) in results]
 23:     confidences = [float(conf) for (_, _, conf) in results]
 24: 
 25:     return {
 26:         "text": "\n".join(lines),
 27:         "avg_confidence": sum(confidences) / len(confidences) if confidences else 0.0,
 28:         "line_count": len(lines)
 29:     }
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[PY]  workers\doc_ocr\kafka_consumer.py
================================================================================
  1: # DORMANT: not imported by the gateway and not deployed. See README.
  2: import asyncio
  3: import json
  4: import logging
  5: import os
  6: from aiokafka import AIOKafkaConsumer
  7: from redis.asyncio import Redis
  8: from inference import extract_text
  9: 
 10: logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
 11: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
 12: 
 13: WORKER_NAME = "doc-ocr-worker"
 14: KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS = os.getenv("KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS", "kafka:9092")
 15: REDIS_URL = os.getenv("REDIS_URL", "redis://redis:6379")
 16: 
 17: 
 18: async def update_job_status(redis: Redis, job_id: str, status: str, result: dict = None, error: str = None):
 19:     """Update job status in Redis."""
 20:     data = {"job_id": job_id, "status": status}
 21: 
 22:     if result:
 23:         data["result"] = result
 24: 
 25:     if error:
 26:         data["error"] = error
 27: 
 28:     if status in ("success", "failed"):
 29:         await redis.setex(f"result:{job_id}", 7200, json.dumps(data))
 30:         await redis.delete(f"job:{job_id}:status")
 31:     else:
 32:         await redis.setex(f"job:{job_id}:status", 3600, json.dumps(data))
 33: 
 34: 
 35: async def handle_job(redis: Redis, job_data: dict) -> bool:
 36:     """
 37:     Process a single job.
 38: 
 39:     Returns:
 40:         bool: True if successful, False if failed
 41:     """
 42:     job_id = job_data["job_id"]
 43:     payload_hex = job_data["payload"]
 44: 
 45:     try:
 46:         logger.info(f"Processing job {job_id}")
 47:         await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "processing")
 48: 
 49:         # Decode hex payload back to bytes
 50:         payload = bytes.fromhex(payload_hex)
 51: 
 52:         # Run inference
 53:         result = extract_text(payload)
 54: 
 55:         # Store result
 56:         await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "success", result=result)
 57:         logger.info(f"Job {job_id} completed successfully")
 58:         return True
 59: 
 60:     except Exception as e:
 61:         logger.error(f"Job {job_id} failed: {e}")
 62:         await update_job_status(redis, job_id, "failed", error=str(e))
 63:         return False
 64: 
 65: 
 66: async def consume_loop():
 67:     """Main consumer loop for Kafka."""
 68:     redis = Redis.from_url(REDIS_URL, decode_responses=False)
 69: 
 70:     consumer = AIOKafkaConsumer(
 71:         "inference-jobs",
 72:         bootstrap_servers=KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS,
 73:         group_id=f"{WORKER_NAME}-workers",
 74:         value_deserializer=lambda m: json.loads(m.decode("utf-8")),
 75:         auto_offset_reset="earliest",
 76:         enable_auto_commit=True
 77:     )
 78: 
 79:     try:
 80:         await consumer.start()
 81:         logger.info(f"Started Kafka consumer for {WORKER_NAME}")
 82: 
 83:         async for msg in consumer:
 84:             try:
 85:                 job_data = msg.value
 86: 
 87:                 # Filter by worker_name
 88:                 if job_data.get("worker_name") != WORKER_NAME:
 89:                     logger.debug(f"Skipping job for different worker: {job_data.get('worker_name')}")
 90:                     continue
 91: 
 92:                 # Process job
 93:                 await handle_job(redis, job_data)
 94: 
 95:             except Exception as e:
 96:                 logger.error(f"Error processing message: {e}")
 97: 
 98:     except asyncio.CancelledError:
 99:         logger.info("Kafka consumer cancelled")
100:     finally:
101:         await consumer.stop()
102:         await redis.close()
103: 
104: 
105: if __name__ == "__main__":
106:     asyncio.run(consume_loop())
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 10:     try:
 11:         result = extract_text(image_bytes)
 12:     except ValueError as exc:
 13:         raise HTTPException(400, str(exc))

[PY]  workers\doc_ocr\metrics.py
================================================================================
  1: import logging
  2: import os
  3: import time
  4: 
  5: log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
  6: 
  7: _client = None
  8: _skip_until = 0.0
  9: 
 10: 
 11: def _get_client():
 12:     global _client
 13:     if _client is None:
 14:         import clickhouse_connect
 15: 
 16:         _client = clickhouse_connect.get_client(
 17:             host=os.environ["CLICKHOUSE_HOST"],
 18:             port=int(os.getenv("CLICKHOUSE_PORT", "8123")),
 19:             connect_timeout=2,
 20:             send_receive_timeout=5,
 21:         )
 22:     return _client
 23: 
 24: 
 25: def log_metric(
 26:     request_id: str,
 27:     model_id: str,
 28:     model_name: str,
 29:     model_version: str,
 30:     worker_name: str,
 31:     status: str,
 32:     latency_ms: int,
 33: ):
 34:     """Write one metric row without surfacing ClickHouse failures to workers."""
 35:     global _client, _skip_until
 36:     if not os.getenv("CLICKHOUSE_HOST"):
 37:         return
 38:     now = time.monotonic()
 39:     if now < _skip_until:
 40:         return
 41:     try:
 42:         _get_client().insert(
 43:             "request_metrics",
 44:             [[
 45:                 request_id,
 46:                 model_id,
 47:                 model_name,
 48:                 model_version,
 49:                 worker_name,
 50:                 status,
 51:                 int(latency_ms),
 52:             ]],
 53:             column_names=[
 54:                 "request_id",
 55:                 "model_id",
 56:                 "model_name",
 57:                 "model_version",
 58:                 "worker_name",
 59:                 "status",
 60:                 "latency_ms",
 61:             ],
 62:         )
 63:     except Exception as exc:
 64:         _client = None
 65:         _skip_until = time.monotonic() + 60.0
 66:         log.warning("metric insert failed (skipping for 60s): %s", exc)
