[PY]  ws_latency_compare.py
================================================================================
  1: import os
  2: #!/usr/bin/env python3
  3: """
  4: Compare WebSocket inference latency: HTTP-backed vs gRPC-backed workers.
  5: 
  6: This times the actual code path that responds to protocol switching
  7: (/v1/ws/infer/{model_name}), unlike Artillery against /v1/infer/{model_name}
  8: which bypasses the registry's protocol field entirely.
  9: 
 10: Usage:
 11:     python ws_latency_compare.py <admin_key> <model_name> <test_file> [n_requests]
 12: 
 13: Example:
 14:     python ws_latency_compare.py $ADMIN_KEY doc-ocr test-image.png 20
 15:     python ws_latency_compare.py $ADMIN_KEY asr test-audio.wav 20
 16: """
 17: import asyncio
 18: import json
 19: import statistics
 20: import sys
 21: import time
 22: 
 23: import httpx
 24: import websockets
 25: 
 26: GATEWAY_HTTP = os.environ.get("GW", "http://localhost:8000")
 27: GATEWAY_WS = os.environ.get("GW", "http://localhost:8000").replace("http://", "ws://")
 28: 
 29: 
 30: async def get_model_id(admin_key: str, model_name: str) -> str:
 31:     async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
 32:         resp = await client.get(f"{GATEWAY_HTTP}/v1/models/{model_name}")
 33:         resp.raise_for_status()
 34:         return resp.json()["id"]
 35: 
 36: 
 37: async def set_protocol(admin_key: str, model_id: str, protocol: str, endpoint: str):
 38:     async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
 39:         resp = await client.patch(
 40:             f"{GATEWAY_HTTP}/v1/models/{model_id}",
 41:             headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {admin_key}"},
 42:             json={"protocol": protocol, "endpoint": endpoint},
 43:         )
 44:         resp.raise_for_status()
 45: 
 46: 
 47: async def time_one_request(admin_key: str, model_name: str, file_bytes: bytes) -> float:
 48:     """Returns latency in ms for one full WS round trip (connect -> final result)."""
 49:     uri = f"{GATEWAY_WS}/v1/ws/infer/{model_name}?api_key={admin_key}&version=v1"
 50:     start = time.perf_counter()
 51: 
 52:     async with websockets.connect(uri) as ws:
 53:         ready_raw = await ws.recv()
 54:         ready = json.loads(ready_raw)
 55:         if "error" in ready:
 56:             raise RuntimeError(f"Server error before send: {ready['error']}")
 57: 
 58:         await ws.send(file_bytes)
 59: 
 60:         while True:
 61:             msg_raw = await asyncio.wait_for(ws.recv(), timeout=30.0)
 62:             msg = json.loads(msg_raw)
 63:             if "error" in msg:
 64:                 raise RuntimeError(f"Server error: {msg['error']}")
 65:             if msg.get("is_final"):
 66:                 break
 67:             if "result" in msg:
 68:                 break
 69: 
 70:     elapsed_ms = (time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000
 71:     return elapsed_ms
 72: 
 73: 
 74: async def run_batch(admin_key: str, model_name: str, file_bytes: bytes, n: int) -> list:
 75:     latencies = []
 76:     for i in range(n):
 77:         try:
 78:             ms = await time_one_request(admin_key, model_name, file_bytes)
 79:             latencies.append(ms)
 80:             print(f"  request {i+1}/{n}: {ms:.1f} ms")
 81:         except Exception as e:
 82:             print(f"  request {i+1}/{n}: FAILED ({e})")
 83:     return latencies
 84: 
 85: 
 86: def summarize(label: str, latencies: list):
 87:     if not latencies:
 88:         print(f"\n{label}: no successful requests")
 89:         return
 90:     latencies_sorted = sorted(latencies)
 91:     n = len(latencies_sorted)
 92:     p50 = statistics.median(latencies_sorted)
 93:     p95 = latencies_sorted[min(int(n * 0.95), n - 1)]
 94:     p99 = latencies_sorted[min(int(n * 0.99), n - 1)]
 95:     print(f"\n{label} (n={n}):")
 96:     print(f"  min:    {min(latencies_sorted):.1f} ms")
 97:     print(f"  p50:    {p50:.1f} ms")
 98:     print(f"  p95:    {p95:.1f} ms")
 99:     print(f"  p99:    {p99:.1f} ms")
100:     print(f"  max:    {max(latencies_sorted):.1f} ms")
101:     print(f"  mean:   {statistics.mean(latencies_sorted):.1f} ms")
102: 
103: 
104: HTTP_ENDPOINTS = {
105:     "doc-ocr": "http://doc-ocr-worker.modelmesh.svc.cluster.local:8001/infer",
106:     "asr": "http://asr-worker.modelmesh.svc.cluster.local:8002/infer",
107: }
108: GRPC_ENDPOINTS = {
109:     "doc-ocr": "doc-ocr-worker.modelmesh.svc.cluster.local:50051",
110:     "asr": "asr-worker.modelmesh.svc.cluster.local:50051",
111: }
112: 
113: 
114: async def main():
115:     if len(sys.argv) < 4:
116:         print(__doc__)
117:         sys.exit(1)
118: 
119:     admin_key = sys.argv[1]
120:     model_name = sys.argv[2]
121:     test_file = sys.argv[3]
122:     n = int(sys.argv[4]) if len(sys.argv) > 4 else 20
123: 
124:     if model_name not in HTTP_ENDPOINTS:
125:         print(f"Unknown model_name '{model_name}', expected one of {list(HTTP_ENDPOINTS)}")
126:         sys.exit(1)
127: 
128:     with open(test_file, "rb") as f:
129:         file_bytes = f.read()
130: 
131:     model_id = await get_model_id(admin_key, model_name)
132:     print(f"Model '{model_name}' resolved to id={model_id}")
133: 
134:     print(f"\n=== Setting {model_name} to protocol=http ===")
135:     await set_protocol(admin_key, model_id, "http", HTTP_ENDPOINTS[model_name])
136:     await asyncio.sleep(0.5)
137:     print(f"Running {n} requests over WebSocket (http-backed)...")
138:     http_latencies = await run_batch(admin_key, model_name, file_bytes, n)
139: 
140:     print(f"\n=== Setting {model_name} to protocol=grpc ===")
141:     await set_protocol(admin_key, model_id, "grpc", GRPC_ENDPOINTS[model_name])
142:     await asyncio.sleep(0.5)
143:     print(f"Running {n} requests over WebSocket (grpc-backed)...")
144:     grpc_latencies = await run_batch(admin_key, model_name, file_bytes, n)
145: 
146:     print("\n" + "=" * 50)
147:     print(f"RESULTS: {model_name}, n={n} requests each")
148:     print("=" * 50)
149:     summarize("HTTP-backed", http_latencies)
150:     summarize("gRPC-backed", grpc_latencies)
151: 
152:     if http_latencies and grpc_latencies:
153:         http_p50 = statistics.median(http_latencies)
154:         grpc_p50 = statistics.median(grpc_latencies)
155:         diff_pct = ((grpc_p50 - http_p50) / http_p50) * 100
156:         direction = "slower" if diff_pct > 0 else "faster"
157:         print(f"\ngRPC p50 is {abs(diff_pct):.1f}% {direction} than HTTP p50 for this payload/model.")
158: 
159: 
160: if __name__ == "__main__":
161:     asyncio.run(main())
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
